nc)(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    } catch (error) {
      console.log(`cleanup-skipped: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : error}`);
    }
    import_electron.app.exit(code);
  }
});

function containerPlan(share) {
	return {
		image: "murasame-isolation:local",
		network: share?.network ?? "bridge",
		mounts: share?.hostPath ? [{
			source: share.hostPath,
			target: "/shared",
			readOnly: share.readOnly
		}] : [],
		devices: [],
		memory: "2g",
		cpus: "1.5",
		hostExecution: false
	};
}
function hostPathAllowed(candidate, explicitShare) {
	if (!explicitShare) return false;
	return candidate.replace(/[\\/]+$/, "").toLowerCase() === explicitShare.replace(/[\\/]+$/, "").toLowerCase();
}
function xdotoolCommand(action) {
	if (action.kind === "click") return [
		"xdotool",
		"mousemove",
		String(Math.round(action.x)),
		String(Math.round(action.y)),
		"click",
		"1"
	];
	if (action.kind === "type") return [
		"xdotool",
		"type",
		"--delay",
		"12",
		action.text
	];
	return [
		"xdotool",
		"key",
		action.key
	];
}
function dockerDesktopPath(exists = node_fs.existsSync) {
	const roots = [
		process.env.ProgramFiles,
		process.env["ProgramFiles(x86)"],
		process.env.LOCALAPPDATA
	].filter((item) => Boolean(item));
	for (const root of roots) {
		const candidate = (0, node_path.join)(root, "Docker", "Docker", "Docker Desktop.exe");
		if (exists(candidate)) return candidate;
	}
	return null;
}
/** Docker 没开就自己启动并等待。用户不需要先去打开 Docker Desktop。 */
async function ensureDocker(deps = {}) {
	const available = deps.available ?? (() => dockerAvailableAsync(void 0, { signal: deps.signal }));
	const cancelled = () => ({
		ok: false,
		detail: "活动准备已取消"
	});
	if (deps.signal?.aborted) return cancelled();
	const ready = await available();
	if (deps.signal?.aborted) return cancelled();
	if (ready) return { ok: true };
	const executable = (deps.locate ?? dockerDesktopPath)();
	if (!executable) return {
		ok: false,
		detail: "这台电脑还没有安装 Docker Desktop，隔离桌面需要它。安装好之后重新打开本软件，再点一次开始即可。"
	};
	deps.onStatus?.("正在启动运行环境，第一次大约一两分钟。");
	try {
		(deps.launch ?? launchDockerDesktop)(executable);
	} catch (error) {
		return {
			ok: false,
			detail: `运行环境没能自动启动：${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "未知原因"}。`
		};
	}
	const wait = deps.wait ?? ((ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)));
	const attempts = deps.attempts ?? 24;
	for (let index = 0; index < attempts; index += 1) {
		if (deps.signal?.aborted) return cancelled();
		await wait(5e3);
		if (deps.signal?.aborted) return cancelled();
		const ready = await available();
		if (deps.signal?.aborted) return cancelled();
		if (ready) return { ok: true };
	}
	return {
		ok: false,
		detail: "运行环境还在启动。等一会儿再点一次「开始活动」。"
	};
}
function launchDockerDesktop(executable) {
	(0, node_child_process.spawn)(executable, [], {
		detached: true,
		stdio: "ignore",
		windowsHide: true
	}).unref();
}
/** 官方源连不上时改用的国内镜像源。 */
var DOMESTIC_BASE_IMAGE = "docker.m.daocloud.io/library/debian:bookworm";
/** 一次构建里同时写上两个国内软件源，一个包从第一个下不来就改从第二个下。失败后再原样重试一次，已下好的层会复用。 */
var DOMESTIC_MIRRORS = "mirrors.aliyun.com,mirrors.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn";
var DOMESTIC_ATTEMPTS = [{
	baseImage: DOMESTIC_BASE_IMAGE,
	aptMirror: DOMESTIC_MIRRORS
}, {
	baseImage: DOMESTIC_BASE_IMAGE,
	aptMirror: DOMESTIC_MIRRORS
}];
/** 把下载日志收成一句用户能看懂的话。技术细节不往页面上放。 */
function friendlyBuildLine(line) {
	const text = line.trim();
	if (!text) return null;
	if (/^连不上|^软件包下载中断|failed to (authorize|fetch anonymous token|fetch oauth token)|auth\.docker\.io/.test(text)) return "官方下载地址连不上，正在改用国内地址继续。";
	if (/naming to|exporting to image/.test(text)) return "隔离环境已经准备好。";
	if (/firefox-esr/.test(text)) return "正在安装浏览器。";
	if (/fonts-wqy|fontconfig/.test(text)) return "正在安装中文字体。";
	if (/xfce4/.test(text)) return "正在安装桌面。";
	if (/apt-get update|Reading package lists|^Get:/.test(text)) return "正在获取软件清单。";
	if (/apt-get .*install|Setting up |Unpacking /.test(text)) return "正在安装隔离环境需要的软件。";
	if (/load metadata|Pulling|Downloading|pulling from/.test(text)) return "正在下载基础系统。";
	return null;
}
function buildArgs(contextDir, source) {
	const args = [
		"build",
		"-t",
		"murasame-isolation:local"
	];
	if (source.baseImage) args.push("--build-arg", `BASE_IMAGE=${source.baseImage}`);
	if (source.aptMirror) args.push("--build-arg", `APT_MIRROR=${source.aptMirror}`);
	return [...args, contextDir];
}
function buildOnce(contextDir, source, onLine, signal) {
	return new Promise((resolve) => {
		const tail = [];
		const child = (0, node_child_process.spawn)("docker", buildArgs(contextDir, source), {
			windowsHide: true,
			signal
		});
		const read = (chunk) => {
			for (const line of chunk.toString("utf8").split(/\r?\n/)) {
				const text = line.trim();
				if (!text) continue;
				tail.push(text);
				if (tail.length > 120) tail.shift();
				if (!signal?.aborted) onLine(text);
			}
		};
		child.stdout.on("data", read);
		child.stderr.on("data", read);
		child.on("error", (error) => resolve({
			code: -1,
			output: `没能运行 docker：${error.message}`
		}));
		child.on("close", (code) => resolve({
			code,
			output: tail.join("\n")
		}));
	});
}
/** 国内网络下最常见的两种失败：拉不到基础镜像，或者软件包下载到一半断掉。 */
function networkFailure(output) {
	if (/failed to (authorize|fetch anonymous token|fetch oauth token|resolve source metadata)|auth\.docker\.io|registry-1\.docker\.io/.test(output)) return "registry";
	if (/Failed to fetch|Unable to connect to|Temporary failure resolving|Could not resolve/.test(output)) return "apt";
	return null;
}
function explainBuildFailure(output) {
	const kind = networkFailure(output);
	if (kind === "registry" || kind === "apt") return "隔离环境没能下载完。检查网络后再点一次「开始活动」，已经下载的部分会接着用。";
	if (/no space left on device/.test(output)) return "磁盘空间不够，隔离环境没有准备完。清理出一些空间后再点一次开始。";
	return (output.split("\n").filter((line) => /ERROR|\bE: /.test(line)).slice(-3).join(" ") || output.split("\n").at(-1) || "没有输出").slice(0, 400);
}
/** 用 isolation/Dockerfile 构建镜像。第一次要下载系统和浏览器，可能要十几分钟。 */
async function buildIsolationImage(contextDir, onLine, options = {}) {
	if (options.signal?.aborted) return {
		ok: false,
		detail: "活动准备已取消"
	};
	let attempt = await buildOnce(contextDir, {}, onLine, options.signal);
	if (options.signal?.aborted) return {
		ok: false,
		detail: "活动准备已取消"
	};
	if (attempt.code === 0) return {
		ok: true,
		detail: "隔离环境已经准备好。"
	};
	let kind = networkFailure(attempt.output);
	for (const source of DOMESTIC_ATTEMPTS) {
		if (!kind) break;
		onLine(kind === "registry" ? "连不上官方地址，改用国内地址继续。" : "软件包下载中断，用国内地址再试一次。");
		if (options.signal?.aborted) return {
			ok: false,
			detail: "活动准备已取消"
		};
		attempt = await buildOnce(contextDir, source, onLine, options.signal);
		if (options.signal?.aborted) return {
			ok: false,
			detail: "活动准备已取消"
		};
		if (attempt.code === 0) return {
			ok: true,
			detail: "隔离环境已经准备好。"
		};
		kind = networkFailure(attempt.output);
	}
	return {
		ok: false,
		detail: explainBuildFailure(attempt.output)
	};
}
function dockerUnavailableMessage() {
	return "Docker 没有启动。自主活动已停止，不会改到宿主桌面。请先启动 Docker Desktop，再重新开启活动。";
}
function createArgs(plan, port = 6901) {
	const args = [
		"run",
		"-d",
		"--network",
		plan.network,
		"--memory",
		plan.memory,
		"--cpus",
		plan.cpus,
		"--pids-limit",
		"1024"
	];
	for (const mount of plan.mounts) {
		const parsed = typeof mount === "string" ? /^(.*):([^:]+):(ro|rw)$/.exec(mount) : null;
		const value = typeof mount === "string" ? parsed ? {
			source: parsed[1],
			target: parsed[2],
			readOnly: parsed[3] === "ro"
		} : null : mount;
		if (!value || /[,\r\n]/.test(value.source + value.target) || !value.target.startsWith("/")) throw new Error("共享目录格式无效");
		args.push("--mount", `type=bind,${value.readOnly ? "readonly," : ""}source=${value.source},target=${value.target}`);
	}
	if (plan.devices.length > 0) args.push("--device", plan.devices.join(","));
	args.push("--label", OWNER_LABEL, "--label", `io.murasame.profile=${ownerProfile}`, "--label", `io.murasame.session=${plan.sessionId ?? RUNTIME_SESSION}`);
	if (port > 0) args.push("-p", `127.0.0.1:${port}:6901`);
	args.push("--name", `murasame-isolation-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`);
	args.push(plan.image);
	return args;
}
/** One process session owns only containers carrying both labels. */
var OWNER_LABEL = "io.murasame.owner=desktop";
var IMAGE_SCHEMA_TEMPLATE = "{{index .Config.Labels \"io.murasame.isolation.schema\"}}";
var RUNTIME_SESSION = (0, node_crypto.randomUUID)();
var ownerProfile = `unconfigured-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`;
/** Configure once before creating a container. Pass a stable hash of this app's userData path. */
function configureContainerOwner(profile) {
	if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$/.test(profile)) throw new Error("隔离容器配置标识无效");
	ownerProfile = profile;
}
/** Bounded async subprocess, also used by local perception. No shell interpolation. */
function runCommandAsync(command, args, options = {}) {
	if (options.signal?.aborted) return Promise.reject(/* @__PURE__ */ new Error("操作已取消"));
	return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
		const child = (0, node_child_process.execFile)(command, args, {
			encoding: "utf8",
			windowsHide: true,
			timeout: options.timeoutMs ?? 6e4,
			maxBuffer: 25165824,
			signal: options.signal,
			env: options.env
		}, (error, stdout, stderr) => {
			if (error) reject(/* @__PURE__ */ new Error(options.signal?.aborted ? "操作已取消" : `${command} 执行失败（${error.killed ? "超时或中止" : error.code ?? "未知状态"}）${stderr || stdout ? `: ${(stderr || stdout).slice(-2e3)}` : ""}`));
			else resolve(stdout);
		});
		child.stdin?.on("error", () => {});
		child.stdin?.end(options.input);
	});
}
async function dockerAvailableAsync(run = runCommandAsync, options = {}) {
	if (options.signal?.aborted) return false;
	try {
		await run("docker", ["version"], {
			...options,
			timeoutMs: 8e3
		});
		return true;
	} catch {
		return false;
	}
}
async function runInContainerAsync(id, command, run = runCommandAsync, options = {}) {
	if (options.signal?.aborted) throw new Error("操作已取消");
	return run("docker", [
		"exec",
		...[
			...options.input !== void 0 ? ["-i"] : [],
			...options.detach ? ["-d"] : [],
			...options.desktop ? ["-u", "murasame"] : []
		],
		id,
		...options.desktop ? ["env", "DISPLAY=:1"] : [],
		...command
	], options);
}
async function containerRunningAsync(id, run = runCommandAsync, options = {}) {
	try {
		return (await run("docker", [
			"inspect",
			"-f",
			"{{.State.Running}}",
			id
		], {
			...options,
			timeoutMs: 8e3
		})).trim() === "true";
	} catch {
		return false;
	}
}
async function isolationEnvironmentAsync(run = runCommandAsync, options = {}) {
	if (!await dockerAvailableAsync(run, options)) return {
		docker: false,
		image: false,
		detail: "开始活动时会准备隔离运行环境。"
	};
	try {
		const exists = Boolean((await run("docker", [
			"images",
			"-q",
			"murasame-isolation:local"
		], {
			...options,
			timeoutMs: 8e3
		})).trim());
		const image = exists && (await run("docker", [
			"image",
			"inspect",
			"-f",
			IMAGE_SCHEMA_TEMPLATE,
			"murasame-isolation:local"
		], {
			...options,
			timeoutMs: 8e3
		})).trim() === "3";
		return {
			docker: true,
			image,
			detail: image ? "隔离环境已就绪。" : exists ? "隔离镜像需要升级，开始活动时会准备新版运行配置。" : "首次活动需要准备隔离镜像。"
		};
	} catch {
		return {
			docker: true,
			image: false,
			detail: "无法查询隔离镜像。"
		};
	}
}
async function stopContainerAsync(id, run = runCommandAsync) {
	try {
		await run("docker", [
			"rm",
			"-f",
			id
		], { timeoutMs: 15e3 });
		return true;
	} catch {
		return false;
	}
}
async function startContainerAsync(plan, port = 6901, run = runCommandAsync, options = {}) {
	const args = createArgs(plan, port);
	if (options.readiness === "process") {
		args[args.indexOf("--pids-limit") + 1] = "256";
		args.push("sleep", "infinity");
	}
	const name = args[args.indexOf("--name") + 1];
	let id = "";
	let creationAttempted = false;
	try {
		if (options.signal?.aborted) throw new Error("操作已取消");
		if ((await run("docker", [
			"image",
			"inspect",
			"-f",
			IMAGE_SCHEMA_TEMPLATE,
			plan.image
		], {
			signal: options.signal,
			timeoutMs: 8e3
		})).trim() !== "3") return { failed: "隔离镜像需要升级，请先在自主活动中准备新版运行环境。" };
		const old = await run("docker", [
			"ps",
			"-aq",
			"--filter",
			`label=${OWNER_LABEL}`,
			"--filter",
			`label=io.murasame.profile=${ownerProfile}`,
			"--filter",
			`label=io.murasame.session=${plan.sessionId ?? RUNTIME_SESSION}`,
			"--filter",
			"status=exited"
		], { timeoutMs: 8e3 });
		for (const candidate of old.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)) {
			if (options.signal?.aborted) throw new Error("操作已取消");
			if (!await stopContainerAsync(candidate, run)) throw new Error("旧隔离容器清理失败");
		}
		if (options.signal?.aborted) throw new Error("操作已取消");
		creationAttempted = true;
		id = (await run("docker", args, { timeoutMs: 3e4 })).trim();
		if (!id) throw new Error("容器没有启动，没有拿到容器 ID");
		if (options.readiness === "process") {
			if (!await containerRunningAsync(id, run, { signal: options.signal })) throw new Error("隔离进程没有就绪");
			if (options.signal?.aborted) throw new Error("操作已取消");
			return {
				id,
				port,
				reason: "隔离进程已启动"
			};
		}
		const wait = options.wait ?? ((ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms)));
		for (let attempt = 0; attempt < (options.readinessAttempts ?? 30); attempt++) {
			if (options.signal?.aborted) throw new Error("操作已取消");
			try {
				await runInContainerAsync(id, [
					"sh",
					"-c",
					"xdotool getdisplaygeometry >/dev/null && command -v scrot >/dev/null && command -v libreoffice >/dev/null && command -v code-server >/dev/null && python3 -c \"import socket, urllib.request; urllib.request.urlopen('http://127.0.0.1:6901/vnc.html', timeout=2).read(1); socket.create_connection(('127.0.0.1',9222),2).close()\""
				], run, {
					desktop: true,
					signal: options.signal,
					timeoutMs: 4e3
				});
				if (options.signal?.aborted) throw new Error("操作已取消");
				return {
					id,
					port,
					reason: "隔离桌面已启动并确认可以采集画面"
				};
			} catch {
				if (attempt + 1 >= (options.readinessAttempts ?? 30)) throw new Error("隔离桌面没有就绪");
				await wait(500);
			}
		}
		throw new Error("隔离桌面没有就绪");
	} catch (error) {
		if (!creationAttempted) return { failed: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "容器准备检查失败" };
		const removed = await stopContainerAsync(id || name, run);
		return {
			failed: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "容器启动失败",
			...!removed ? { id: id || name } : {}
		};
	}
}
/** Startup-only recovery. Caller must run this before accepting new activities/tasks. */
async function cleanupOwnedContainersAsync(run = runCommandAsync, options = {}) {
	const removed = [];
	const failed = [];
	const ids = (await run("docker", [
		"ps",
		"-aq",
		"--filter",
		`label=${OWNER_LABEL}`,
		"--filter",
		`label=io.murasame.profile=${ownerProfile}`
	], { timeoutMs: 8e3 })).trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
	for (const id of ids) {
		if (options.activeIds?.includes(id)) continue;
		if (await stopContainerAsync(id, run)) removed.push(id);
		else failed.push(id);
	}
	return {
		removed,
		failed
	};
}

//#region src/main/privacy/redact.ts
function safeLogLine(message) {
	return message.replace(/sk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}/g, "sk-••••").replace(/data:image\/[a-z0-9.+-]+;base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]{16,}/gi, "[已省略屏幕图像]");
}
function logExpired(ageDays, retentionDays = 14) {
	return ageDays > retentionDays;
}

var logger_exports = /* @__PURE__ */ __exportAll({ default: () => logger_default });
electron_log_main.default.initialize();
electron_log_main.default.hooks.push((message) => ({
	...message,
	data: message.data.map((item) => typeof item === "string" ? safeLogLine(item) : item)
}));
electron_log_main.default.transports.file.level = "info";
electron_log_main.default.transports.console.level = process.env.NODE_ENV === "development" ? "debug" : "info";
electron_log_main.default.transports.file.maxSize = 5242880;
electron_log_main.default.transports.file.format = "[{y}-{m}-{d} {h}:{i}:{s}.{ms}] [{level}] {text}";
var logger_default = electron_log_main.default;

	return quitting;

	quitting = true;
}

	quitting = true;
	electron.app.quit();
}

//#region src/main/data/capacity.ts
var DATABASE_FILE_NAME = "murasame.db";
var SOFT_LIMIT_BYTES = 2147483648;
function databasePath(userDataDir) {
	return (0, node_path.join)(userDataDir, DATABASE_FILE_NAME);
}
function capacityStatus(sizeBytes) {
	if (sizeBytes >= Math.floor(1717986918.4)) return {
		warning: true,
		message: `本地数据库已达到容量软上限的 80%（${sizeBytes} / ${SOFT_LIMIT_BYTES} 字节）。请清理历史、记忆或日志。`
	};
	return {
		warning: false,
		message: null
	};
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/data/recovery.ts
var DATABASE_KEY_FILE = "murasame-db-key.json";
function databaseRecoveryError(reason, file, cause) {
	const directory = (0, node_path.dirname)(file);
	return new Error(`${reason}。已停止打开，现有数据库和密钥文件保留原样。\n\n请先退出应用并备份整个资料目录：${directory}\n使用创建这些数据时的 Windows 帐户，恢复同一份完整资料备份后再启动，必须包括 Local State、数据库 ${file} 及其日志文件和密钥 ${(0, node_path.join)(directory, DATABASE_KEY_FILE)}。\n请勿单独删除或重新生成密钥；没有原始密钥无法恢复原有加密数据。`, { cause });
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/data/cipher.ts
function openCipherDatabase(file, key) {
	if (!/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(key)) throw new Error("数据库密钥格式无效");
	const isNew = !(0, node_fs.existsSync)(file) || (0, node_fs.statSync)(file).size === 0;
	const db = new better_sqlite3_multiple_ciphers.default(file);
	try {
		db.pragma(`cipher='sqlcipher'`);
		db.pragma("legacy=4");
		db.pragma(isNew ? `rekey='${key}'` : `key='${key}'`);
		db.prepare("SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_master").get();
		db.pragma("foreign_keys = ON");
		return db;
	} catch (cause) {
		db.close();
		if (!isNew && cause && typeof cause === "object" && "code" in cause && (cause.code === "SQLITE_NOTADB" || cause.code === "SQLITE_CORRUPT")) throw databaseRecoveryError("现有数据库无法使用已保存的密钥打开，可能是密钥不匹配或数据库已损坏", file, cause);
		throw cause;
	}
}

//#region src/main/data/keys.ts
var store$1 = null;
/**
* 延迟构造：electron-store 在构造时就固定 userData 路径，
* 过早创建会让 MURASAME_USER_DATA 这类路径覆盖失效。
*/
function keyStore() {
	if (!store$1) store$1 = new electron_store.default({
		name: "murasame-db-key",
		defaults: { encryptedKey: null }
	});
	return store$1;
}
function getDatabaseKey() {
	const file = databasePath(getUserDataDir());
	const keyFile = (0, node_path.join)(getUserDataDir(), DATABASE_KEY_FILE);
	const hasDatabase = (0, node_fs.existsSync)(file);
	const hasSidecars = [
		"-wal",
		"-shm",
		"-journal"
	].some((suffix) => (0, node_fs.existsSync)(`${file}${suffix}`));
	if (!hasDatabase && hasSidecars) throw databaseRecoveryError("数据库主文件缺失，但仍有数据库日志文件；没有创建新数据库", file);
	let existing;
	if ((0, node_fs.existsSync)(keyFile)) try {
		const saved = JSON.parse((0, node_fs.readFileSync)(keyFile, "utf8"));
		if (!saved || typeof saved !== "object" || Array.isArray(saved)) throw new Error("密钥记录格式无效");
		existing = saved.encryptedKey;
	} catch (cause) {
		throw databaseRecoveryError("无法读取已保存的数据库密钥记录", file, cause);
	}
	if (hasDatabase && (typeof existing !== "string" || !existing)) throw databaseRecoveryError("检测到已有数据库，但缺少对应的密钥记录；没有生成替代密钥", file);
	if (!electron.safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) throw databaseRecoveryError("Windows 安全存储当前不可用，无法读取或保管数据库主密钥；请先恢复 Windows 安全存储后重试", file);
	if (existing !== void 0 && existing !== null) try {
		if (typeof existing !== "string" || !existing) throw new Error("密钥记录格式无效");
		const key = electron.safeStorage.decryptString(Buffer.from(existing, "base64"));
		if (!/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(key)) throw new Error("数据库密钥格式无效");
		return key;
	} catch (cause) {
		throw databaseRecoveryError("本机安全存储无法解密或验证已保存的数据库密钥", file, cause);
	}
	const key = (0, node_crypto.randomBytes)(32).toString("hex");
	keyStore().set("encryptedKey", electron.safeStorage.encryptString(key).toString("base64"));
	return key;
}
function getUserDataDir() {
	return electron.app.getPath("userData");
}

//#region src/main/data/upgrade.ts
function rejectDowngrade(storedVersion, supportedVersion) {
	if (storedVersion > supportedVersion) return `数据库版本 ${storedVersion} 高于本程序支持的 ${supportedVersion}。已拒绝打开，避免损坏数据。`;
	return null;
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/data/migrate.ts
function migrate(db, migrations) {
	db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL)`);
	const currentRow = db.prepare(`SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key = 'schema_version'`).get();
	let current = Number(currentRow?.value ?? 0);
	const newest = [...migrations].reduce((max, item) => Math.max(max, item.version), 0);
	const rejection = rejectDowngrade(current, newest);
	if (rejection) throw new Error(rejection);
	const writeVersion = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO meta(key, value) VALUES('schema_version', ?)
     ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value`);
	const ordered = [...migrations].sort((a, b) => a.version - b.version);
	for (const migration of ordered) {
		if (migration.version <= current) continue;
		db.transaction(() => {
			db.exec(migration.sql);
			writeVersion.run(String(migration.version));
		})();
		current = migration.version;
	}
	return current;
}

var cached$1 = null;
function openMigratedDatabase(file, key, migrations = embeddedMigrations) {
	const db = openCipherDatabase(file, key);
	try {
		migrate(db, migrations);
		return db;
	} catch (error) {
		db.close();
		throw error;
	}
}
function getDatabase() {
	if (cached$1) return cached$1;
	cached$1 = openMigratedDatabase(databasePath(getUserDataDir()), getDatabaseKey());
	return cached$1;
}
function databaseCapacityWarning(file = databasePath(getUserDataDir())) {
	try {
		return capacityStatus((0, node_fs.statSync)(file).size).message;
	} catch {
		return null;
	}
}

//#region src/main/model/transport.ts
function redactProviderText(text, secrets = []) {
	let safe = text;
	for (const secret of secrets.filter(Boolean)) for (const form of /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
		secret,
		encodeURIComponent(secret),
		JSON.stringify(secret).slice(1, -1)
	])) safe = safe.split(form).join("••••");
	return safe.replace(/sk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}/g, "sk-••••").replace(/sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}/g, "sk-ant-••••").replace(/(api[-_ ]?key\s*[:=]\s*)\S+/gi, "$1••••").replace(/(Bearer\s+)\S+/gi, "$1••••").slice(0, 500);
}
function assertProtectedEndpoint(endpoint) {
	let url;
	try {
		url = new URL(endpoint);
	} catch {
		throw new Error("服务地址格式无效，请填写完整的 HTTPS 地址");
	}
	if (url.username || url.password) throw new Error("服务地址不得包含凭据，请在 API Key 栏填写密钥");
	const loopback = url.hostname === "localhost" || url.hostname === "127.0.0.1" || url.hostname === "[::1]";
	if (url.protocol !== "https:" && !(url.protocol === "http:" && loopback)) throw new Error("远程模型服务必须使用 HTTPS，以保护密钥和对话内容；仅本机地址允许 HTTP");
}
function classifyProviderFailure(status, body, secrets = []) {
	const detail = redactProviderText(body.trim(), secrets);
	if (status === 400 && /does not support image/i.test(detail)) return /* @__PURE__ */ new Error("该模型不支持图像输入。请更换支持图像的模型，或改用文字描述。");
	if (status === 401 || status === 403) return /* @__PURE__ */ new Error("API Key 无效、已过期或没有访问该模型的权限（HTTP 401/403）");
	if (status === 404 && /model/i.test(detail)) return /* @__PURE__ */ new Error("模型名称不存在（HTTP 404），请检查模型 ID");
	if (status === 404) return /* @__PURE__ */ new Error("接口地址或模型名称不存在（HTTP 404），请检查服务地址和模型 ID");
	if (status === 429) return /* @__PURE__ */ new Error("请求过于频繁或额度不足（HTTP 429），请稍后重试");
	return /* @__PURE__ */ new Error(detail ? `模型服务返回 HTTP ${status}：${detail}` : `模型服务返回 HTTP ${status}`);
}
function extractText(payload) {
	if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") return "";
	const value = payload;
	const first = (Array.isArray(value.choices) ? value.choices : [])[0];
	if (first) {
		const message = first.message;
		const toolCalls = message?.tool_calls;
		if (Array.isArray(toolCalls) && toolCalls[0] && typeof toolCalls[0] === "object") {
			const fn = toolCalls[0].function;
			if (typeof fn?.arguments === "string") return fn.arguments;
		}
		const delta = first.delta;
		if (typeof delta?.content === "string") return delta.content;
		if (typeof message?.content === "string") return message.content;
	}
	if (value.type === "content_block_delta") {
		const delta = value.delta;
		if (typeof delta?.text === "string") return delta.text;
	}
	if (Array.isArray(value.content)) return value.content.map((item) => {
		if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "";
		const block = item;
		if (block.type === "tool_use" && block.input && typeof block.input === "object") return JSON.stringify(block.input);
		return block.type === "text" && typeof block.text === "string" ? block.text : "";
	}).join("");
	return "";
}
async function readEventStream(response, onDelta) {
	if (!response.body) throw new Error("模型服务没有返回可读取的响应流");
	const reader = response.body.getReader();
	const decoder = new TextDecoder();
	let buffer = "";
	let result = "";
	let completed = false;
	const consumeLine = (line) => {
		const trimmed = line.trim();
		if (!trimmed.startsWith("data:")) return;
		const data = trimmed.slice(5).trim();
		if (!data) return;
		if (data === "[DONE]") {
			completed = true;
			return;
		}
		let payload;
		try {
			payload = JSON.parse(data);
		} catch {
			throw new Error("模型响应流包含无法读取的数据，回复未完成，请重试");
		}
		if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") return;
		if (payload.type === "error" || payload.error) throw new Error("模型响应流返回错误，回复未完成，请重试");
		if (payload.type === "message_stop") completed = true;
		const choices = Array.isArray(payload.choices) ? payload.choices : [];
		for (const choice of choices) {
			const reason = choice && typeof choice === "object" ? choice.finish_reason : null;
			if (typeof reason !== "string" || !reason) continue;
			if (![
				"stop",
				"tool_calls",
				"function_call"
			].includes(reason)) throw new Error(reason === "length" ? "模型回复达到长度限制，内容未完成，请缩小问题范围后重试" : "模型服务中止了回复，内容未完成，请调整输入后重试");
			completed = true;
		}
		if (payload.type === "message_delta" && payload.delta && typeof payload.delta === "object") {
			const reason = payload.delta.stop_reason;
			if (typeof reason === "string" && reason && ![
				"end_turn",
				"stop_sequence",
				"tool_use"
			].includes(reason)) throw new Error(reason === "max_tokens" ? "模型回复达到长度限制，内容未完成，请缩小问题范围后重试" : "模型服务中止了回复，内容未完成，请调整输入后重试");
		}
		const text = extractText(payload);
		if (text) {
			result += text;
			onDelta(text);
		}
	};
	try {
		while (true) {
			const { done, value } = await reader.read();
			if (done) break;
			buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
			const lines = buffer.split("\n");
			buffer = lines.pop() ?? "";
			lines.forEach(consumeLine);
		}
		buffer += decoder.decode();
		if (buffer) consumeLine(buffer);
		if (!completed) throw new Error("模型响应流提前中断，回复未完成，请重试");
		return result;
	} finally {
		await reader.cancel().catch(() => void 0);
		reader.releaseLock();
	}
}
function assertImagePlacement(messages, imagesInUserMessageOnly) {
	if (!imagesInUserMessageOnly) return;
	if (messages.some((message) => message.role !== "user" && (message.images?.length ?? 0) > 0)) throw new Error("当前协议只允许把图片放在用户消息中。请不要把截图放进系统提示。");
}

//#region src/main/model/adapters/anthropic.ts
function anthropicUrl(endpoint) {
	const base = endpoint.trim().replace(/\/+$/, "");
	if (base.endsWith("/messages")) return base;
	if (base.endsWith("/v1")) return `${base}/messages`;
	return `${base}/v1/messages`;
}
function blocks(message) {
	return [...message.content ? [{
		type: "text",
		text: message.content
	}] : [], ...(message.images ?? []).map((image) => ({
		type: "image",
		source: {
			type: "base64",
			media_type: image.mime,
			data: image.base64
		}
	}))];
}
function normalize$2(messages) {
	const result = [];
	for (const message of messages) {
		if (message.role === "system") continue;
		const current = result.at(-1);
		if (!current && message.role === "assistant") continue;
		if (current?.role === message.role) {
			current.content += `\n\n${message.content}`;
			current.images = [...current.images ?? [], ...message.images ?? []];
		} else result.push({
			...message,
			images: message.images ? [...message.images] : void 0
		});
	}
	return result;
}
var anthropicAdapter = {
	id: "anthropic",
	matches: (provider) => {
		const value = provider.toLowerCase();
		return value.includes("anthropic") || value.includes("claude");
	},
	constraints: {
		imagesInUserMessageOnly: true,
		systemMessageStyle: "separate-field",
		toolCallingStyle: "anthropic-tools",
		requiresApiKey: true
	},
	buildChat(model, messages, stream, tools) {
		assertProtectedEndpoint(model.endpoint);
		assertImagePlacement(messages, true);
		const system = messages.find((message) => message.role === "system")?.content;
		return {
			url: anthropicUrl(model.endpoint),
			headers: {
				"Content-Type": "application/json",
				Accept: stream ? "text/event-stream" : "application/json",
				"x-api-key": model.apiKey,
				"anthropic-version": "2023-06-01"
			},
			body: JSON.stringify({
				model: model.model,
				max_tokens: stream ? 4096 : 1024,
				temperature: .7,
				...system ? { system } : {},
				messages: normalize$2(messages).map((message) => ({
					role: message.role,
					content: blocks(message)
				})),
				...tools?.length ? { tools: tools.map((tool) => ({
					name: tool.name,
					description: tool.description,
					input_schema: tool.parameters
				})) } : {},
				stream
			})
		};
	}
};
//#endregion
//#region src/main/model/adapters/openai-compatible.ts
function openAiContent(message) {
	if (!message.images?.length) return message.content;
	return [...message.content ? [{
		type: "text",
		text: message.content
	}] : [], ...message.images.map((image) => ({
		type: "image_url",
		image_url: { url: `data:${image.mime};base64,${image.base64}` }
	}))];
}
function openAiCompatibleUrl(endpoint) {
	let base = endpoint.trim().replace(/\/+$/, "");
	if (base.endsWith("/chat/completions")) return base;
	try {
		const parsed = new URL(base);
		if (!parsed.pathname || parsed.pathname === "/") base = `${parsed.origin}/v1`;
	} catch {}
	return `${base}/chat/completions`;
}
var openAiCompatibleAdapter = {
	id: "openai-compatible",
	matches: () => true,
	constraints: {
		imagesInUserMessageOnly: true,
		systemMessageStyle: "in-messages",
		toolCallingStyle: "openai-tools",
		requiresApiKey: true
	},
	buildChat(model, messages, stream, tools) {
		assertProtectedEndpoint(model.endpoint);
		assertImagePlacement(messages, true);
		return {
			url: openAiCompatibleUrl(model.endpoint),
			headers: {
				"Content-Type": "application/json",
				Accept: stream ? "text/event-stream" : "application/json",
				...model.apiKey ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${model.apiKey}` } : {}
			},
			body: JSON.stringify({
				model: model.model,
				messages: messages.map((message) => ({
					role: message.role,
					content: openAiContent(message)
				})),
				temperature: .7,
				...tools?.length ? {
					tools: tools.map((tool) => ({
						type: "function",
						function: tool
					})),
					tool_choice: "auto"
				} : {},
				...stream ? { stream: true } : {}
			})
		};
	}
};
function withSystem(messages, system, _style) {
	const rest = messages.filter((message) => message.role !== "system");
	if (!system) return rest;
	return [{
		role: "system",
		content: system
	}, ...rest];
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/model/adapters/ollama.ts
var ollamaAdapter = {
	id: "ollama",
	matches: (provider) => provider.toLowerCase().includes("ollama"),
	constraints: {
		...openAiCompatibleAdapter.constraints,
		requiresApiKey: false
	},
	buildChat(model, messages, stream, tools) {
		const endpoint = model.endpoint.trim().replace(/\/+$/, "").replace(/\/api$/, "");
		return openAiCompatibleAdapter.buildChat({
			...model,
			endpoint
		}, messages, stream, tools);
	}
};

//#region src/main/agent/runtime.ts
function compactContext(goal, steps, recentCount = 6) {
	const recent = steps.slice(-recentCount);
	const older = steps.slice(0, Math.max(0, steps.length - recentCount));
	const queryTerms = goal.match(/[\p{L}\p{N}]{2,}/gu) ?? [];
	const candidates = older.flatMap((step, index) => (step.evidence.match(/[^。！？!?\n]+[。！？!?]?/g) ?? []).map((sentence, part) => ({
		step,
		index,
		part,
		sentence,
		score: (/约定|代号|名字|叫我|偏好|喜欢|不吃|过敏|必须|不得|不要|禁止|限制|更正|改为|改成|废弃|目标|尚未|待办|计划|决定|项目/.test(sentence) ? 8 : 0) + (step.role === "user" ? 2 : 0) + (queryTerms.some((term) => sentence.includes(term)) ? 4 : 0)
	})));
	const selected = [];
	let budget = 12e3;
	for (const item of [...candidates].sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || b.index - a.index || a.part - b.part)) {
		const cost = item.sentence.length + item.step.id.length + 50;
		if (cost > budget) continue;
		selected.push(item);
		budget -= cost;
	}
	const summary = selected.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index || a.part - b.part).map(({ step, sentence }) => `[${step.id}${step.role ? ` ${step.role}` : ""}${step.time ? ` ${step.time}` : ""}] ${sentence}`).join("\n");
	const omitted = candidates.length - selected.length;
	return {
		goal,
		recent,
		summary: summary + (omitted ? `\n（更早记录摘录省略${omitted}句；未列出的内容不能视为不存在，可搜索原始历史核实。）` : "")
	};
}
var RequestQueue = class {
	pending = Promise.resolve();
	waiting = 0;
	get waitingCount() {
		return this.waiting;
	}
	enqueue(job) {
		this.waiting += 1;
		const run = this.pending.then(async () => {
			this.waiting -= 1;
			return job();
		});
		this.pending = run.then(() => void 0, () => void 0);
		return run;
	}
};
//#endregion
//#region src/shared/grounded-summary.ts
var SUMMARY_LIMITS = {
	files: 5,
	fileBytes: 32768,
	totalBytes: 65536,
	sourceChars: 24e3,
	draftChars: 12e3,
	runMs: 12e4,
	dispatches: 3
};
//#endregion
//#region src/main/tools/filesystem.ts
function resolveInside(root, target) {
	const base = (0, node_path.resolve)(root);
	const full = (0, node_path.resolve)(base, target);
	if (full !== base && !full.startsWith(base + node_path.sep)) throw new Error("授权目录外的路径被拒绝");
	if ((0, node_fs.existsSync)(base)) {
		const realBase = (0, node_fs.realpathSync)(base);
		let ancestor = full;
		while (!(0, node_fs.existsSync)(ancestor)) {
			try {
				if ((0, node_fs.lstatSync)(ancestor).isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error("授权目录外的重解析路径被拒绝");
			} catch (error) {
				if (error.code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
			}
			if ((0, node_path.dirname)(ancestor) === ancestor) throw new Error("授权目录外的路径被拒绝");
			ancestor = (0, node_path.dirname)(ancestor);
		}
		const actual = (0, node_fs.realpathSync)(ancestor);
		if (actual !== realBase && !actual.startsWith(realBase + node_path.sep)) throw new Error("授权目录外的真实路径被拒绝");
		for (let part = full; part !== base; part = (0, node_path.dirname)(part)) try {
			if ((0, node_fs.lstatSync)(part).isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error("授权目录外的重解析路径被拒绝");
		} catch (error) {
			if (error.code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
		}
	}
	return full;
}
function writeInside(root, relativePath, content) {
	const file = resolveInside(root, relativePath);
	(0, node_fs.mkdirSync)((0, node_path.resolve)(file, ".."), { recursive: true });
	resolveInside(root, relativePath);
	(0, node_fs.writeFileSync)(file, content, {
		encoding: "utf8",
		flag: "wx"
	});
	return {
		path: file,
		hash: (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(content).digest("hex")
	};
}
function readInside(root, relativePath) {
	return (0, node_fs.readFileSync)(resolveInside(root, relativePath), "utf8");
}
function moveInside(root, from, to) {
	const source = resolveInside(root, from);
	const target = resolveInside(root, to);
	(0, node_fs.copyFileSync)(source, target, node_fs.constants.COPYFILE_EXCL);
	(0, node_fs.unlinkSync)(resolveInside(root, from));
}
function folderFor(extension) {
	if ([
		".png",
		".jpg",
		".jpeg",
		".gif",
		".webp",
		".bmp"
	].includes(extension)) return "图片";
	if ([
		".txt",
		".md",
		".doc",
		".docx",
		".pdf",
		".csv",
		".json"
	].includes(extension)) return "文档";
	if ([
		".ts",
		".tsx",
		".js",
		".jsx",
		".py",
		".java",
		".cs",
		".go",
		".rs"
	].includes(extension)) return "代码";
	return "其他";
}
function organizeManifest(root) {
	return (0, node_fs.readdirSync)(resolveInside(root, "."), { withFileTypes: true }).filter((entry) => entry.isFile()).map((entry) => {
		const destination = `${folderFor((0, node_path.extname)(entry.name).toLowerCase())}/${entry.name}`;
		let blocked;
		try {
			resolveInside(root, destination);
		} catch (error) {
			blocked = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
		}
		return {
			source: entry.name,
			destination,
			hash: (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update((0, node_fs.readFileSync)(resolveInside(root, entry.name))).digest("hex"),
			conflict: (0, node_fs.existsSync)((0, node_path.resolve)(root, destination)),
			...blocked ? { blocked } : {}
		};
	});
}
/** Runs only the immutable approved source/target list, preserving every effect. */
async function organizeByKind(root, control) {
	const intents = control?.files ?? organizeManifest(root);
	const moved = [];
	const effects = [];
	let stopped = false;
	let observerError;
	for (const intent of intents) {
		if (control?.stopped() || control?.signal?.aborted) {
			stopped = true;
			break;
		}
		const effect = {
			source: (0, node_path.resolve)(root, intent.source),
			destination: (0, node_path.resolve)(root, intent.destination),
			hash: intent.hash,
			status: "failed"
		};
		try {
			const blocked = control?.guard?.();
			if (blocked) throw new Error(blocked);
			if (intent.blocked) throw new Error(intent.blocked);
			if ((0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update((0, node_fs.readFileSync)(resolveInside(root, intent.source))).digest("hex") !== intent.hash) throw new Error(`批准后原文件已变化，未移动：${intent.source}`);
			(0, node_fs.mkdirSync)(resolveInside(root, (0, node_path.dirname)(intent.destination)), { recursive: true });
			moveInside(root, intent.source, intent.destination);
			effect.status = "moved";
		} catch (error) {
			effect.error = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
			effects.push(effect);
			try {
				control?.effect?.(effect);
			} catch (error) {
				observerError = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
			}
			break;
		}
		effects.push(effect);
		moved.push(effect.destination);
		try {
			control?.effect?.(effect);
			control?.progress(moved.length, intents.length, `${intent.source} → ${(0, node_path.dirname)(intent.destination)}`);
		} catch (error) {
			observerError = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
			break;
		}
		await new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));
	}
	const note = stopped ? `取消前移动了 ${moved.length}/${intents.length} 个文件，其余没有动` : moved.length ? `移动了 ${moved.length} 个批准清单中的文件` : "批准清单里没有已移动的文件";
	return {
		moved,
		effects,
		total: intents.length,
		stopped,
		error: observerError,
		note: observerError ? `${note}；停止：${observerError}` : effects.some((item) => item.status === "failed") ? `${note}；停止：${effects.at(-1)?.error}` : note
	};
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/agent/grounded-summary.ts
var groundedWorkflowSchema = zod.z.object({
	kind: zod.z.literal("summarize-files"),
	sources: zod.z.array(zod.z.string().min(1).max(260)).min(1).max(SUMMARY_LIMITS.files),
	instruction: zod.z.string().trim().min(1).max(2e3),
	outputPath: zod.z.string().min(1).max(260)
}).strict();
var groundedDraftSchema = zod.z.object({
	title: zod.z.string().trim().min(1).max(120),
	sections: zod.z.array(zod.z.object({
		text: zod.z.string().trim().min(1).max(2e3),
		citations: zod.z.array(zod.z.object({
			sourceId: zod.z.string().regex(/^source-[1-5]$/),
			fromLine: zod.z.number().int().min(1),
			toLine: zod.z.number().int().min(1),
			quote: zod.z.string().trim().min(1).max(240)
		}).strict()).min(1).max(5)
	}).strict()).min(1).max(12),
	limitations: zod.z.array(zod.z.string().trim().min(1).max(300)).max(8)
}).strict();
function summaryHash(text) {
	return (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(text).digest("hex");
}
function summaryRelativePath(path, output = false) {
	if ((0, node_path.isAbsolute)(path) || /^[\\/]/.test(path) || /[:*?"<>|]/.test(path) || [...path].some((char) => char.charCodeAt(0) < 32) || path.split(/[\\/]/).some((part) => !part || part === "." || part === ".." || /[. ]$/.test(part) || /^(con|prn|aux|nul|com[1-9]|lpt[1-9])(\.|$)/i.test(part))) throw new Error("摘要只接受授权目录内明确的相对文件路径");
	if (!(output ? [".md"] : [".md", ".txt"]).includes((0, node_path.extname)(path).toLowerCase())) throw new Error(output ? "摘要输出必须是新的 .md 文件" : "摘要来源仅支持 UTF-8 的 .txt 或 .md 文件");
	return path.replaceAll("\\", "/");
}
function metadata(id, relativePath, stat) {
	if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error("摘要来源必须是普通文件，不能是目录或重解析路径");
	if (stat.size > SUMMARY_LIMITS.fileBytes) throw new Error("单份来源大小超过 32 KiB；请缩小来源后重试");
	if (stat.size === 0) throw new Error("摘要来源为空，请提供有内容的文件");
	return {
		id,
		relativePath,
		bytes: stat.size,
		mtimeMs: stat.mtimeMs,
		ctimeMs: stat.ctimeMs,
		dev: stat.dev,
		ino: stat.ino
	};
}
function sameIdentity(left, right) {
	return left.relativePath === right.relativePath && left.bytes === right.bytes && left.mtimeMs === right.mtimeMs && left.ctimeMs === right.ctimeMs && left.dev === right.dev && left.ino === right.ino;
}
function inspectSummarySources(root, paths) {
	if (!paths.length || paths.length > SUMMARY_LIMITS.files) throw new Error("请明确指定 1 至 5 份来源文件");
	const identities = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
	const names = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
	const manifest = paths.map((path, index) => {
		const relativePath = summaryRelativePath(path);
		const meta = metadata(`source-${index + 1}`, relativePath, (0, node_fs.lstatSync)(resolveInside(root, relativePath)));
		const key = `${meta.dev}:${meta.ino}`;
		if (identities.has(key) || names.has(relativePath.toLowerCase())) throw new Error("来源包含重复文件或同一文件的别名");
		identities.add(key);
		names.add(relativePath.toLowerCase());
		return meta;
	});
	if (manifest.reduce((total, source) => total + source.bytes, 0) > SUMMARY_LIMITS.totalBytes) throw new Error("来源总大小超过 64 KiB；请缩小来源后重试");
	return manifest;
}
function decodeSnapshot(meta, bytes) {
	if (bytes.length !== meta.bytes) throw new Error("来源读取期间发生变化，需重新批准");
	let text;
	try {
		text = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(bytes);
	} catch (error) {
		throw new Error("来源不是有效的 UTF-8 文本；请先转换编码", { cause: error });
	}
	if (!text.trim() || text.includes("\0")) throw new Error("来源为空或不是纯文本");
	return {
		...meta,
		sha256: summaryHash(bytes),
		text,
		lines: text.replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n").split("\n")
	};
}
function assertManifest(expected, actual) {
	if (expected.length !== actual.length || actual.some((item, index) => !sameIdentity(expected[index], item))) throw new Error("批准后来源文件发生变化，需重新批准");
}
/** Only this bounded reader reads source contents, after source approval. */
async function readSummarySources(root, paths, signal, expected) {
	signal.throwIfAborted();
	const manifest = inspectSummarySources(root, paths);
	if (expected) assertManifest(expected, manifest);
	const snapshots = [];
	for (const meta of manifest) {
		signal.throwIfAborted();
		const handle = await (0, node_fs_promises.open)(resolveInside(root, meta.relativePath), "r");
		try {
			if (!sameIdentity(meta, metadata(meta.id, meta.relativePath, await handle.stat()))) throw new Error("来源打开期间发生变化，需重新批准");
			const buffer = Buffer.alloc(SUMMARY_LIMITS.fileBytes + 1);
			let offset = 0;
			while (offset < buffer.length) {
				signal.throwIfAborted();
				const read = await handle.read(buffer, offset, buffer.length - offset, offset);
				if (!read.bytesRead) break;
				offset += read.bytesRead;
			}
			const after = metadata(meta.id, meta.relativePath, await handle.stat());
			const pathAfter = metadata(meta.id, meta.relativePath, (0, node_fs.lstatSync)(resolveInside(root, meta.relativePath)));
			if (!sameIdentity(meta, after) || !sameIdentity(meta, pathAfter)) throw new Error("来源读取期间发生变化，需重新批准");
			snapshots.push(decodeSnapshot(meta, buffer.subarray(0, offset)));
		} finally {
			await handle.close();
		}
	}
	signal.throwIfAborted();
	if (snapshots.reduce((count, source) => count + [...source.text].length, 0) > SUMMARY_LIMITS.sourceChars) throw new Error("来源总字符超过 24000；请缩小来源后重试");
	return snapshots;
}
/** Synchronous bounded recheck runs immediately before each actual network dispatch. */
function assertSummarySourcesUnchanged(root, sources) {
	assertManifest(sources, inspectSummarySources(root, sources.map((source) => source.relativePath)));
	for (const meta of sources) {
		if (!meta.sha256) continue;
		const fd = (0, node_fs.openSync)(resolveInside(root, meta.relativePath), "r");
		try {
			if (!sameIdentity(meta, metadata(meta.id, meta.relativePath, (0, node_fs.fstatSync)(fd)))) throw new Error("来源发生变化，需重新批准");
			const buffer = Buffer.alloc(SUMMARY_LIMITS.fileBytes + 1);
			let offset = 0;
			while (offset < buffer.length) {
				const count = (0, node_fs.readSync)(fd, buffer, offset, buffer.length - offset, offset);
				if (!count) break;
				offset += count;
			}
			if (offset !== meta.bytes || summaryHash(buffer.subarray(0, offset)) !== meta.sha256 || !sameIdentity(meta, metadata(meta.id, meta.relativePath, (0, node_fs.fstatSync)(fd))) || !sameIdentity(meta, metadata(meta.id, meta.relativePath, (0, node_fs.lstatSync)(resolveInside(root, meta.relativePath))))) throw new Error("来源内容发生变化，需重新批准");
		} finally {
			(0, node_fs.closeSync)(fd);
		}
	}
}
function plain(text) {
	return text.replace(/[\\`*_{}[\]()<>#!|~]/g, "\\$&");
}
function validateAndRenderSummary(value, sources) {
	const parsed = groundedDraftSchema.safeParse(value);
	if (!parsed.success) throw new Error("摘要结构或引用不符合约定，未生成文件");
	const draft = parsed.data;
	let quoteChars = 0;
	const sections = draft.sections.map((section, index) => {
		const citations = section.citations.map((citation) => {
			const source = sources.find((item) => item.id === citation.sourceId);
			if (!source || citation.fromLine > citation.toLine || citation.toLine > source.lines.length || !source.lines.slice(citation.fromLine - 1, citation.toLine).join("\n").includes(citation.quote)) throw new Error("摘要引用无法在本次真实来源的指定行核对，未生成文件");
			quoteChars += [...citation.quote].length;
			if (quoteChars > 2400) throw new Error("引用片段总量超过限制，需减少引用");
			return `> 来源：${plain(source.relativePath)}，L${citation.fromLine}–L${citation.toLine}（${source.id}）\n> ${plain(citation.quote).replaceAll("\n", "\n> ")}`;
		});
		return `## ${index + 1}\n\n${plain(section.text)}\n\n${citations.join("\n\n")}`;
	});
	const markdown = `# ${plain(draft.title)}\n\n${sections.join("\n\n")}${draft.limitations.length ? `\n\n## 限制与未确定事项\n\n${draft.limitations.map((item) => `- ${plain(item)}`).join("\n")}` : ""}\n\n---\n引用已与本次来源快照核对；这不代表所有语义判断已获独立事实认证。\n`;
	if ([...markdown].length > SUMMARY_LIMITS.draftChars) throw new Error("摘要超过 12000 字符，未生成文件");
	return {
		markdown,
		sha256: summaryHash(markdown)
	};
}

/** 模型常在一个 JSON 后面再跟一句或第二个对象。只取第一个完整对象，避免整段解析失败。 */
function firstJsonObject(text) {
	const start = text.indexOf("{");
	if (start < 0) return null;
	let depth = 0;
	let inString = false;
	let escaped = false;
	for (let index = start; index < text.length; index += 1) {
		const char = text[index];
		if (inString) {
			if (escaped) escaped = false;
			else if (char === "\\") escaped = true;
			else if (char === "\"") inString = false;
			continue;
		}
		if (char === "\"") inString = true;
		else if (char === "{") depth += 1;
		else if (char === "}") {
			depth -= 1;
			if (depth === 0) return text.slice(start, index + 1);
		}
	}
	return null;
}
/** 任务计划。信息不够时 question 写追问，步骤只描述打算做什么，执行结果另外核验。 */

		]).optional(),
		network: zod.z.enum(["none", "bridge"]).optional(),
		workspace: zod.z.string().max(500).optional(),
		allowedTools: zod.z.array(zod.z.enum([
			"filesystem",

			"browser"
		])).min(1).max(4).optional(),
		limits: zod.z.object({ timeoutMs: zod.z.number().int().min(1).max(6e4).optional() }).optional(),
		outputs: zod.z.array(zod.z.object({

			expectedContent: zod.z.string().max(1e5).optional()
		})).max(100).optional(),
		args: zod.z.object({
			action: zod.z.enum([
				"organize",
