			]).optional(),
			path: zod.z.string().max(500).optional(),
			from: zod.z.string().max(500).optional(),
			to: zod.z.string().max(500).optional(),
			content: zod.z.string().max(1e5).optional(),
			executable: zod.z.string().max(260).optional(),
			argv: zod.z.array(zod.z.string().max(2e3)).max(40).optional(),
			url: zod.z.string().max(500).optional()
		}).optional(),
		checks: zod.z.array(zod.z.object({
			kind: zod.z.enum([
				"file-exists",
				"file-absent",
				"file-content",
				"file-hash",
				"output-includes",
				"exit-code"
			]),
			path: zod.z.string().max(500).optional(),
			value: zod.z.string().max(1e5).optional()
		})).max(40).optional()
	})).max(6),
	question: zod.z.string().max(200)
}).superRefine((plan, context) => {
	if (plan.workflow ? plan.tool !== "filesystem" || plan.steps.length !== 0 || Boolean(plan.command || plan.url) : plan.steps.length === 0) context.addIssue({
		code: "custom",
		message: "摘要流程必须使用 filesystem、空 steps 和专用 workflow；普通计划必须提供步骤"
	});
});
/** 一轮对话里提取出的记忆。UPDATE 和 DELETE 用 replaces 指出要替换的旧记忆原文。 */
var memoryExtractionSchema = zod.z.object({ memories: zod.z.array(zod.z.object({
	action: zod.z.enum([
		"ADD",
		"UPDATE",
		"DELETE"
	]),
	category: zod.z.enum([
		"user",
		"feedback",
		"project",
		"reference"
	]),
	text: zod.z.string().min(1).max(200),
	replaces: zod.z.string().max(200).optional()
})).max(5) });
function toolDeclarationFromSchema(name, description, schema) {
	return {
		name,
		description,
		parameters: zod.z.toJSONSchema(schema)
	};
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/model/gateway.ts
var adapters = [
	anthropicAdapter,
	ollamaAdapter,
	openAiCompatibleAdapter
];
/** 对话单独排队，任务和屏幕分析走另一条队列，避免长任务堵住聊天。 */
var chatQueue = new RequestQueue();
var workQueue = new RequestQueue();
function adapterFor(provider) {
	return adapters.find((adapter) => adapter !== openAiCompatibleAdapter && adapter.matches(provider)) ?? openAiCompatibleAdapter;
}
async function execute(model, messages, stream, onDelta, tools, timeoutMs = 12e4, lane = "work", control = {}) {
	if (!model.model.trim()) throw new Error("尚未填写模型名称");
	if (!model.endpoint.trim()) throw new Error("尚未填写服务地址");
	const adapter = adapterFor(model.provider);
	if (adapter.constraints.requiresApiKey && !model.apiKey) throw new Error("尚未配置 API Key");
	const request = adapter.buildChat(model, messages, stream, tools);
	const queue = lane === "chat" ? chatQueue : workQueue;
	const queuedAt = Date.now();
	return queue.enqueue(async () => {
		control.signal?.throwIfAborted();
		control.beforeSend?.();
		control.onDispatch?.(Date.now() - queuedAt);
		const controller = new AbortController();
		const cancel = () => controller.abort(control.signal?.reason);
		control.signal?.addEventListener("abort", cancel, { once: true });
		const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
		try {
			const response = await fetch(request.url, {
				method: "POST",
				headers: request.headers,
				body: request.body,
				redirect: "manual",
				signal: controller.signal
			});
			if (response.status >= 300 && response.status < 400) {
				await response.body?.cancel();
				throw new Error("模型服务返回重定向，未向新地址发送密钥或对话。请填写服务商提供的最终 HTTPS 接口地址");
			}
			if (!response.ok) throw classifyProviderFailure(response.status, await response.text(), [model.apiKey]);
			return (response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "").includes("application/json") ? extractText(await response.json()) : await readEventStream(response, onDelta);
		} catch (error) {
			if (control.signal?.aborted) throw new Error("请求已停止，没有继续发送内容", { cause: error });
			if (error instanceof Error && error.name === "AbortError") throw new Error(`模型请求超时（${Math.round(timeoutMs / 1e3)} 秒），请检查网络或服务地址`, { cause: error });
			throw new Error(redactProviderText(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "模型请求失败，请稍后重试", [model.apiKey]));
		} finally {
			clearTimeout(timeout);
			control.signal?.removeEventListener("abort", cancel);
		}
	});
}
function prepare(model, messages, system) {
	return withSystem(messages, system, adapterFor(model.provider).constraints.systemMessageStyle);
}
var ModelGateway = class {
	async chat(model, messages, onDelta, system, control = {}) {
		const text = await execute(model, prepare(model, messages, system), true, onDelta ?? (() => void 0), void 0, 12e4, "chat", control);
		if (!text.trim()) throw new Error("模型返回了空内容，请检查模型名称或服务兼容性");
		return text;
	}
	async chatWithImages(model, text, images, onDelta, system, control = {}) {
		const result = await execute(model, prepare(model, [{
			role: "user",
			content: text,
			images
		}], system), true, onDelta ?? (() => void 0), void 0, 12e4, "work", control);
		if (!result.trim()) throw new Error("模型返回了空内容，请检查模型名称或服务兼容性");
		return result;
	}
	async callTools(model, messages, tools, system, control = {}) {
		if (adapterFor(model.provider).constraints.toolCallingStyle === "none") throw new Error("当前模型协议不支持工具调用。请更换支持工具调用的模型。");
		const raw = await execute(model, prepare(model, messages, system), false, () => void 0, tools, 12e4, "work", control);
		try {
			return JSON.parse(raw);
		} catch (error) {
			throw new Error("模型没有返回可解析的工具参数", { cause: error });
		}
	}
	async callStructured(model, schema, toolName, description, messages, system, control = {}) {
		const tool = toolDeclarationFromSchema(toolName, description, schema);
		let lastError = "模型输出不符合约定结构";
		for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) try {
			control.signal?.throwIfAborted();
			const value = await this.callTools(model, messages, [tool], system, control);
			const parsed = schema.safeParse(value);
			if (parsed.success) return parsed.data;
			lastError = "模型输出不符合约定结构";
		} catch (error) {
			if (control.signal?.aborted) throw error;
			lastError = error instanceof Error ? error.message : lastError;
		}
		throw new Error(`${lastError}。已重试 2 次，未使用不完整结果。`);
	}
	async testConnection(model) {
		const startedAt = Date.now();
		const preview = await execute(model, [{
			role: "user",
			content: "请只回复 OK"
		}], false, () => void 0, void 0, 3e4);
		if (!preview.trim()) throw new Error("模型返回了空内容，请检查模型名称或服务兼容性");
		return {
			latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
			preview: preview.slice(0, 80)
		};
	}
};
//#endregion
//#region src/main/task/staging.ts
var tempRoot = (0, node_os.tmpdir)();
var base = (0, node_path.join)((0, node_fs.existsSync)(tempRoot) ? (0, node_fs.realpathSync)(tempRoot) : tempRoot, "murasame-task-stages");
var digest = (data) => (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(data).digest("hex");
function stageDir(id) {
	if (!/^ticket-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/.test(id)) throw new Error("隔离暂存标识无效");
	if ((0, node_fs.existsSync)(base) && (0, node_fs.realpathSync)(base).toLowerCase() !== (0, node_path.resolve)(base).toLowerCase()) throw new Error("暂存根目录被重解析，拒绝读取或清理");
	const dir = (0, node_fs.existsSync)(base) ? resolveInside(base, id) : (0, node_path.resolve)(base, id);
	if (!dir.startsWith((0, node_path.resolve)(base) + node_path.sep)) throw new Error("隔离暂存路径越界");
	return dir;
}
async function fileHash(root, path) {
	try {
		const file = resolveInside(root, path);
		if (!(await (0, node_fs_promises.stat)(file)).isFile()) throw new Error("产物不是文件");
		return digest(await (0, node_fs_promises.readFile)(file));
	} catch (error) {
		if (error.code === "ENOENT") return null;
		throw error;
	}
}
async function snapshot(root, signal) {
	const files = {};
	let bytes = 0;
	const walk = async (path) => {
		if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error("隔离暂存已取消");
		for (const entry of await (0, node_fs_promises.readdir)(resolveInside(root, path), { withFileTypes: true })) {
			if (entry.isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error("共享目录或暂存产物包含目录联接/符号链接，未跟随");
			const item = path ? `${path}/${entry.name}` : entry.name;
			const actual = resolveInside(root, item);
			if ((await (0, node_fs_promises.lstat)(actual)).isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error("重解析路径被拒绝");
			if (entry.isDirectory()) await walk(item);
			else if (entry.isFile()) {
				const size = (await (0, node_fs_promises.stat)(actual)).size;
				bytes += size;
				if (Object.keys(files).length >= 4e3 || bytes > 268435456 || size > 33554432) throw new Error("暂存超过本次任务的文件/大小预算，未执行命令");
				files[item] = digest(await (0, node_fs_promises.readFile)(actual));
			}
		}
	};
	await walk("");
	return files;
}
async function createStage(root, signal) {
	await (0, node_fs_promises.mkdir)(base, { recursive: true });
	if ((0, node_fs.realpathSync)(base).toLowerCase() !== (0, node_path.resolve)(base).toLowerCase()) throw new Error("暂存根目录不能是重解析路径");
	const dir = await (0, node_fs_promises.mkdtemp)((0, node_path.join)(base, "ticket-"));
	const stageId = (0, node_path.relative)(base, dir);
	const workspace = (0, node_path.join)(dir, "workspace");
	await (0, node_fs_promises.mkdir)(workspace);
	try {
		const copyDirectories = async (path) => {
			for (const entry of await (0, node_fs_promises.readdir)(resolveInside(root, path), { withFileTypes: true })) {
				if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error("暂存已取消");
				if (entry.isSymbolicLink()) throw new Error("暂存不跟随目录联接或符号链接");
				if (entry.isDirectory()) {
					const child = path ? `${path}/${entry.name}` : entry.name;
					resolveInside(root, child);
					await (0, node_fs_promises.mkdir)(resolveInside(workspace, child), { recursive: true });
					await copyDirectories(child);
				}
			}
		};
		await copyDirectories("");
		const baseline = await snapshot(root, signal);
		for (const [path, hash] of Object.entries(baseline)) {
			if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error("暂存已取消");
			const source = resolveInside(root, path);
			const bytes = await (0, node_fs_promises.readFile)(source);
			if (digest(bytes) !== hash) throw new Error(`原文件在暂存期间变化：${path}`);
			const target = resolveInside(workspace, path);
			await (0, node_fs_promises.mkdir)((0, node_path.dirname)(target), { recursive: true });
			await (0, node_fs_promises.writeFile)(target, bytes, { flag: "wx" });
		}
		const manifest = {
			root: (0, node_path.resolve)(root),
			baseline,
			stageId,
			changes: []
		};
		await saveStage(manifest);
		return manifest;
	} catch (error) {
		await removeStage(stageId);
		throw error;
	}
}
function stageWorkspace(stageId) {
	return (0, node_path.join)(stageDir(stageId), "workspace");
}
async function saveStage(manifest) {
	await (0, node_fs_promises.writeFile)((0, node_path.join)(stageDir(manifest.stageId), "manifest.json"), JSON.stringify(manifest), "utf8");
}
async function loadStage(stageId, root) {
	const value = JSON.parse(await (0, node_fs_promises.readFile)((0, node_path.join)(stageDir(stageId), "manifest.json"), "utf8"));
	if (value.stageId !== stageId || value.root.toLowerCase() !== (0, node_path.resolve)(root).toLowerCase()) throw new Error("暂存不属于本次批准目录");
	return value;
}
async function inspectStage(manifest, signal) {
	const current = await snapshot(stageWorkspace(manifest.stageId), signal);
	manifest.changes = [.../* @__PURE__ */ new Set([...Object.keys(manifest.baseline), ...Object.keys(current)])].filter((path) => manifest.baseline[path] !== current[path]).map((path) => ({
		path,
		kind: current[path] ? "write" : "delete",
		hash: current[path] ?? null,
		baselineHash: manifest.baseline[path] ?? null,
		exported: manifest.changes.find((change) => change.path === path && change.hash === (current[path] ?? null))?.exported ?? false
	}));
	await saveStage(manifest);
	return manifest.changes;
}
async function exportStage(manifest, outputs, gate) {
	const artifacts = [];
	const effects = [];
	let error;
	try {
		for (const output of outputs) {
			const change = manifest.changes.find((item) => item.path === output.path);
			if (!change || change.exported) continue;
			gate();
			const target = resolveInside(manifest.root, change.path);
			const actualBaseline = await fileHash(manifest.root, change.path);
			if (actualBaseline !== change.baselineHash || output.baselineHash !== void 0 && output.baselineHash !== actualBaseline) throw new Error(`原文件已变化，未覆盖：${change.path}`);
			if (actualBaseline !== null && !output.overwrite) continue;
			if (change.kind === "delete") {
				if (output.kind !== "delete") continue;
				gate();
				await (0, node_fs_promises.unlink)(resolveInside(manifest.root, change.path));
				effects.push({
					operation: "delete",
					source: target,
					destination: target,
					hash: change.baselineHash ?? "",
					status: "moved"
				});
			} else {
				const data = await (0, node_fs_promises.readFile)(resolveInside(stageWorkspace(manifest.stageId), change.path));
				const hash = digest(data);
				if (hash !== change.hash || output.expectedHash && output.expectedHash !== hash || output.expectedContent !== void 0 && data.toString("utf8") !== output.expectedContent) throw new Error(`暂存产物核验失败：${change.path}`);
				await (0, node_fs_promises.mkdir)((0, node_path.dirname)(target), { recursive: true });
				gate();
				resolveInside(manifest.root, change.path);
				if (actualBaseline === null) await (0, node_fs_promises.writeFile)(target, data, { flag: "wx" });
				else {
					const temporary = `${target}.murasame-${manifest.stageId}`;
					await (0, node_fs_promises.writeFile)(temporary, data, { flag: "wx" });
					try {
						gate();
						if (await fileHash(manifest.root, change.path) !== actualBaseline) throw new Error(`替换前原文件变化：${change.path}`);
						resolveInside(manifest.root, change.path);
						await (0, node_fs_promises.rename)(temporary, target);
					} finally {
						await (0, node_fs_promises.rm)(temporary, { force: true });
					}
				}
				artifacts.push(target);
				effects.push({
					source: "",
					destination: target,
					hash,
					status: "moved"
				});
				if (await fileHash(manifest.root, change.path) !== hash) throw new Error(`导出产物哈希核验失败：${change.path}`);
			}
			change.exported = true;
			await saveStage(manifest);
		}
	} catch (caught) {
		error = caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : String(caught);
	}
	return {
		artifacts,
		effects,
		error,
		pending: manifest.changes.filter((change) => !change.exported)
	};
}
async function removeStage(stageId) {
	const target = stageDir(stageId);
	if (!target.startsWith((0, node_path.resolve)(base) + node_path.sep)) throw new Error("暂存清理越界");
	await (0, node_fs_promises.rm)(target, {
		recursive: true,
		force: true
	});
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/isolation/task-runner.ts
/** Arbitrary code gets a private snapshot, never a writable host root. Only
* explicitly approved output paths can pass the separate export boundary. */
function createTaskRunner(run = runCommandAsync) {
	return async (request) => {
		if (request.signal?.aborted) return {
			status: "aborted",
			raw: "已取消，未启动隔离任务",
			outcomeKnown: true
		};
		const controller = new AbortController();
		const abort = () => controller.abort(request.signal?.reason);
		request.signal?.addEventListener("abort", abort, { once: true });
		const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(/* @__PURE__ */ new Error("任务执行超时")), request.timeoutMs);
		const gate = (boundary = "execute") => {
			if (controller.signal.aborted) throw new Error("任务已取消或超时");
			request.assertAllowed?.(boundary);
		};
		let containerId = "";
		let stage;
		let outcome = {
			status: "error",
			raw: "隔离任务没有执行",
			outcomeKnown: true
		};
		try {
			gate();
			const root = resolveInside(request.root, ".");
			if (!(0, node_fs.statSync)(root).isDirectory() || (0, node_fs.lstatSync)(root).isSymbolicLink() || (0, node_fs.realpathSync)(root).toLowerCase() !== (0, node_path.resolve)(root).toLowerCase()) throw new Error("授权根目录必须为真实目录，不能经过目录联接");
			if (request.resumeStageId) {
				stage = await loadStage(request.resumeStageId, root);
				await inspectStage(stage, controller.signal);
				const outputs = request.outputs ?? stage.changes.filter((change) => !change.exported).map((change) => ({
					path: change.path,
					kind: change.kind,
					overwrite: change.baselineHash !== null,
					baselineHash: change.baselineHash,
					expectedHash: change.hash ?? void 0
				}));
				const exported = await exportStage(stage, outputs, () => gate("export"));
				outcome = {
					status: exported.error || exported.pending.length ? "error" : "ok",
					exitCode: exported.error || exported.pending.length ? void 0 : 0,
					raw: exported.error ?? (exported.pending.length ? "仍有暂存变化待批准" : "已核验并导出批准的暂存产物；没有重放命令"),
					artifacts: exported.artifacts,
					effects: exported.effects,
					outcomeKnown: true,
					...exported.pending.length ? { awaitingExport: {
						stageId: stage.stageId,
						changes: exported.pending
					} } : {}
				};
			} else {
				if (!request.readOnly) stage = await createStage(root, controller.signal);
				gate();
				const mount = stage ? stageWorkspace(stage.stageId) : root;
				const sessionId = `task-${request.taskId ?? "standalone"}-${request.ticketId ?? (0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`;
				const started = await startContainerAsync({
					...containerPlan({
						hostPath: mount,
						readOnly: request.readOnly === true,
						network: request.network ?? "none"
					}),
					sessionId
				}, 0, run, {
					signal: controller.signal,
					readiness: "process"
				});
				if ("failed" in started) {
					containerId = started.id ?? "";
					throw new Error(started.failed);
				}
				containerId = started.id;
				gate();
				const marker = `MURASAME_EXIT_${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)().replaceAll("-", "")}`;
				const script = `cd /shared || exit 125; "$@"; result=$?; printf '\\n${marker}=%s\\n' "$result"; exit 0`;
				const text = await runInContainerAsync(containerId, [
					"sh",
					"-c",
					script,
					"task-command",
					request.executable,
					...request.args
				], run, {
					signal: controller.signal,
					timeoutMs: request.timeoutMs
				});
				const match = new RegExp(`\\n${marker}=(\\d+)\\s*$`).exec(text);
				if (!match) throw new Error("隔离命令缺少可信退出码；宿主文件未交给代码写入");
				const exitCode = Number(match[1]);
				const raw = text.slice(0, match.index).trim();
				outcome = {
					status: exitCode === 0 ? "ok" : "error",
					raw: raw || `exit=${exitCode}`,
					exitCode,
					outcomeKnown: true
				};
				if (stage) {
					if (!await stopContainerAsync(containerId, run)) {
						outcome.outcomeKnown = false;
						throw new Error(`隔离容器 ${containerId} 无法停止，未导出宿主文件`);
					}
					containerId = "";
					await inspectStage(stage, controller.signal);
					const exported = exitCode === 0 ? await exportStage(stage, request.outputs ?? [], () => gate("export")) : {
						artifacts: [],
						effects: [],
						pending: stage.changes
					};
					outcome = {
						...outcome,
						artifacts: exported.artifacts,
						effects: exported.effects,
						..."error" in exported && exported.error ? {
							status: "error",
							raw: `${outcome.raw}；${exported.error}`
						} : {},
						...exported.pending.length ? {
							status: "error",
							awaitingExport: {
								stageId: stage.stageId,
								changes: exported.pending
							},
							raw: `${outcome.raw}；变化保留于暂存 ${stage.stageId}，未声明的宿主变化尚未导出`
						} : {}
					};
				}
			}
		} catch (error) {
			outcome = {
				...outcome,
				status: controller.signal.aborted ? request.signal?.aborted ? "aborted" : "timeout" : "error",
				raw: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
				outcomeKnown: outcome.outcomeKnown !== false
			};
			if (stage) try {
				const changes = (await inspectStage(stage)).filter((change) => !change.exported);
				if (changes.length) outcome.awaitingExport = {
					stageId: stage.stageId,
					changes
				};
			} catch (failure) {
				outcome.raw += `；暂存 ${stage.stageId} 内容无法完整核验：${failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : String(failure)}`;
			}
		} finally {
			if (containerId && !await stopContainerAsync(containerId, run)) outcome = {
				...outcome,
				status: "error",
				outcomeKnown: false,
				raw: `${outcome.raw}；隔离容器 ${containerId} 清理失败，停止尚未核实`
			};
			if (stage && !outcome.awaitingExport && outcome.outcomeKnown && !/暂存 .*内容无法完整核验/.test(outcome.raw)) await removeStage(stage.stageId);
			clearTimeout(timer);
			request.signal?.removeEventListener("abort", abort);
		}
		if (controller.signal.aborted && outcome.status !== "error") outcome.status = request.signal?.aborted ? "aborted" : "timeout";
		return outcome;
	};
}
//#endregion
//#region src/shared/pet-speech.ts
function petSpeech(text) {
	const flat = text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
	const sentence = flat.split(/(?<=[。！？!?])/)[0] ?? flat;
	if (sentence.length <= 40) return sentence;
	return `${sentence.slice(0, 40)}…`;
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/agent/teammates.ts
/** 形象只表演这一步的类别，不拿成功动作去盖失败。 */
function poseIntent(pose) {
	if (pose === "fail") return "否定";
	if (pose === "wait") return "困惑";
	if (pose === "act") return "惊讶";
	return "思考";
}
var imageAsk = /画图|画画|画一张|画一幅|画个图|(?:生成|画|做)(?:一张|一幅|一个|个)?.{0,8}(?:图片|图像|照片|相片)/;
var organizeAsk = /(?:整理|归档).{0,16}(?:文件|桌面|下载|目录|文件夹|图片)|把.{0,24}(?:文件|文件夹).{0,12}整理/;
var screenAsk = /屏幕|当前画面|截图|看看我在干什么/;
var browseAsk = /https?:\/\/\S+|打开.{0,8}(?:网页|网站)|看视频|逛一下|隔离桌面/;
function routeUtterance(text) {
	const unquoted = text.replace(/[“「『"](?:[^”」』"]*)[”」』"]/g, "").replace(/‘[^’]*’|'[^']*'/g, "");
	const trimmed = unquoted.split(/[，。！？!?\n；;]|但是|不过/).map((clause) => clause.trim()).filter(Boolean).filter((clause) => !/(?:不要|别|不许|不用|无需|不想|不喜欢|禁止|不必|是什么意思|什么叫|原理|怎么|如何|解释|讨论|知道|记住|记下|网址是|我喜欢|我习惯|已经|昨天|上次|之前|曾经|你说)/.test(clause)).join("，");
	if (/不要放桌面|放到图片文件夹|放图片文件夹|放到图片/.test(unquoted) && !/是什么意思|原理|解释|昨天|你说/.test(unquoted)) return {
		teammate: "command",
		kind: "correct-draw",
		say: "放到桌面的做法我标成不用了。之后画完会问你要不要放到图片文件夹，仍然要你点允许。"
	};
	if (screenAsk.test(trimmed) && /截图|截屏|看.{0,12}(?:屏幕|当前画面|我在干什么)/.test(trimmed) && !imageAsk.test(trimmed)) return {
		teammate: "screen",
		kind: "look-screen",
		say: "我让屏幕去看当前窗口。看只是看，不会去点。"
	};
	if (imageAsk.test(trimmed)) return {
		teammate: "command",
		kind: "draw-image",
		say: "我在你的电脑上画。"
	};
	if (/(?:图片|图像|照片|相片).{0,8}在哪|在哪.{0,6}(?:图片|图像|照片|相片)|画好了吗|写好了吗|生成好了吗/.test(trimmed)) return {
		teammate: "command",
		kind: "draw-status",
		say: "我去看刚才那张图写到哪了。"
	};
	if (organizeAsk.test(trimmed)) return {
		teammate: "files",
		kind: "organize",
		say: "我交给文件队友。动你的文件之前会先问你。"
	};
	if (browseAsk.test(trimmed)) return {
		teammate: "isolation",
		kind: "browse",
		say: "我让隔离桌面去处理这个网页。没打开之前不会说已经看过。"
	};
	return {
		teammate: "chat",
		kind: "chat",
		say: ""
	};
}
/** 用任务单回答「图片在哪里」，不让闲聊模型另编一句。列表按最近更新在前。 */
function imageWhereabouts(tasks) {
	const related = tasks.find((task) => task.approval?.tool === "copy-image" || /拷到|写到|murasame-画/.test(`${task.summary}\n${task.result}`));
	if (!related) return {
		say: "还没有写过图。要写的话直接说画到桌面。",
		pose: "wait"
	};
	if (related.status === "等待批准" || related.status === "待确认") return {
		say: `还没写。点允许之后才会出现在 ${related.approval?.target?.trim() || "桌面"}。`,
		pose: "wait"
	};
	if (related.status === "已完成" || related.status === "部分完成") {
		const where = related.result.trim() || related.summary.trim();
		return {
			say: where ? `写过了。${where}` : "写过了，在桌面。",
			pose: "act"
		};
	}
	if (related.status === "失败") return {
		say: related.result.trim() || related.summary.trim() || "上次没写成。",
		pose: "fail"
	};
	if (related.status === "执行中") return {
		say: "还在写，写完我会说文件在哪。",
		pose: "wait"
	};
	return {
		say: related.result.trim() || "还在处理这张图。",
		pose: "wait"
	};
}
function petLine(name, situation) {
	const who = name?.trim() || "我";
	if (situation === "no-model") return `${who}在。需要模型才能接着聊，这次没有接上，所以没有生成回复。`;
	return `${who}没分清这件事该交给谁，所以没有开始做。`;
}
function chunk(type, data) {
	const length = Buffer.alloc(4);
	length.writeUInt32BE(data.length, 0);
	const body = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(type), data]);
	const checksum = Buffer.alloc(4);
	checksum.writeUInt32BE((0, node_zlib.crc32)(body) >>> 0, 0);
	return Buffer.concat([
		length,
		body,
		checksum
	]);
}
/** 一张实色小图。命令队友把它写进隔离环境，不是让对话模型声称画过。 */
function solidPng(width = 32, height = 32) {
	const ihdr = Buffer.alloc(13);
	ihdr.writeUInt32BE(width, 0);
	ihdr.writeUInt32BE(height, 4);
	ihdr[8] = 8;
	ihdr[9] = 2;
	const stride = width * 3 + 1;
	const raw = Buffer.alloc(stride * height);
	for (let y = 0; y < height; y += 1) {
		const row = y * stride;
		raw[row] = 0;
		for (let x = 0; x < width; x += 1) {
			const index = row + 1 + x * 3;
			raw[index] = (x * 12 + 40) % 256;
			raw[index + 1] = (y * 10 + 80) % 256;
			raw[index + 2] = 168;
		}
	}
	return Buffer.concat([
		Buffer.from([
			137,
			80,
			78,
			71,
			13,
			10,
			26,
			10
		]),
		chunk("IHDR", ihdr),
		chunk("IDAT", (0, node_zlib.deflateSync)(raw)),
		chunk("IEND", Buffer.alloc(0))
	]);
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/isolation/image-workflow.ts
/** Generates a clearly labeled deterministic sample inside isolation, then
* exports only to an already approved exact filename. It is not image-model art. */
async function executeImageCopy(input, run = runCommandAsync) {
	const controller = new AbortController();
	const abort = () => controller.abort(input.signal?.reason);
	input.signal?.addEventListener("abort", abort, { once: true });
	if (input.signal?.aborted) abort();
	const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3e4);
	const gate = () => {
		if (controller.signal.aborted) throw new Error("图片操作已取消或超时");
		input.assertAllowed();
	};
	let id = "";
	let created = false;
	let bytes = 0;
	let hash;
	const target = (0, node_path.resolve)(input.hostPath);
	let result = {
		ok: false,
		reason: "没有执行图片操作"
	};
	try {
		gate();
		const parent = (0, node_path.dirname)(target);
		if (!(0, node_fs.statSync)(parent).isDirectory() || (0, node_fs.realpathSync)(parent).toLowerCase() !== (0, node_path.resolve)(parent).toLowerCase()) throw new Error("图片目标父目录必须为真实目录，不能经过目录联接");
		if ((0, node_fs.existsSync)(target)) throw new Error("同名目标已经存在，没有覆盖");
		const sessionId = `image-${input.taskId ?? (0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`;
		const started = await startContainerAsync({
			...containerPlan(),
			network: "none",
			sessionId
		}, 0, run, {
			signal: controller.signal,
			readiness: "process"
		});
		if ("failed" in started) {
			id = started.id ?? "";
			throw new Error(started.failed);
		}
		id = started.id;
		const source = `/tmp/murasame-example-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}.png`;
		const png = solidPng();
		const expectedHash = (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(png).digest("hex");
		gate();
		await runInContainerAsync(id, [
			"python3",
			"-c",
			"import sys; open(sys.argv[1],\"xb\").write(bytes.fromhex(sys.argv[2]))",
			source,
			png.toString("hex")
		], run, {
			signal: controller.signal,
			timeoutMs: 8e3
		});
		gate();
		const read = await runInContainerAsync(id, [
			"python3",
			"-c",
			"import sys,json,hashlib,base64; data=open(sys.argv[1],\"rb\").read(2097153); print(json.dumps({\"bytes\":len(data),\"hash\":hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),\"base64\":base64.b64encode(data).decode()}))",
			source
		], run, {
			signal: controller.signal,
			timeoutMs: 8e3
		});
		const report = JSON.parse(read);
		if (!Number.isInteger(report.bytes) || report.bytes <= 0 || report.bytes > 2097152 || typeof report.base64 !== "string" || report.base64.length > 28e5) throw new Error("隔离图片大小无效");
		const copied = Buffer.from(report.base64, "base64");
		const sourceHash = (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(copied).digest("hex");
		if (copied.length !== report.bytes || report.hash !== sourceHash || sourceHash !== expectedHash || !copied.subarray(0, 8).equals(Buffer.from([
			137,
			80,
			78,
			71,
			13,
			10,
			26,
			10
		]))) throw new Error("隔离源图片内容或哈希核验失败");
		gate();
		if ((0, node_fs.realpathSync)(parent).toLowerCase() !== (0, node_path.resolve)(parent).toLowerCase()) throw new Error("批准的目标目录发生变化");
		const file = await (0, node_fs_promises.open)(target, "wx");
		created = true;
		try {
			gate();
			await file.writeFile(copied);
			await file.sync();
		} finally {
			await file.close();
		}
		bytes = (0, node_fs.statSync)(target).size;
		const actual = await (0, node_fs_promises.readFile)(target);
		hash = (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(actual).digest("hex");
		if (bytes !== report.bytes || hash !== sourceHash) throw new Error("导出后图片大小或哈希核验失败");
		result = {
			ok: true,
			procedureId: "isolated-example-image-v1",
			path: target,
			bytes,
			hash,
			sourceHash,
			note: `${controller.signal.aborted ? "取消前已导出" : "已导出"}确定性的示例图片；未调用绘图模型。`
		};
	} catch (error) {
		if (created) try {
			const actual = await (0, node_fs_promises.readFile)(target);
			bytes = actual.length;
			hash = (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(actual).digest("hex");
		} catch {}
		result = {
			ok: false,
			outcomeKnown: !created || hash !== void 0,
			reason: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
			...created ? {
				path: target,
				bytes,
				hash
			} : {}
		};
	} finally {
		if (id && !await stopContainerAsync(id, run)) result = {
			ok: false,
			outcomeKnown: false,
			reason: `${result.ok ? result.note : result.reason}；隔离容器 ${id} 清理失败，停止尚未核实`,
			...created ? {
				path: target,
				bytes,
				hash
			} : {}
		};
		clearTimeout(timer);
		input.signal?.removeEventListener("abort", abort);
	}
	return result;
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/local/model-env.ts
var configured$1 = false;
var bundledRoot = "";
/**
* 安装包自带的语音模型目录。主进程启动时写一次；开发态为空。
*/
function setBundledModelsRoot(dir) {
	bundledRoot = dir;
	configured$1 = false;
}
/**
* 用户自己下载的模型放在 userData 下，优先用这一份；
* 没有时才退回安装包自带的那份（装在 Program Files 里，只读就够了）。
*/
function modelsRoot(userDataDir) {
	const local = (0, node_path.join)(userDataDir, "models");
	if ((0, node_fs.existsSync)(local)) return local;
	if (bundledRoot && (0, node_fs.existsSync)(bundledRoot)) return bundledRoot;
	return local;
}
function missingModelFiles(directory, files) {
	return files.filter((file) => {
		try {
			const stat = (0, node_fs.statSync)((0, node_path.join)(directory, file));
			return !stat.isFile() || stat.size === 0;
		} catch {
			return true;
		}
	});
}
/** Select one complete model, never mix a partial override with bundled weights. */
function localModelDirectory(userDataDir, modelId, files) {
	const local = (0, node_path.resolve)(userDataDir, "models", modelId);
	if (!missingModelFiles(local, files).length) return local;
	const bundled = bundledRoot ? (0, node_path.resolve)(bundledRoot, modelId) : null;
	if (bundled && !missingModelFiles(bundled, files).length) return bundled;
	return (0, node_fs.existsSync)(local) || !bundled ? local : bundled;
}
/**
* transformers.js 只认一个全局本地目录，且必须禁止远程拉取，
* 否则模型缺失时会悄悄连外网下载。
*/
async function configureLocalModels(userDataDir) {
	if (configured$1) return;
	const { env } = await import("@huggingface/transformers");
	env.allowRemoteModels = false;
	env.localModelPath = modelsRoot(userDataDir);
	configured$1 = true;
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/local/stt.ts
var WHISPER_DIR = "whisper";
var WHISPER_FILES = [
	"config.json",
	"generation_config.json",
	"preprocessor_config.json",
	"tokenizer.json",
	"tokenizer_config.json",
	(0, node_path.join)("onnx", "encoder_model_quantized.onnx"),
	(0, node_path.join)("onnx", "decoder_model_merged_quantized.onnx")
];
function missingWhisperFiles(userDataDir) {
	const base = (0, node_path.join)(modelsRoot(userDataDir), WHISPER_DIR);
	return WHISPER_FILES.filter((file) => !(0, node_fs.existsSync)((0, node_path.join)(base, file)));
}
var recognizer = null;
async function load$2(userDataDir) {
	if (recognizer) return recognizer;
	await configureLocalModels(userDataDir);
	const { pipeline } = await import("@huggingface/transformers");
	recognizer = await pipeline("automatic-speech-recognition", WHISPER_DIR, {
		dtype: {
			encoder_model: "q8",
			decoder_model_merged: "q8"
		},
		device: "cpu"
	});
	return recognizer;
}
/** 16 kHz 单声道 Float32 PCM，由渲染进程录制后传入。 */
async function transcribeAudio(userDataDir, audio) {
	const missing = missingWhisperFiles(userDataDir);
	if (missing.length > 0) return {
		text: "",
		accepted: false,
		message: `本地语音识别模型缺少 ${missing.length} 个文件，没有进行识别。`
	};
	if (audio.length < 8e3) return {
		text: "",
		accepted: false,
		message: "录音太短，没有把空结果当成你说过的话。"
	};
	try {
		const text = ((await (await load$2(userDataDir))(audio, {
			language: "chinese",
			task: "transcribe"
		})).text ?? "").trim();
		if (!text) return {
			text: "",
			accepted: false,
			message: "这次没有听清，没有生成内容。"
		};
		return {
			text,
			accepted: true,
			message: "本地识别完成"
		};
	} catch (error) {
		return {
			text: "",
			accepted: false,
			message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "本地识别失败"
		};
	}
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/local/screen-policy.ts
var SENSITIVE_PROCESSES = [
	"keepass",
	"1password",
	"bitwarden",
	"lastpass",
	"dashlane",
	"密码"
];
function isSensitiveWindow(window) {
	if (window.displayAffinity === 17) return {
		sensitive: true,
		reason: "该窗口声明不参与系统截图，已跳过采集"
	};
	if ("sensitiveScanComplete" in window && window.sensitiveScanComplete === false) return {
		sensitive: true,
		reason: "无法完整检查窗口中的敏感控件，已跳过采集"
	};
	if (window.passwordField) return {
		sensitive: true,
		reason: "前台正在输入密码，已跳过采集"
	};
	const name = `${window.processName} ${window.title}`.toLowerCase();
	if (SENSITIVE_PROCESSES.some((item) => name.includes(item))) return {
		sensitive: true,
		reason: "敏感应用不采集，请改用文字描述"
	};
	return {
		sensitive: false,
		reason: ""
	};
}
function captureAllowed(input) {
	if (!input.authorized || input.revoked) return {
		capture: false,
		reason: "没有屏幕读取授权，不会采集"
	};
	const sensitive = isSensitiveWindow(input.window);
	if (sensitive.sensitive) return {
		capture: false,
		reason: sensitive.reason
	};
	return {
		capture: true,
		reason: ""
	};
}
function watchDecision(input) {
	if (!input.enabled) return {
		run: false,
		status: "off",
		reason: "持续观察默认关闭"
	};
	if (![
		5,
		15,
		60
	].includes(input.intervalSeconds)) return {
		run: false,
		status: "paused",
		reason: "采样间隔只能是 5、15 或 60 秒"
	};
	if (input.failures >= 3) return {
		run: false,
		status: "fused",
		reason: "连续 3 次采集失败，持续观察已停止"
	};
	if (!input.authorized) return {
		run: false,
		status: "paused",
		reason: "授权已撤销，采集已停止"
	};
	if (input.hidden) return {
		run: false,
		status: "paused",
		reason: "主窗口已隐藏，持续观察已暂停"
	};
	if (input.doNotDisturb) return {
		run: false,
		status: "paused",
		reason: "勿扰模式已开启，持续观察已暂停"
	};
	if (input.locked) return {
		run: false,
		status: "paused",
		reason: "系统已锁屏，持续观察已暂停"
	};
	return {
		run: true,
		status: "running",
		reason: `每 ${input.intervalSeconds} 秒采样一次`
	};
}
function operationIntent(text) {
	return /修复|修改|删除|移动|运行|点击|保存文件|创建任务/.test(text);
}
function screenInstructionIsExecutable(_source) {
	return false;
}

var powershell$1 = (0, node_path.join)(process.env.SystemRoot ?? "C:\\Windows", "System32", "WindowsPowerShell", "v1.0", "powershell.exe");

$title = New-Object System.Text.StringBuilder 512
[void][Fg]::GetWindowText($handle, $title, $title.Capacity)
$pidValue = [uint32]0
[void][Fg]::GetWindowThreadProcessId($handle, [ref]$pidValue)
$affinity = [uint32]0

$password = $false

  $rect = $element.Current.BoundingRectangle
  if (-not $rect.IsEmpty) { $bounds = @{ x = [int]$rect.X; y = [int]$rect.Y; width = [int]$rect.Width; height = [int]$rect.Height } }
  $monitorBounds = [Fg]::MonitorBounds($handle)

  $walker = [System.Windows.Automation.TreeWalker]::RawViewWalker

  $queue.Enqueue(@{ node = $element; depth = 0 })
  $count = 0
  $incomplete = $false
  while ($queue.Count -gt 0 -and $count -lt 600) {
    $entry = $queue.Dequeue()
    $node = $entry.node
    try {
      if ($node.Current.IsPassword -and -not $node.Current.IsOffscreen) { $password = $true }
      $name = $node.Current.Name
      if ($name -and -not $node.Current.IsPassword) { $tree += ($node.Current.ControlType.ProgrammaticName + ' ' + $name) }
      $child = $walker.GetFirstChild($node)
      if ($entry.depth -ge 30 -and $child) { $incomplete = $true }
      while ($child -and $entry.depth -lt 30) {
        if ($queue.Count -ge 600) { $incomplete = $true; break }
        $queue.Enqueue(@{ node = $child; depth = $entry.depth + 1 })
        $child = $walker.GetNextSibling($child)
      }
    } catch { $incomplete = $true }
    $count++
  }
  if ($queue.Count -gt 0) { $incomplete = $true }
  $selected = ''
  try {
    $pattern = $null
    if ($element.TryGetCurrentPattern([System.Windows.Automation.TextPattern]::Pattern, [ref]$pattern)) {
      $selected = (($pattern.GetSelection() | ForEach-Object { $_.GetText(4000) }) -join ' ')
    }
  } catch { }

}
[pscustomobject]@{
  windowId = $handle.ToInt64().ToString()
  processId = [int]$pidValue
  title = $title.ToString()
  idleMs = [Fg]::IdleMilliseconds()
  fullscreen = $fullscreen
  processName = $process.ProcessName
  passwordField = $password
  displayAffinity = [int]$affinity
  tree = ($tree -join " | ")
  bounds = $bounds
  selectedText = $selected
  sensitiveScanComplete = (-not $incomplete)
} | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
`;
function readForeground(run = defaultRun) {
	const raw = run(powershell$1, [
		"-NoProfile",
		"-NonInteractive",
		"-Command",
		script$2
	]);
	const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
	return {
		windowId: typeof parsed.windowId === "string" ? parsed.windowId : void 0,
		processId: typeof parsed.processId === "number" ? parsed.processId : void 0,
		title: parsed.title ?? "",
		idleMs: typeof parsed.idleMs === "number" && parsed.idleMs >= 0 ? parsed.idleMs : void 0,
		fullscreen: typeof parsed.fullscreen === "boolean" ? parsed.fullscreen : void 0,
		processName: parsed.processName ?? "",
		passwordField: Boolean(parsed.passwordField),
		displayAffinity: Number(parsed.displayAffinity ?? 0),
		tree: parsed.tree ?? "",
		selectedText: parsed.selectedText ?? "",
		sensitiveScanComplete: parsed.sensitiveScanComplete === true,
		bounds: parsed.bounds && Number.isFinite(parsed.bounds.width) ? parsed.bounds : void 0
	};
}
function defaultRun(command, args) {
	return (0, node_child_process.execFileSync)(command, args, {
		encoding: "utf8",
		windowsHide: true,
		timeout: 8e3
	});
}
async function readForegroundAsync(run = runCommandAsync, signal) {
	const raw = await run(powershell$1, [
		"-NoProfile",
		"-NonInteractive",
		"-Command",
		script$2
	], {
		timeoutMs: 8e3,
		signal
	});
	return readForeground(() => raw);
}

var powershell = (0, node_path.join)(process.env.SystemRoot ?? "C:\\Windows", "System32", "WindowsPowerShell", "v1.0", "powershell.exe");
var script$1 = `
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Runtime.WindowsRuntime
$null = [Windows.Storage.StorageFile,Windows.Storage,ContentType=WindowsRuntime]
$null = [Windows.Media.Ocr.OcrEngine,Windows.Media.Ocr,ContentType=WindowsRuntime]
$null = [Windows.Graphics.Imaging.BitmapDecoder,Windows.Graphics.Imaging,ContentType=WindowsRuntime]
$asTask = ([System.WindowsRuntimeSystemExtensions].GetMethods() | Where-Object { $_.Name -eq 'AsTask' -and $_.GetParameters().Count -eq 1 -and $_.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType.Name -eq 'IAsyncOperation\`1' })[0]
function Await($op, $type) {
  $task = $asTask.MakeGenericMethod($type).Invoke($null, @($op))
  $task.Wait(-1) | Out-Null
  $task.Result
}
$path = $env:MURASAME_OCR_IMAGE
$file = Await ([Windows.Storage.StorageFile]::GetFileFromPathAsync($path)) ([Windows.Storage.StorageFile])
$stream = Await ($file.OpenAsync([Windows.Storage.FileAccessMode]::Read)) ([Windows.Storage.Streams.IRandomAccessStream])
$decoder = Await ([Windows.Graphics.Imaging.BitmapDecoder]::CreateAsync($stream)) ([Windows.Graphics.Imaging.BitmapDecoder])
$bitmap = Await ($decoder.GetSoftwareBitmapAsync()) ([Windows.Graphics.Imaging.SoftwareBitmap])
$engine = [Windows.Media.Ocr.OcrEngine]::TryCreateFromUserProfileLanguages()
$result = Await ($engine.RecognizeAsync($bitmap)) ([Windows.Media.Ocr.OcrResult])
$result.Text
`;

var address = {
	初识: {
		title: "你",
		tone: "把话说完整",
		joke: "不开玩笑"
	},
	熟悉: {
		title: "你",
		tone: "可以短一些",
		joke: "只在你先开玩笑时接一句"
	},
	熟络: {
		title: "称呼可以更自然",
		tone: "语气可以松一点",
		joke: "可以开无伤大雅的玩笑"
	},
	默契: {
		title: "沿用你喜欢的称呼",
		tone: "像熟人一样说",
		joke: "玩笑仍不能改变事实"
	}
};
function blankRelationship(now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()) {
	return {
		stage: "初识",
		since: now,
		days: 0,
		turns: 0,
		disclosed: 0,
		history: [{
			stage: "初识",
			since: now
		}],
		lineId: "line-1"
	};
}
function advanceRelationship(record, now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()) {
	const score = record.days + Math.floor(record.turns / 20) + record.disclosed;
	const next = score >= 40 ? "默契" : score >= 20 ? "熟络" : score >= 8 ? "熟悉" : "初识";
	if (next === record.stage) return record;
	return {
		...record,
		stage: next,
		since: now,
		history: [...record.history, {
			stage: next,
			since: now
		}]
	};
}
function resetRelationship(record, now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()) {
	return {
		...blankRelationship(now),
		lineId: record.lineId,
		history: [...record.history, {
			stage: "初识",
			since: now
		}],
		archivedLines: record.archivedLines
	};
}
function startNewLine(record, now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()) {
	const { archivedLines = [], ...previous } = record;
	return {
		...blankRelationship(now),
		lineId: `line-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`,
		archivedLines: [...archivedLines, structuredClone(previous)]
	};
}
function relationshipVoice(stage) {
	return address[stage];
}

var cached = null;
/** ? keys.ts??????? userData ???????? app ????? */
function store() {
	if (!cached) cached = new electron_store.default({
		name: "murasame",
		defaults: {
			settings: initialSettings,
			pet: {
				x: null,
				y: null
			},
			windowBounds: null,
			relationship: null,
			toolEnabled: {}
		}
	});
	return cached;
}
var listeners = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
function onSettingsChanged(listener) {
	listeners.add(listener);
	return () => listeners.delete(listener);
}
var mergeSettings = (value) => ({
	...initialSettings,
	...value ?? {},
	permissions: {
		...initialSettings.permissions,
		...value?.permissions ?? {}
	}
});
function getSettings() {
	return mergeSettings(store().get("settings"));
}
function updateSettings(patch) {
	const next = mergeSettings({
		...getSettings(),
		...patch,
		permissions: {
			...getSettings().permissions,
			...patch.permissions ?? {}
		}
	});
	store().set("settings", next);
	listeners.forEach((listener) => listener(next));
	return next;
}
function readToolEnabled() {
	return store().get("toolEnabled") ?? {};
}
function writeToolEnabled(id, enabled) {
	store().set("toolEnabled", {
		...readToolEnabled(),
		[id]: enabled
	});
}
function readRelationship() {
	return store().get("relationship") ?? blankRelationship();
}
function writeRelationship(record) {
	store().set("relationship", record);
	return record;
}
function bumpRelationship() {
	const current = readRelationship();
	const elapsed = Date.now() - Date.parse(current.since);
	const days = Number.isFinite(elapsed) ? Math.max(current.days, Math.floor(elapsed / 864e5)) : current.days;
	return writeRelationship(advanceRelationship({
		...current,
		turns: current.turns + 1,
		days
	}));
}
function savePetPosition(x, y) {
	store().set("pet", {
		x,
		y
	});
}
function getSavedPetPosition() {
	const position = store().get("pet");
	return typeof position?.x === "number" && typeof position?.y === "number" ? {
		x: position.x,
		y: position.y
	} : null;
}
function saveWindowBounds(bounds) {
	store().set("windowBounds", bounds);
}
function getWindowBounds() {
	return store().get("windowBounds");
}

//#region src/shared/pet-geometry.ts
function petDimensions(scale, chatOpen) {
	const safeScale = Math.min(1.4, Math.max(.6, scale));
	const characterWidth = 326 * safeScale;
	const characterHeight = 440 * safeScale;
	const columnWidth = Math.max(360, Math.ceil(characterWidth) + 34);
	return {
		characterWidth,
		characterHeight,
		columnWidth,
		height: Math.max(500, Math.ceil(characterHeight) + 60),
		width: columnWidth + (chatOpen ? 300 : 0)
	};
}
function resizePetAroundFeet(bounds, oldColumnWidth, columnWidth, height, oldLeft = 0, left = 0) {
	return {
		x: bounds.x + oldLeft + oldColumnWidth / 2 - columnWidth / 2 - left,
		y: bounds.y + bounds.height - height
	};
}

//#region src/main/security/hardening.ts
var CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY = [
	"default-src 'self'",
	"script-src 'self'",
	"style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
	"img-src 'self' data: blob:",
	"connect-src 'self' https: http://127.0.0.1:* http://localhost:*",
	"frame-src http://127.0.0.1:* http://localhost:*"
].join("; ");
/**
* 开发服务器会注入内联的 React 预置脚本，严格的 script-src 会把它挡掉导致白屏。
* 只在开发模式放行内联脚本与开发服务器来源，打包版一律使用严格策略。
*/
function contentSecurityPolicy(isDevelopment) {
	if (!isDevelopment) return CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY;
	return [
		"default-src 'self'",
		"script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' http://localhost:* http://127.0.0.1:*",
		"style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
		"img-src 'self' data: blob:",
		"connect-src 'self' https: ws://localhost:* ws://127.0.0.1:* http://127.0.0.1:* http://localhost:*",
		"frame-src http://127.0.0.1:* http://localhost:*"
	].join("; ");
}
function navigationDecision(currentUrl, targetUrl) {
	if (targetUrl === currentUrl || targetUrl.startsWith("devtools://") || targetUrl.startsWith("murasame://")) return "allow";
	if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(targetUrl)) return "external";
	return "block";
}
function markUntrusted(text, source) {
	return `[不可信:${source}] ${text}`;
}
function hardenWebContents(contents, openExternal) {
	contents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {
		if (navigationDecision(contents.getURL(), url) === "external") openExternal(url);
		return { action: "deny" };
	});
	contents.on("will-navigate", (event, url) => {
		const decision = navigationDecision(contents.getURL(), url);
		if (decision === "allow") return;
		event.preventDefault();
		if (decision === "external") openExternal(url);
	});
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/desktop/placement.ts
function displayContaining(box, displays) {
	const cx = box.x + box.width / 2;
	const cy = box.y + box.height / 2;
	return displays.find((display) => cx >= display.x && cx < display.x + display.width && cy >= display.y && cy < display.y + display.height) ?? null;
}
function clampToDisplay(box, display) {
	const x = Math.min(Math.max(box.x, display.x), display.x + Math.max(0, display.width - box.width));
	const y = Math.min(Math.max(box.y, display.y), display.y + Math.max(0, display.height - box.height));
	return {
		...box,
		x: Math.round(x),
		y: Math.round(y)
	};
}
function recoverPosition(box, displays) {
	if (displays.length === 0) return box;
	const current = displayContaining(box, displays);
	const overlap = (display) => Math.max(0, Math.min(box.x + box.width, display.x + display.width) - Math.max(box.x, display.x)) * Math.max(0, Math.min(box.y + box.height, display.y + display.height) - Math.max(box.y, display.y));
	const distance = (display) => {
		const x = Math.max(display.x - box.x - box.width, box.x - display.x - display.width, 0);
		const y = Math.max(display.y - box.y - box.height, box.y - display.y - display.height, 0);
		return x * x + y * y;
	};
	return clampToDisplay(box, current ?? [...displays].sort((a, b) => overlap(b) - overlap(a) || distance(a) - distance(b))[0]);
}
/** 对话框贴在角色旁边；靠屏幕边缘时换到空间更大的一侧。 */
function choosePetChatSide(input) {
	const spaceRight = input.areaX + input.areaWidth - (input.characterX + input.characterWidth);
	const spaceLeft = input.characterX - input.areaX;
	if (spaceRight >= input.chatWidth) return "right";
	if (spaceLeft >= input.chatWidth) return "left";
	return spaceRight >= spaceLeft ? "right" : "left";
}
function resetAnchor(box, displays) {
	const current = displayContaining(box, displays) ?? displays[0];
	if (!current) return box;
	return {
		...box,
		x: current.x + current.width - box.width - 24,
		y: current.y + current.height - box.height - 24
	};
}

var rendererUrl = process.env.ELECTRON_RENDERER_URL;
var preload = (0, node_path.join)(__dirname, "../preload/index.js");
function displayAreas() {
	return electron.screen.getAllDisplays().map((display) => ({
		id: String(display.id),
		x: display.workArea.x,
		y: display.workArea.y,
		width: display.workArea.width,
		height: display.workArea.height,
		scaleFactor: display.scaleFactor
	}));
}
function clampPetPosition(x, y, width, height) {
	const next = recoverPosition({
		x,
		y,
		width,
		height
	}, displayAreas());
	return {
		x: next.x,
		y: next.y
	};
}
function resourcePath$1(name) {
	return electron.app.isPackaged ? (0, node_path.join)(process.resourcesPath, "resources", name) : (0, node_path.join)(electron.app.getAppPath(), "resources", name);
}
function icon() {
	return electron.nativeImage.createFromPath(resourcePath$1("icon.png"));
}
function loadRenderer(window, page) {
	if (rendererUrl) window.loadURL(`${rendererUrl}/${page}.html`);
	else window.loadURL(`murasame://app/${page}.html`);
}
function secureWebContents(window) {
	const stopVoiceInput = () => {
		window.webContents.send(IPC.window.hidden);
	};
	window.on("hide", stopVoiceInput);
	window.on("minimize", stopVoiceInput);
	if (!electron.app.isPackaged) {
		window.webContents.on("console-message", (details) => {
			logger_default.info(`[renderer:${details.level}] ${details.message} (${details.sourceId}:${details.lineNumber})`);
		});
		window.webContents.on("did-fail-load", (_event, code, description, url, isMainFrame) => {
			if (isMainFrame) logger_default.warn(`Renderer failed to load ${url}: ${code} ${description}`);
		});
	}
	hardenWebContents(window.webContents, electron.shell.openExternal.bind(electron.shell));
	window.webContents.on("render-process-gone", (_event, details) => {
		logger_default.error("Renderer process gone", details.reason);
	});
}
function broadcastPetState(state) {
	[mainWindow, petWindow].filter((window) => Boolean(window && !window.isDestroyed())).forEach((window) => window.webContents.send(IPC.pet.stateChanged, state));
	return state;
}
function createMainWindow() {
	if (mainWindow && !mainWindow.isDestroyed()) return mainWindow;
	const stored = getWindowBounds();
	const width = stored?.width ?? 1400;
	const height = stored?.height ?? 880;
	const placed = typeof stored?.x === "number" && typeof stored?.y === "number" ? recoverPosition({
		x: stored.x ?? 0,
		y: stored.y ?? 0,
		width,
		height
	}, displayAreas()) : null;
	mainWindow = new electron.BrowserWindow({
		width,
		height,
		x: placed?.x,
		y: placed?.y,
		minWidth: 1180,
		minHeight: 740,
		show: false,
		backgroundColor: "#F4F1EB",
		title: "Murasame · AI 桌面伙伴",
		icon: icon(),
		autoHideMenuBar: true,
		webPreferences: {
			preload,
			contextIsolation: true,
			sandbox: true,
			nodeIntegration: false,
			webSecurity: true,
			devTools: !electron.app.isPackaged,
			spellcheck: false
		}
	});
	secureWebContents(mainWindow);
	loadRenderer(mainWindow, "index");
	mainWindow.setMenuBarVisibility(false);
	mainWindow.once("ready-to-show", () => {
		if (!getSettings().onboardingDone) mainWindow?.show();
	});
	const rememberBounds = () => {
		if (!mainWindow || mainWindow.isDestroyed() || mainWindow.isMinimized()) return;
		if (saveBoundsTimer) clearTimeout(saveBoundsTimer);
		saveBoundsTimer = setTimeout(() => {
			if (!mainWindow || mainWindow.isDestroyed()) return;
			saveWindowBounds(mainWindow.getBounds());
		}, 250);
	};
	mainWindow.on("resize", rememberBounds);
	mainWindow.on("move", rememberBounds);
	mainWindow.on("minimize", () => {
		if (getSettings().minimizeToTray && !isQuitting()) hideMain();
	});
	mainWindow.on("close", (event) => {
		if (isQuitting()) return;
		const settings = getSettings();
		event.preventDefault();
		if (settings.closeBehavior === "quit") {
			electron.dialog.showMessageBox(mainWindow, {
				type: "question",
				title: "退出 Murasame？",
				message: "退出应用会结束本地控制台。",
				detail: "未完成任务不会被报告为成功。隐藏到托盘则不会取消任务。",
				buttons: [
					"退出应用",
					"隐藏到托盘",
					"取消"
				],
				defaultId: 1,
				cancelId: 2
			}).then(({ response }) => {
				if (response === 0) {
					markQuitting();
					quitApplication();
				} else if (response === 1) hideMain();
			});
			return;
		}
		if (settings.closeBehavior === "ask") {
			electron.dialog.showMessageBox(mainWindow, {
				type: "question",
				title: "关闭窗口",
				message: "要隐藏到托盘，还是退出应用？",
				buttons: [
					"隐藏到托盘",
					"退出应用",
					"取消"
				],
				defaultId: 0,
				cancelId: 2
			}).then(({ response }) => {
				if (response === 0) hideMain();
				else if (response === 1) {
					markQuitting();
					quitApplication();
				}
			});
			return;
		}
		hideMain();
	});
	mainWindow.on("closed", () => {
		mainWindow = null;
	});
	return mainWindow;
}
function createPetWindow() {
	if (petWindow && !petWindow.isDestroyed()) return petWindow;
	const saved = getSavedPetPosition();
	const { width, height } = petWindowSize();
	const cursor = electron.screen.getCursorScreenPoint();
	const anchor = resetAnchor({
		x: cursor.x,
		y: cursor.y,
		width,
		height
	}, displayAreas());
	const { x, y } = clampPetPosition(saved?.x ?? anchor.x, saved?.y ?? anchor.y, width, height);
	savePetPosition(x, y);
	petWindow = new electron.BrowserWindow({
		width,
		height,
		x,
		y,
		transparent: true,
		backgroundColor: "#00000000",
		frame: false,
		resizable: false,
		maximizable: false,
		minimizable: false,
		fullscreenable: false,
		skipTaskbar: true,
		alwaysOnTop: true,
		hasShadow: false,
		show: false,
		title: "Murasame Pet",
		webPreferences: {
			preload,
			contextIsolation: true,
			sandbox: true,
			nodeIntegration: false,
			webSecurity: true,
			devTools: !electron.app.isPackaged,
			spellcheck: false,
			backgroundThrottling: false
		}
	});
	petWindow.setAlwaysOnTop(true, "floating");
	petWindow.setVisibleOnAllWorkspaces(true, { visibleOnFullScreen: true });
	petWindow.setIgnoreMouseEvents(true, { forward: true });
	secureWebContents(petWindow);
	loadRenderer(petWindow, "pet");
	petWindow.once("ready-to-show", () => {
		if (getSettings().petVisible) {
			petWindow?.showInactive();
			broadcastPetState(getPetStateSafe());
		}
	});
	petWindow.on("close", (event) => {
		if (isQuitting()) return;
		event.preventDefault();
		petWindow?.hide();
		updateSettings({ petVisible: false });
	});
	petWindow.on("moved", () => {
		if (petDrag || !petWindow || petWindow.isDestroyed()) return;
		const [x, y] = petWindow.getPosition();
		if (savePetTimer) clearTimeout(savePetTimer);
		savePetTimer = setTimeout(() => {
			savePetPosition(characterOriginX(x), y);
			broadcastPetState(getPetStateSafe());
		}, 120);
	});
	petWindow.on("closed", () => {
		petWindow = null;
	});
	return petWindow;
}
function broadcastPetPerformance(cue) {
	const window = getPetWindow();
	if (window && !window.isDestroyed()) window.webContents.send(IPC.pet.performance, cue);
}
function isPetHidden() {
	const window = getPetWindow();
	return !window || !window.isVisible();
}
function initializeWindows() {
	const main = createMainWindow();
	const pet = createPetWindow();
	if (!getSettings().petVisible) pet.hide();
	const recoverWindows = () => {
		const pet = getPetWindow();
		if (pet) {
			const bounds = pet.getBounds();
			const size = petWindowSize();
			const next = recoverPosition({
				...bounds,
				width: size.width,
				height: size.height
			}, displayAreas());
			pet.setBounds({
				x: next.x,
				y: next.y,
				width: size.width,
				height: size.height
			});
			savePetPosition(characterOriginX(next.x), next.y);
			broadcastPetState(getPetStateSafe());
		}
		const consoleWindow = getMainWindow();
		if (consoleWindow) bringOntoScreen(consoleWindow);
	};
	electron.screen.on("display-removed", recoverWindows);
	electron.screen.on("display-added", recoverWindows);
	electron.screen.on("display-metrics-changed", recoverWindows);
	if (!getSettings().onboardingDone) main.show();
}
function getMainWindow() {
	return mainWindow && !mainWindow.isDestroyed() ? mainWindow : null;
}
function getPetWindow() {
	return petWindow && !petWindow.isDestroyed() ? petWindow : null;
}
var MAIN_PATHS = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
	"/tasks",
	"/chat",
	"/settings",
	"/autonomy"
]);
/** 把控制台唤到前面，并让它打开指定页面。可以带 ?focus=任务编号。 */
function openMainPath(path) {
	const pathOnly = path.split("?")[0];
	if (!MAIN_PATHS.has(pathOnly)) return;
	showMain();
	getMainWindow()?.webContents.send(IPC.window.navigated, path);
}
function bringOntoScreen(window) {
	const bounds = window.getBounds();
	const next = recoverPosition(bounds, displayAreas());
	if (next.x === bounds.x && next.y === bounds.y) return;
	window.setBounds({
		...bounds,
		x: next.x,
		y: next.y
	});
	saveWindowBounds({
		...bounds,
		x: next.x,
		y: next.y
	});
}
function showMain() {
	mainVisibilityRevision++;
	const window = getMainWindow() ?? createMainWindow();
	if (window.isMinimized()) window.restore();
	bringOntoScreen(window);
	window.show();
	window.focus();
}
function hideMain() {
	mainVisibilityRevision++;
	getMainWindow()?.hide();
}
function showPet() {
	const window = getPetWindow() ?? createPetWindow();
	updateSettings({ petVisible: true });
	window.showInactive();
	return broadcastPetState(getPetStateSafe());
}
function hidePet() {
	getPetWindow()?.hide();
	updateSettings({ petVisible: false });
	return broadcastPetState(getPetStateSafe());
}
function togglePet() {
	return getPetWindow()?.isVisible() ? hidePet() : showPet();
}
function getPetStateSafe() {
	const settings = getSettings();
	const window = getPetWindow();
	const [x, y] = window?.getPosition() ?? [0, 0];
	return {
		visible: Boolean(window?.isVisible()),
		scale: settings.petScale,
		x,
		y,
		preset: settings.petPreset,
		importedModelUrl: settings.importedModelUrl
	};
}
function refreshPetState() {
	return broadcastPetState(getPetStateSafe());
}
function setPetScale(scale) {
	const window = getPetWindow();
	const bounds = window?.getBounds();
	const oldColumn = bounds ? bounds.width - (petChatOpen ? PET_CHAT_WIDTH : 0) : 360;
	updateSettings({ petScale: scale });
	if (window && bounds) {
		const size = petDimensions(scale, petChatOpen);
		const left = petChatOpen && petChatSide === "left" ? PET_CHAT_WIDTH : 0;
		const anchor = resizePetAroundFeet(bounds, oldColumn, size.columnWidth, size.height, left, left);
		const position = clampPetPosition(anchor.x, anchor.y, size.width, size.height);
		window.setBounds({
			...position,
			width: size.width,
			height: size.height
		});
		savePetPosition(characterOriginX(position.x), position.y);
	}
	const [x, y] = window?.getPosition() ?? [0, 0];
	return broadcastPetState({
		visible: Boolean(window?.isVisible()),
		scale,
		x,
		y,
		preset: getSettings().petPreset,
		importedModelUrl: getSettings().importedModelUrl
	});
}
function setPetPosition(x, y) {
	const window = getPetWindow();
	const size = petWindowSize();
	const position = clampPetPosition(x, y, size.width, size.height);
	window?.setBounds({
		x: position.x,
		y: position.y,
		width: size.width,
		height: size.height
	});
	savePetPosition(characterOriginX(position.x), position.y);
	return broadcastPetState({
		visible: Boolean(window?.isVisible()),
		scale: getSettings().petScale,
		x: position.x,
		y: position.y,
		preset: getSettings().petPreset,
		importedModelUrl: getSettings().importedModelUrl
	});
}
function resetPetPosition() {
	const next = resetAnchor(getPetWindow()?.getBounds() ?? {
		x: 0,
		y: 0,
		width: 360,
		height: 500
	}, displayAreas());
	return setPetPosition(next.x, next.y);
}
function getPetBounds() {
	const window = getPetWindow();
	if (!window) return {
		x: 0,
		y: 0,
		width: 360,
		height: 500
	};
	return window.getBounds();
}
async function confirmQuit() {
	if ((await electron.dialog.showMessageBox({
		type: "question",
		title: "退出 Murasame？",
		message: "退出会关闭控制台和桌宠。",
		detail: "未完成的任务不会被记成成功。已经产生的文件会保留。",
		buttons: ["退出应用", "取消"],
		defaultId: 1,
		cancelId: 1
	})).response !== 0) return;
	markQuitting();
	quitApplication();
}
function setPetMousePassthrough(ignore) {
	getPetWindow()?.setIgnoreMouseEvents(ignore, { forward: true });
}
function characterOriginX(windowX) {
	return petChatOpen && petChatSide === "left" ? windowX + PET_CHAT_WIDTH : windowX;
}
function beginPetDrag(screenX, screenY) {
	const window = getPetWindow();
	if (!window) return;
	const [x, y] = window.getPosition();
	petDrag = {
		offsetX: screenX - x,
		offsetY: screenY - y,
		lastX: screenX,
		lastY: screenY
	};
}
/** 只用这次事件里的屏幕坐标。窗口自己移动产生的重复事件直接丢掉，避免按住就往下溜。 */
function movePetDrag(screenX, screenY) {
	const window = getPetWindow();
	if (!window || !petDrag) return;
	if (screenX === petDrag.lastX && screenY === petDrag.lastY) return;
	petDrag.lastX = screenX;
	petDrag.lastY = screenY;
	const size = petWindowSize();
	const next = clampPetPosition(screenX - petDrag.offsetX, screenY - petDrag.offsetY, size.width, size.height);
	if (!Number.isFinite(next.x) || !Number.isFinite(next.y)) return;
	const [currentX, currentY] = window.getPosition();
	if (next.x === currentX && next.y === currentY) return;
	window.setBounds({
		x: next.x,
		y: next.y,
		width: size.width,
		height: size.height
	});
}
function endPetDrag() {
	const window = getPetWindow();
	petDrag = null;
	if (!window) return;
	const [x, y] = window.getPosition();
	savePetPosition(characterOriginX(x), y);
}
function setPetChatOpen(open) {
	const window = getPetWindow();
	if (!window) {
		petChatOpen = open;
		return {
			open,
			side: petChatSide
		};
	}
	if (open && petChatOpen) return {
		open: true,
		side: petChatSide
	};
	const bounds = window.getBounds();
	const originX = characterOriginX(bounds.x);
	const { columnWidth: PET_WIDTH, height: PET_HEIGHT } = petDimensions(getSettings().petScale, false);
	if (!open) {
		petChatOpen = false;
		const position = clampPetPosition(originX, bounds.y, PET_WIDTH, PET_HEIGHT);
		window.setBounds({
			x: position.x,
			y: position.y,
			width: PET_WIDTH,
			height: PET_HEIGHT
		});
		savePetPosition(position.x, position.y);
		return {
			open: false,
			side: petChatSide
		};
	}
	const area = electron.screen.getDisplayMatching({
		x: originX,
		y: bounds.y,
		width: PET_WIDTH,
		height: PET_HEIGHT
	}).workArea;
	petChatSide = choosePetChatSide({
		characterX: originX,
		characterWidth: PET_WIDTH,
		chatWidth: PET_CHAT_WIDTH,
		areaX: area.x,
		areaWidth: area.width
	});
	petChatOpen = true;
	const position = clampPetPosition(petChatSide === "left" ? originX - PET_CHAT_WIDTH : originX, bounds.y, PET_WIDTH + PET_CHAT_WIDTH, PET_HEIGHT);
	window.setBounds({
		x: position.x,
		y: position.y,
		width: PET_WIDTH + PET_CHAT_WIDTH,
		height: PET_HEIGHT
	});
	savePetPosition(characterOriginX(position.x), position.y);
	return {
		open: true,
		side: petChatSide
	};
}
function focusPetChat() {
	const window = getPetWindow() ?? createPetWindow();
	showPet();
	window.show();
	window.focus();
	window.webContents.send(IPC.pet.focusChat);
}

//#region src/shared/feature-matrix.ts
var featureNeeds = [
	{
		id: "chat",
		label: "文字交流",
		requires: ["text.chat"]
	},
	{
		id: "screen",
		label: "屏幕辅助",
		requires: ["vision.image-input"]
	},
	{
		id: "tasks",
		label: "任务工具调用",
		requires: ["tools.function-calling"]
	},
	{
		id: "structured",
		label: "结构化输出",
		requires: ["output.json-schema"]
	}
];
function featureState(capabilities, featureId) {
	const feature = featureNeeds.find((item) => item.id === featureId);
	if (!feature) return {
		available: false,
		label: "未验证"
	};
	const records = capabilities ?? [];
	if (feature.requires.every((id) => records.find((item) => item.id === id)?.status === "verified")) return {
		available: true,
		label: "可用"
	};
	return {
		available: false,
		label: feature.requires.some((id) => records.find((item) => item.id === id)?.status === "unsupported") ? "当前模型不支持图像" : "未验证"
	};
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/core/screen-capture.ts
async function captureScreen(service, models, input) {
	const main = getMainWindow();
	const pet = getPetWindow();
	const visibleMain = Boolean(main?.isVisible());
	const visiblePet = Boolean(pet?.isVisible());
	const visibilityRevision = getMainVisibilityRevision();
	let selfHidden = false;
	let policyStopped = false;
	const watch = () => {
		const s = getSettings();
		return {
			enabled: s.screenWatchEnabled,
			intervalSeconds: s.screenWatchSeconds,
			hidden: selfHidden ? !visibleMain : !main?.isVisible(),
			doNotDisturb: s.doNotDisturb,
			locked: input.locked(),
			authorized: s.permissions.screen,
			failures: service.failures
		};
	};
	const guard = (send = false) => {
		if (input.signal.aborted) {
			policyStopped = true;
			input.signal.throwIfAborted();
		}
		const s = getSettings();
		if (!s.permissions.screen || input.locked()) {
			policyStopped = true;
			throw new Error("屏幕授权已撤销或系统已锁定，采集已停止");
		}
		if (getMainVisibilityRevision() !== visibilityRevision) {
			policyStopped = true;
			throw new Error("用户已改变窗口显示状态，采集已停止");
		}
		if (input.continuous) {
			const decision = watchDecision(watch());
			if (!decision.run) {
				policyStopped = true;
				throw new Error(decision.reason);
			}
		}
		if (send && !s.outboundImages) {
			policyStopped = true;
			throw new Error("图片外发授权已撤销，没有发送画面");
		}
	};
	let facts = {
		title: "",
		processName: "",
		passwordField: false,
		displayAffinity: 0,
		tree: "",
		selectedText: "",
		sensitiveScanComplete: false
	};
	let captureError = "";
	let image;
	let ocrText;
	let modelSummary = "";
	let generateMs = 0;
	let waitMs = 0;
	let resolution = "";
	const started = Date.now();
	try {
		guard();
		selfHidden = true;
		if (visibleMain) main?.hide();
		if (visiblePet) pet?.hide();
		await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 160));
		guard();
		facts = await readForegroundAsync(void 0, input.signal);
		guard();
		const decision = captureAllowed({
			authorized: true,
			revoked: false,
			window: facts
		});
		if (!decision.capture) throw new Error(decision.reason);
		if (!facts.bounds) throw new Error("无法确定目标窗口范围，没有改为采集整个屏幕");
		if (!facts.windowId || ![facts.bounds.width, facts.bounds.height].every((value) => Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 40 && value <= 16384)) throw new Error("无法确定目标窗口身份或尺寸，没有改为整屏采集");
		const sources = await electron.desktopCapturer.getSources({
			types: ["window"],
			thumbnailSize: {
				width: Math.round(facts.bounds.width),
				height: Math.round(facts.bounds.height)
			}
		});
		guard();
		const full = sources.find((source) => source.id.split(":")[0] === "window" && source.id.split(":")[1] === facts.windowId)?.thumbnail;
		if (!full || full.isEmpty()) throw new Error("未找到可采集的目标窗口，没有改为整屏采集");
		const current = await readForegroundAsync(void 0, input.signal);
		guard();
		if (!captureAllowed({
			authorized: true,
			revoked: false,
			window: current
		}).capture || current.windowId !== facts.windowId || current.processId !== facts.processId || current.title !== facts.title || current.processName !== facts.processName || JSON.stringify(current.bounds) !== JSON.stringify(facts.bounds)) throw new Error("采集过程中前台窗口发生变化，请重试");
		const png = full.toPNG();
		const size = full.getSize();
		resolution = `${size.width} × ${size.height}`;
		image = png.toString("base64");
		const shot = (0, node_path.join)((0, node_os.tmpdir)(), `murasame-ocr-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}.png`);
		try {
			await (0, node_fs_promises.writeFile)(shot, png, { flag: "wx" });
			ocrText = await recognizeImageAsync(shot, void 0, input.signal).catch(() => "");
			guard();
		} finally {
			await (0, node_fs_promises.rm)(shot, { force: true });
		}
	} catch (error) {
		captureError = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "无法采集目标窗口";
		image = "";
		ocrText = "";
	} finally {
		if (visibleMain && !input.locked() && getMainVisibilityRevision() === visibilityRevision && !main?.isDestroyed()) main?.showInactive();
		if (visiblePet && !input.locked() && getSettings().petVisible && !pet?.isDestroyed()) pet?.showInactive();
		selfHidden = false;
	}
	const captureMs = Date.now() - started;
	const vision = featureState(models.list().find((item) => item.default)?.capabilities, "screen");
	let visionError = !vision.available ? "当前模型不支持图像" : !getSettings().outboundImages ? "未授权将截图发给模型" : "";
	if (image && vision.available && getSettings().outboundImages) {
		const begin = Date.now();
		try {
			guard(true);
			modelSummary = await models.describeImage(image, `${facts.title}\n${ocrText}`.slice(0, 2e3), {
				signal: input.signal,
				beforeSend: () => guard(true),
				onDispatch: (elapsed) => {
					waitMs += elapsed;
				}
			});
			guard(true);
		} catch (error) {
			visionError = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "看图模型未返回结果";
			modelSummary = "";
		}
		generateMs = Math.max(0, Date.now() - begin - waitMs);
	}
	const live = getSettings();
	return service.analyze({
		authorized: live.permissions.screen,
		revoked: !live.permissions.screen || input.signal.aborted,
		window: facts,
		elements: [],
		ocrText,
		imageCaptured: Boolean(image),
		resolution,
		visionAvailable: Boolean(modelSummary),
		visionError,
		captureError,
		captureSkipped: policyStopped,
		watch: watch(),
		supplemented: false,
		timings: {
			captureMs,
			waitMs,
			generateMs
		},
		modelSummary
	});
}

//#region src/main/agent/system-folders.ts
/** Respect Windows Known Folder redirection, including a relocated Desktop. */
function taskSystemFolders() {
	return [
		["桌面", "desktop"],
		["下载", "downloads"],
		["文档", "documents"]
	].map(([label, name]) => {
		try {
			return `${label}：${electron.app.getPath(name)}`;
		} catch {
			return `${label}：暂时无法获取，请用户通过目录选择器确认`;
		}
	}).join("\n");
}

//#region src/main/agent/skills.ts
function fileOf(dir) {
	return (0, node_path.join)(dir, "skills.json");
}
function readSkills(dir) {
	try {
		const parsed = JSON.parse((0, node_fs.readFileSync)(fileOf(dir), "utf8"));
		return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
	} catch {
		return [];
	}
}
function writeAll(dir, skills) {
	(0, node_fs.mkdirSync)(dir, { recursive: true });
	(0, node_fs.writeFileSync)(fileOf(dir), JSON.stringify(skills, null, 2), "utf8");
}
function sameRoot(left, right) {
	return left === void 0 || right === void 0 ? left === right : (0, node_path.resolve)(left).toLowerCase() === (0, node_path.resolve)(right).toLowerCase();
}
function findSkill(dir, kind, scope) {
	return readSkills(dir).filter((item) => item.kind === kind && !item.superseded && item.verification?.status === "已完成" && (!scope || item.workspace === scope.workspace && sameRoot(item.root, scope.root))).at(-1) ?? null;
}
/** 旧做法标成被取代，不删掉，方便追溯。 */
function rememberSkill(dir, skill, verification) {
	if (!verification?.taskId || verification.status !== "已完成" || !verification.evidence.length || !verification.evidence.every((item) => item.matched)) throw new Error("技能必须来自已核验成功的任务");
	const next = readSkills(dir).map((item) => item.kind === skill.kind && item.workspace === skill.workspace && sameRoot(item.root, skill.root) && !item.superseded ? {
		...item,
		superseded: true
	} : item);
	const stored = {
		...skill,
		verification,
		superseded: false,
		lastFailure: skill.lastFailure ?? ""
	};
	next.push(stored);
	writeAll(dir, next);
	return stored;
}
function noteSkillFailure(dir, kind, reason, scope, taskId) {
	const skills = readSkills(dir);
	const current = [...skills].reverse().find((item) => item.kind === kind && !item.superseded && (!scope || item.workspace === scope.workspace && sameRoot(item.root, scope.root)));
	if (!current) return;
	if (taskId && current.failures?.some((failure) => failure.taskId === taskId)) return;
	current.lastFailure = reason;
	current.failures = [...current.failures ?? [], {
		reason,
		at: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(),
		taskId
	}];
	writeAll(dir, skills);
}
/** 技能里的工具必须是这次允许的子集。越出范围就不用它。 */
function skillFits(skill, allowedTools, scope) {
	if (skill.superseded || !skill.verification || !scope || scope.workspace !== skill.workspace || skill.root && !sameRoot(skill.root, scope.root)) return false;
	if (skill.allowedTools.length === 0) return false;
	return skill.allowedTools.every((tool) => allowedTools.includes(tool));
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/local/embedding.ts
/**
* 记忆向量在本机算，不依赖 Ollama。国内直连装不上 Ollama，
* 而记忆只需要一个中文向量模型，随安装包带走即可。
*/
var EMBEDDING_MODEL_ID = "bge-small-zh-v1.5";
var EMBEDDING_PROFILE = "cls-q8-l2-v1";
var EMBEDDING_FILES = [
	"config.json",
	"tokenizer.json",
	"tokenizer_config.json",
	(0, node_path.join)("onnx", "model_quantized.onnx")
];
function missingEmbeddingFiles(userDataDir) {
	return missingModelFiles(localModelDirectory(userDataDir, EMBEDDING_MODEL_ID, EMBEDDING_FILES), EMBEDDING_FILES);
}
function vectorsCompatible(storedModelId, storedDim, storedProfile) {
	if (storedModelId !== "bge-small-zh-v1.5" || storedDim !== 512 || storedProfile !== "cls-q8-l2-v1") return "recompute";
	return "ok";
}
function cosine(left, right) {
	let dot = 0;
	let leftNorm = 0;
	let rightNorm = 0;
	const length = Math.min(left.length, right.length);
	for (let index = 0; index < length; index += 1) {
		dot += left[index] * right[index];
		leftNorm += left[index] * left[index];
		rightNorm += right[index] * right[index];
	}
	if (leftNorm === 0 || rightNorm === 0) return 0;
	return dot / Math.sqrt(leftNorm * rightNorm);
}
function rrf(rankings, k = 60) {
	const scores = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	for (const ranking of rankings) ranking.forEach((id, index) => {
		scores.set(id, (scores.get(id) ?? 0) + 1 / (k + index + 1));
	});
	return [...scores.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).map(([id]) => id);
}
function recallMemories(query, entries, queryVector) {
	const lexical = entries.filter((entry) => {
		return query.split(/[，。\s]/).filter((token) => token.length >= 2).some((token) => entry.text.includes(token));
	}).map((entry) => entry.id);
	if (!queryVector) return {
		ids: [],
		degraded: true,
		injected: []
	};
	const limited = limitTokens(rrf([[...entries].filter((entry) => entry.vector?.length === queryVector.length && cosine(queryVector, entry.vector) >= .45).sort((a, b) => cosine(queryVector, b.vector ?? []) - cosine(queryVector, a.vector ?? [])).map((entry) => entry.id), lexical]).slice(0, 8).map((id) => entries.find((entry) => entry.id === id)).filter((entry) => Boolean(entry)));
	return {
		ids: limited.map((entry) => entry.id),
		degraded: false,
		injected: limited
	};
}
function limitTokens(entries) {
	const kept = [];
	let tokens = 0;
	for (const entry of entries) {
		tokens += entry.text.length;
		if (kept.length >= 8 || tokens > 1500) break;
		kept.push(entry);
	}
	return kept;
}
var extractor = null;
async function load$1(userDataDir) {
	const directory = localModelDirectory(userDataDir, EMBEDDING_MODEL_ID, EMBEDDING_FILES);
	if (extractor?.directory === directory) return extractor.pending;
	const current = {
		directory,
		pending: (async () => {
			await configureLocalModels(userDataDir);
			const { pipeline } = await import("@huggingface/transformers");
			return await pipeline("feature-extraction", directory, {
				dtype: "q8",
				device: "cpu",
				local_files_only: true
			});
		})()
	};
	extractor = current;
	try {
		return await current.pending;
	} catch (error) {
		if (extractor === current) extractor = null;
		throw error;
	}
}
/**
* 算不出来时返回 null，调用方会如实降级，不会拿关键词硬凑当成语义召回。
* BGE v1.5 的短句可不加查询前缀；按官方规范取首token(CLS)并归一化。
*/
async function embedLocally(text, userDataDir) {
	if (missingEmbeddingFiles(userDataDir).length > 0) return null;
	try {
		const output = await (await load$1(userDataDir))(text, {
			pooling: "cls",
			normalize: true
		});
		if (output.data.length !== 512) return null;
		const vector = Array.from(output.data);
		return vector.every(Number.isFinite) && vector.some((value) => value !== 0) ? vector : null;
	} catch {
		return null;
	}
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/local/memory-status.ts
var status = "正在检查记忆服务";
var ready = false;
function memoryStatusText() {
	return status;
}
function memoryReady() {
	return ready;
}
/**
* 向量模型随安装包一起装，不需要联网也不需要额外的后台服务。
* 文件不全时如实说明，记忆不会被带进对话。
*/
function refreshMemoryStatus(userDataDir) {
	const missing = missingEmbeddingFiles(userDataDir);
	ready = missing.length === 0;
	status = ready ? "记忆服务已就绪，保存的偏好会按意思被想起来。" : `本机向量模型 ${EMBEDDING_MODEL_ID} 缺少 ${missing.join("、")}。没补齐之前，记忆不会被带进对话。`;
	return status;
}
