//#region src/main/memory/facts.ts
var secretPattern = /sk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}|api[_-]?key|密码是|原始屏幕|screenshot/i;
function normalizeKey(text) {
	return text.replace(/\s+/g, "").toLowerCase();
}
function extractCandidates(text) {
	const found = [];
	for (const rule of [
		{
			category: "user",
			pattern: /请叫我([^，。！\s]{1,20})/
		},
		{
			category: "user",
			pattern: /我喜欢([^，。！]{1,40})/
		},
		{
			category: "feedback",
			pattern: /我不([^，。！]{1,40})/
		},
		{
			category: "user",
			pattern: /我习惯([^，。！]{1,40})/
		},
		{
			category: "project",
			pattern: /记住[：:]([^，。！]{1,80})/
		},
		{
			category: "user",
			pattern: /我的名字是([^，。！\s]{1,20})/
		},
		{
			category: "user",
			pattern: /以后叫我([^，。！\s]{1,20})/
		},
		{
			category: "feedback",
			pattern: /我希望([^，。！]{1,40})/
		},
		{
			category: "project",
			pattern: /请记住([^，。！]{1,80})/
		}
	]) {
		const match = rule.pattern.exec(text);
		if (match?.[1]) found.push({
			category: rule.category,
			text: match[1].trim()
		});
	}
	return found.filter((item) => safeCandidate(item.text));
}
function emotionOf(text) {
	if (/不|讨厌|烦|怕/.test(text)) return "aversion";
	if (/喜欢|习惯|偏好/.test(text)) return "preference";
	if (/累|困/.test(text)) return "fatigue";
	if (/急|担心/.test(text)) return "concern";
	if (/开心|高兴/.test(text)) return "joy";
	if (/专注|目标/.test(text)) return "focus";
	if (/平静|还好/.test(text)) return "calm";
	return "neutral";
}
/** 模型提出的候选里可能混进密钥或提交信息，这些不进记忆。 */
function safeCandidate(text) {
	return !secretPattern.test(text) && !/git\s|commit/i.test(text);
}
function applyMemoryAction(existing, candidate, action, now, suppressed, replaces) {
	if (action === "NOOP") return existing;
	const key = normalizeKey(candidate.text);
	if (suppressed.has(key) && action !== "DELETE") return existing;
	if (action === "ADD" && existing.some((item) => item.normalizedKey === key && !item.validUntil)) return existing;
	if (action === "ADD") return [...existing, {
		id: `mem-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`,
		category: candidate.category,
		text: candidate.text,
		normalizedKey: key,
		validUntil: null,
		emotion: emotionOf(candidate.text),
		createdAt: now,
		updatedAt: now
	}];
	const targetKey = replaces?.trim() ? normalizeKey(replaces) : key;
	const target = existing.find((item) => (item.id === replaces || item.normalizedKey === targetKey) && !item.validUntil);
	if (action === "UPDATE" && !target) return existing;
	return existing.map((item) => (item.id === replaces || item.normalizedKey === targetKey) && !item.validUntil ? {
		...item,
		validUntil: now,
		updatedAt: now
	} : item).concat(action === "UPDATE" ? [{
		id: `mem-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`,
		category: candidate.category,
		text: candidate.text,
		normalizedKey: key,
		validUntil: null,
		emotion: emotionOf(candidate.text),
		source: target?.source,
		createdAt: now,
		updatedAt: now
	}] : []);
}
function activeMemories(entries) {
	return entries.filter((item) => !item.validUntil);
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/agent/memory-extractor.ts
function guardExtractionTool(purpose) {
	if (purpose === "read" || purpose === "memory-write") return {
		allowed: true,
		reason: ""
	};
	return {
		allowed: false,
		reason: "提取过程只能读取和写入记忆，已拒绝该调用"
	};
}
function extractionOrder(calls) {
	if (calls.some((call) => !guardExtractionTool(call.purpose).allowed)) return "denied";
	const purposes = calls.map((call) => call.purpose);
	const firstWrite = purposes.indexOf("memory-write");
	const lastRead = purposes.lastIndexOf("read");
	if (firstWrite !== -1 && lastRead > firstWrite) return "interleaved";
	return "separated";
}
function runExtraction(text, existing, suppressed, calls = [], judge = () => "ADD") {
	const denied = calls.filter((call) => !guardExtractionTool(call.purpose).allowed).map((call) => call.toolId);
	const reads = calls.filter((call) => call.purpose === "read");
	const writes = calls.filter((call) => call.purpose === "memory-write");
	if (extractionOrder([...reads, ...writes]) === "interleaved") return {
		facts: [...existing],
		denied,
		failed: true
	};
	try {
		const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
		let facts = [...existing];
		const candidates = extractCandidates(text).filter((item) => !suppressed.has(normalizeKey(item.text)));
		for (const candidate of candidates) facts = applyMemoryAction(facts, candidate, judge(candidate), now, suppressed);
		return {
			facts,
			denied,
			failed: false
		};
	} catch {
		return {
			facts: [...existing],
			denied,
			failed: true
		};
	}
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/core/services/memory-service.ts
var ok$3 = (data) => ({
	ok: true,
	data
});
var fail$3 = (error) => ({
	ok: false,
	error
});
var labels = {
	user: "偏好",
	feedback: "反馈",
	project: "项目",
	reference: "参考"
};
var aliases = {
	user: "user",
	feedback: "feedback",
	project: "project",
	reference: "reference",
	偏好: "user",
	用户: "user",
	反馈: "feedback",
	项目: "project",
	参考: "reference"
};
var toEntry = (fact) => ({
	id: fact.id,
	category: labels[fact.category],
	text: fact.text,
	source: fact.validUntil ? `失效于 ${fact.validUntil}，仍可追溯` : fact.source ?? "长期记忆",
	updated: fact.validUntil ? "已失效" : "有效"
});
function readVector(value) {
	if (!value) return null;
	try {
		const vector = JSON.parse(value);
		return Array.isArray(vector) && vector.length === 512 && vector.every((part) => typeof part === "number" && Number.isFinite(part)) ? vector : null;
	} catch {
		return null;
	}
}
var MemoryService = class {
	db;
	userDataDir;
	facts = [];
	suppressed = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
	generation = 0;
	deletedSources = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
	sourceVersions = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	listeners = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
	recallOn = true;
	extractionOn = true;
	extractionQueues = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	factVersions = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	storageReady = true;
	reindexing;
	indexController;
	pendingIndexEmbedding;
	indexListeners = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
	index = {
		state: "idle",
		completed: 0,
		total: 0,
		message: "没有待恢复的记忆索引"
	};
	constructor(db, userDataDir = "") {
		this.db = db;
		this.userDataDir = userDataDir;
		this.load();
		this.publishIndex("paused", "记忆索引尚未完整，后续交流时在后台恢复");
	}
	indexStatus() {
		return { ...this.index };
	}
	subscribeIndex(listener) {
		this.indexListeners.add(listener);
		return () => {
			this.indexListeners.delete(listener);
		};
	}
	/** Legacy master switch. Automatic extraction can also be disabled independently. */
	get enabled() {
		return this.recallOn && this.extractionOn;
	}
	set enabled(value) {
		this.configure({
			recallEnabled: value,
			extractionEnabled: value
		});
	}
	get recallEnabled() {
		return this.recallOn;
	}
	get extractionEnabled() {
		return this.extractionOn;
	}
	configure(settings) {
		const changed = this.recallOn !== settings.recallEnabled || this.extractionOn !== settings.extractionEnabled;
		if (this.extractionOn && !settings.extractionEnabled) this.generation++;
		this.recallOn = settings.recallEnabled;
		this.extractionOn = settings.extractionEnabled;
		if (changed) {
			this.cancelIndex("记忆设置已改变，索引恢复已暂停");
			this.notify();
		}
	}
	subscribe(listener) {
		this.listeners.add(listener);
		return () => {
			this.listeners.delete(listener);
		};
	}
	invalidateSource(source) {
		this.deletedSources.add(source);
		this.cancelIndex("来源已删除，索引恢复已暂停");
	}
	list() {
		return this.active().map(toEntry);
	}
	history() {
		return this.facts.map(toEntry);
	}
	active() {
		return structuredClone(activeMemories(this.facts));
	}
	async recall(query) {
		if (!this.recallOn) return {
			text: "",
			ids: [],
			degraded: false,
			skipped: "disabled"
		};
		const generation = this.generation;
		const queryVector = await embedLocally(query, this.userDataDir);
		if (!this.recallOn) return {
			text: "",
			ids: [],
			degraded: false,
			skipped: "disabled"
		};
		if (generation !== this.generation) return {
			text: "",
			ids: [],
			degraded: true,
			skipped: "changed"
		};
		const active = this.active();
		const result = recallMemories(query, active.map((fact) => ({
			id: fact.id,
			text: fact.text,
			category: fact.category,
			vector: fact.vector ?? null
		})), queryVector);
		if (queryVector && this.storageReady) this.startIndex(generation);
		return {
			text: result.injected.map((item) => item.text).join("\n"),
			ids: result.ids,
			degraded: !this.storageReady || result.degraded || active.some((fact) => !fact.vector)
		};
	}
	publishIndex(state, message) {
		const active = activeMemories(this.facts);
		const completed = active.filter((fact) => fact.vector).length;
		const total = active.length;
		if (!this.storageReady) {
			state = "failed";
			message = "记忆存储读取失败，索引恢复未完成；请重启后重试";
		} else if (completed === total) {
			state = "idle";
			message = total ? "现有记忆索引已就绪" : "没有待恢复的记忆索引";
		}
		this.index = {
			state,
			completed,
			total,
			message
		};
		for (const listener of this.indexListeners) try {
			listener(this.indexStatus());
		} catch {}
	}
	cancelIndex(message) {
		this.indexController?.abort();
		this.publishIndex("paused", message);
	}
	startIndex(generation) {
		if (this.reindexing || !this.active().some((fact) => !fact.vector)) return;
		if (this.pendingIndexEmbedding) return;
		const controller = new AbortController();
		this.indexController = controller;
		this.publishIndex("rebuilding", "正在后台恢复记忆索引，当前对话可继续");
		const run = Promise.resolve().then(() => this.rebuildMissingVectors(generation, controller.signal)).catch(() => {
			if (!controller.signal.aborted) this.publishIndex("failed", "记忆索引恢复失败，已完成部分保留，后续交流时重试");
		});
		this.reindexing = run;
		run.finally(() => {
			if (this.reindexing === run) this.reindexing = void 0;
			if (this.indexController === controller) this.indexController = void 0;
		});
	}
	async rebuildMissingVectors(generation, signal) {
		const deadline = Date.now() + 3e4;
		let processed = 0;
		for (const fact of this.active().filter((item) => !item.vector)) {
			if (signal.aborted || !this.recallOn || generation !== this.generation) return;
			if (Date.now() >= deadline) {
				this.publishIndex("paused", "本轮索引恢复达到时间限额，已完成部分保留，后续交流时继续");
				return;
			}
			if (!this.facts.some((item) => item.id === fact.id && !item.validUntil && !item.vector)) continue;
			const work = Promise.resolve().then(() => embedLocally(fact.text, this.userDataDir));
			this.pendingIndexEmbedding = work;
			const release = () => {
				if (this.pendingIndexEmbedding === work) this.pendingIndexEmbedding = void 0;
			};
			work.then(release, release);
			const outcome = await this.waitForIndex(work, Math.min(2e3, deadline - Date.now()), signal);
			if (signal.aborted || !this.recallOn || generation !== this.generation) return;
			if (outcome.kind !== "ready" || !outcome.vector) {
				this.publishIndex("failed", outcome.kind === "timeout" ? "本地记忆索引计算超时，已完成部分保留；等待本地计算结束后可重试" : "本地记忆索引暂不可用，已完成部分保留，后续交流时重试");
				return;
			}
			const current = this.facts.find((item) => item.id === fact.id && !item.validUntil && !item.vector);
			if (current) {
				try {
					this.db?.prepare(`UPDATE memories SET vector_json = ? WHERE id = ? AND valid_until IS NULL`).run(JSON.stringify(outcome.vector), current.id);
					current.vector = outcome.vector;
				} catch {
					this.publishIndex("failed", "记忆索引保存失败，已完成部分保留，后续交流时重试");
					return;
				}
				this.publishIndex("rebuilding", "正在后台恢复记忆索引，当前对话可继续");
			}
			processed++;
			if (processed % 16 === 0) await new Promise((done) => setTimeout(done, 0));
		}
		if (!signal.aborted) this.publishIndex("paused", "尚有待恢复的记忆索引，后续交流时继续");
	}
	waitForIndex(work, timeoutMs, signal) {
		return new Promise((resolve) => {
			const finish = (result) => {
				clearTimeout(timer);
				signal.removeEventListener("abort", abort);
				resolve(result);
			};
			const abort = () => finish({ kind: "aborted" });
			const timer = setTimeout(() => finish({ kind: "timeout" }), Math.max(0, timeoutMs));
			signal.addEventListener("abort", abort, { once: true });
			if (signal.aborted) abort();
			work.then((vector) => finish({
				kind: "ready",
				vector
			}), () => finish({ kind: "failed" }));
		});
	}
	async add(category, text) {
		const mapped = aliases[category.trim()];
		text = text.trim();
		if (!mapped || !text) return fail$3("记忆类别或内容无效");
		const generation = this.generation;
		const key = normalizeKey(text);
		const version = this.factVersions.get(key);
		const vector = await embedLocally(text, this.userDataDir);
		if (generation !== this.generation) return fail$3("记忆已清除，请重新保存");
		if (version !== this.factVersions.get(key)) return fail$3("记忆已被更改，请刷新后重试");
		const suppressed = new Set(this.suppressed);
		suppressed.delete(normalizeKey(text));
		const next = applyMemoryAction(this.facts, {
			category: mapped,
			text
		}, "ADD", (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), suppressed).map((fact) => !fact.validUntil && fact.normalizedKey === normalizeKey(text) ? {
			...fact,
			vector
		} : fact);
		const created = next.find((fact) => !fact.validUntil && fact.normalizedKey === normalizeKey(text));
		return created && this.commit(next, suppressed) ? ok$3(toEntry(created)) : fail$3("保存失败，修改未完成");
	}
	async update(id, category, text) {
		const mapped = aliases[category.trim()];
		text = text.trim();
		const current = this.facts.find((fact) => fact.id === id && !fact.validUntil);
		if (!mapped || !text || !current) return fail$3("记忆条目不存在或内容无效");
		if (current.category === mapped && current.text === text) return ok$3(toEntry(current));
		const generation = this.generation;
		const vector = await embedLocally(text, this.userDataDir);
		if (generation !== this.generation || !this.facts.some((fact) => fact.id === id && !fact.validUntil)) return fail$3("记忆已被更改，请刷新后重试");
		if (this.facts.some((fact) => fact.id !== id && !fact.validUntil && fact.normalizedKey === normalizeKey(text))) return fail$3("已有相同内容的记忆，请编辑该条目");
		const suppressed = new Set(this.suppressed);
		suppressed.add(current.normalizedKey);
		suppressed.delete(normalizeKey(text));
		const next = applyMemoryAction(this.facts, {
			category: mapped,
			text
		}, "UPDATE", (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), suppressed, id).map((fact) => !fact.validUntil && fact.normalizedKey === normalizeKey(text) ? {
			...fact,
			source: current.source,
			vector
		} : fact);
		const edited = next.find((fact) => !fact.validUntil && fact.normalizedKey === normalizeKey(text));
		return edited && this.commit(next, suppressed) ? ok$3(toEntry(edited)) : fail$3("保存失败，修改未完成");
	}
	async delete(id) {
		const current = this.facts.find((fact) => fact.id === id && !fact.validUntil);
		if (!current) return fail$3("记忆条目不存在");
		const suppressed = new Set(this.suppressed).add(current.normalizedKey);
		const next = applyMemoryAction(this.facts, current, "DELETE", (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), suppressed, id);
		return this.commit(next, suppressed) ? ok$3(id) : fail$3("保存失败，修改未完成");
	}
	async clear() {
		this.generation++;
		this.cancelIndex("记忆已清除，索引恢复已停止");
		const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
		const suppressed = new Set(this.suppressed);
		const next = this.facts.map((fact) => {
			if (fact.validUntil) return fact;
			suppressed.add(fact.normalizedKey);
			return {
				...fact,
				validUntil: now,
				updatedAt: now
			};
		});
		return this.commit(next, suppressed) ? ok$3(this.list()) : fail$3("保存失败，修改未完成");
	}
	ingest(text) {
		if (!this.extractionOn) return this.active();
		const outcome = runExtraction(text, this.facts, this.suppressed);
		if (!outcome.failed) this.commit(outcome.facts);
		return this.active();
	}
	async ingestJudged(text, judge, source = "长期记忆") {
		return this.ingestProposed(text, async () => {
			const proposals = [];
			for (const candidate of extractCandidates(text)) {
				const action = await judge(candidate);
				if (action !== "NOOP") proposals.push({
					...candidate,
					action
				});
			}
			return proposals;
		}, source);
	}
	async ingestProposed(text, propose, source = "长期记忆") {
		const generation = this.generation;
		const sourceVersion = this.sourceVersions.get(source) ?? 0;
		if (source === "长期记忆") return this.applyProposed(text, propose, source, generation, sourceVersion);
		const run = (this.extractionQueues.get(source) ?? Promise.resolve()).then(() => this.applyProposed(text, propose, source, generation, sourceVersion));
		const settled = run.then(() => void 0, () => void 0);
		this.extractionQueues.set(source, settled);
		try {
			return await run;
		} finally {
			if (this.extractionQueues.get(source) === settled) this.extractionQueues.delete(source);
		}
	}
	async applyProposed(text, propose, source, generation, sourceVersion) {
		if (!this.extractionOn || this.deletedSources.has(source)) return {
			status: "skipped",
			reason: "记忆提取已停用或来源已删除"
		};
		if (generation !== this.generation || sourceVersion !== (this.sourceVersions.get(source) ?? 0)) return {
			status: "skipped",
			reason: "数据已清除或提取已停用"
		};
		const snapshot = this.active();
		try {
			const proposals = await propose(text, snapshot.map((fact) => fact.text));
			const indexed = await Promise.all(proposals.filter((proposal) => [
				"ADD",
				"UPDATE",
				"DELETE"
			].includes(proposal.action) && Boolean(labels[proposal.category]) && proposal.text?.trim() && safeCandidate(proposal.text)).map(async (proposal) => ({
				...proposal,
				text: proposal.text.trim(),
				vector: proposal.action === "DELETE" ? null : await embedLocally(proposal.text, this.userDataDir)
			})));
			if (!this.extractionOn || generation !== this.generation || this.deletedSources.has(source) || sourceVersion !== (this.sourceVersions.get(source) ?? 0)) return {
				status: "skipped",
				reason: "数据已清除、停用或来源已删除"
			};
			let next = this.facts;
			const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
			const suppressed = new Set(this.suppressed);
			for (const proposal of indexed) {
				const targetKey = normalizeKey(proposal.replaces ?? proposal.text);
				const target = snapshot.find((fact) => fact.id === proposal.replaces || fact.normalizedKey === targetKey);
				if (proposal.action !== "ADD" && (!target || !next.some((fact) => fact.id === target.id && !fact.validUntil))) continue;
				if (proposal.action === "ADD" && suppressed.has(normalizeKey(proposal.text))) continue;
				if (proposal.action === "ADD" && target && !next.some((fact) => fact.id === target.id && !fact.validUntil)) continue;
				if (proposal.action === "UPDATE" && target?.category === proposal.category && target.text === proposal.text) continue;
				const duplicate = proposal.action === "UPDATE" && next.some((fact) => !fact.validUntil && fact.id !== target?.id && fact.normalizedKey === normalizeKey(proposal.text));
				const beforeIds = new Set(next.map((fact) => fact.id));
				const applied = applyMemoryAction(next, proposal, duplicate ? "DELETE" : proposal.action, now, suppressed, target?.id);
				if (applied === next) continue;
				next = applied;
				if (proposal.action === "DELETE" && target) suppressed.add(target.normalizedKey);
				const factSource = source === "长期记忆" ? target?.source ?? source : source;
				next = next.map((fact) => beforeIds.has(fact.id) ? fact : {
					...fact,
					source: factSource,
					vector: proposal.vector
				});
			}
			if (next === this.facts) return { status: "unchanged" };
			return this.commit(next, suppressed) ? { status: "saved" } : {
				status: "failed",
				reason: "记忆保存失败"
			};
		} catch {
			return {
				status: "failed",
				reason: "记忆提取失败，已有记忆未更改"
			};
		}
	}
	async clearSource(source) {
		const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
		const suppressed = new Set(this.suppressed);
		const next = this.facts.map((fact) => {
			if (fact.source !== source || fact.validUntil) return fact;
			suppressed.add(fact.normalizedKey);
			return {
				...fact,
				validUntil: now,
				updatedAt: now
			};
		});
		const version = this.sourceVersions.get(source) ?? 0;
		this.sourceVersions.set(source, version + 1);
		if (this.commit(next, suppressed)) return ok$3(this.list());
		this.sourceVersions.set(source, version);
		return fail$3("保存失败，修改未完成");
	}
	commit(next, suppressed = this.suppressed) {
		if (!this.storageReady) return false;
		if (!this.persist(next, suppressed)) return false;
		const before = new Map(activeMemories(this.facts).map((fact) => [fact.normalizedKey, fact.id]));
		const after = new Map(activeMemories(next).map((fact) => [fact.normalizedKey, fact.id]));
		for (const key of /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([...before.keys(), ...after.keys()])) if (before.get(key) !== after.get(key)) this.factVersions.set(key, (this.factVersions.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);
		this.facts = next;
		this.suppressed = suppressed;
		if ([...before].some(([key, id]) => after.get(key) !== id)) this.cancelIndex("记忆条目已更改，旧索引恢复已停止");
		else this.publishIndex(this.index.state === "rebuilding" ? "rebuilding" : "paused", "记忆索引尚未完整，后续交流时在后台恢复");
		this.notify();
		return true;
	}
	notify() {
		for (const listener of this.listeners) try {
			listener();
		} catch {}
	}
	load() {
		if (!this.db) return;
		try {
			const rows = this.db.prepare(`SELECT id, category, text, source, normalized_key, valid_until, emotion, vector_json, created_at, updated_at FROM memories`).all();
			this.facts = rows.map((row) => ({
				id: row.id,
				category: row.category,
				text: row.text,
				normalizedKey: normalizeKey(row.text),
				validUntil: row.valid_until,
				emotion: row.emotion ?? "neutral",
				source: row.source ?? void 0,
				vector: readVector(row.vector_json),
				createdAt: row.created_at,
				updatedAt: row.updated_at
			}));
			this.db.prepare(`SELECT normalized_key FROM suppressed_facts`).all().forEach((row) => this.suppressed.add(row.normalized_key));
			this.alignEmbeddingStamp();
		} catch {
			this.storageReady = false;
			this.facts = this.facts.map((fact) => ({
				...fact,
				vector: null
			}));
		}
	}
	alignEmbeddingStamp() {
		if (!this.db) return;
		const stamp = `${EMBEDDING_MODEL_ID}:512:${EMBEDDING_PROFILE}`;
		const row = this.db.prepare(`SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key = 'embedding'`).get();
		const [modelId, dim, profile] = (row?.value ?? "").split(":");
		if (!row || vectorsCompatible(modelId, Number(dim), profile) === "recompute") {
			this.facts = this.facts.map((fact) => ({
				...fact,
				vector: null
			}));
			const db = this.db;
			db.transaction(() => {
				db.prepare(`UPDATE memories SET vector_json = NULL`).run();
				db.prepare(`INSERT INTO meta(key, value) VALUES('embedding', ?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value`).run(stamp);
			})();
		}
	}
	persist(facts, suppressed) {
		if (!this.db) return true;
		const db = this.db;
		try {
			db.transaction(() => {
				db.prepare(`DELETE FROM memories`).run();
				const insert = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO memories(id, category, text, source, created_at, updated_at, valid_from, valid_until, emotion, normalized_key, vector_json)
           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`);
				const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
				for (const fact of facts) insert.run(fact.id, fact.category, fact.text, fact.source ?? "长期记忆", fact.createdAt ?? now, fact.updatedAt ?? now, fact.createdAt ?? now, fact.validUntil, fact.emotion, fact.normalizedKey, fact.vector ? JSON.stringify(fact.vector) : null);
				db.prepare(`DELETE FROM suppressed_facts`).run();
				const suppress = db.prepare(`INSERT INTO suppressed_facts(normalized_key, created_at) VALUES (?, ?)`);
				for (const key of suppressed) suppress.run(key, now);
			})();
			return true;
		} catch {
			return false;
		}
	}
};
//#endregion
//#region src/main/core/services/character-service.ts
var ok$2 = (data) => ({
	ok: true,
	data
});
var fail$2 = (error) => ({
	ok: false,
	error
});
var empty$1 = () => ({
	name: "",
	style: "",
	example: "",
	keywords: [],
	proactive: false,
	memory: true
});
var CharacterService = class {
	db;
	constructor(db) {
		this.db = db;
	}
	load() {
		if (!this.db) return empty$1();
		try {
			const row = this.db.prepare(`SELECT name, style, example, keywords_json, proactive, memory_enabled FROM character_profile WHERE id = 1`).get();
			if (!row) return empty$1();
			return {
				name: row.name,
				style: row.style,
				example: row.example,
				keywords: JSON.parse(row.keywords_json),
				proactive: row.proactive === 1,
				memory: row.memory_enabled === 1
			};
		} catch {
			return empty$1();
		}
	}
	save(profile) {
		if (!profile.name.trim() || !profile.style.trim()) return Promise.resolve(fail$2("角色名称和交流风格不能为空"));
		if (!this.db) return Promise.resolve(fail$2("保存失败，修改未完成"));
		const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
		try {
			this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO character_profile(id, name, style, example, keywords_json, proactive, memory_enabled, updated_at)
         VALUES (1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
         ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
           name = excluded.name,
           style = excluded.style,
           example = excluded.example,
           keywords_json = excluded.keywords_json,
           proactive = excluded.proactive,
           memory_enabled = excluded.memory_enabled,
           updated_at = excluded.updated_at`).run(profile.name.trim(), profile.style.trim(), profile.example, JSON.stringify(profile.keywords), profile.proactive ? 1 : 0, profile.memory ? 1 : 0, now);
		} catch {
			return Promise.resolve(fail$2("保存失败，修改未完成"));
		}
		const saved = this.load();
		if (saved.name !== profile.name.trim()) return Promise.resolve(fail$2("保存失败，修改未完成"));
		return Promise.resolve(ok$2(saved));
	}
};
//#endregion
//#region src/main/local/error-patterns.ts
var patterns = [
	{
		kind: "Python Traceback",
		pattern: /File "([^"]+)", line (\d+).*\n(?:.*\n)*?(\w*(?:Error|Exception): .+)/
	},
	{
		kind: "Node 堆栈",
		pattern: /at .+\(([^():]+):(\d+):\d+\)[\s\S]*?(Error: .+)/
	},
	{
		kind: "编译错误",
		pattern: /([^:\s]+\.(?:c|cc|cpp|h|rs|go)):(\d+):\d+: error: (.+)/
	},
	{
		kind: "HTTP 状态码",
		pattern: /\bHTTP(?:\/\d(?:\.\d)?)?\s+([45]\d\d)\b[^\n]*?(?:[:：]\s*(.+))?/
	},
	{
		kind: "Windows 错误码",
		pattern: /(?:错误|error)\s*(?:代码|code)?\s*[:：]?\s*(0x[0-9a-fA-F]+|\d{3,})[^\n]*?(?:[:：]\s*(.+))?/
	}
];
function parseKnownError(text) {
	for (const rule of patterns) {
		const match = rule.pattern.exec(text);
		if (!match) continue;
		if (rule.kind === "HTTP 状态码") return {
			kind: rule.kind,
			file: "",
			line: "",
			message: `HTTP ${match[1]}${match[2] ? ` ${match[2]}` : ""}`
		};
		if (rule.kind === "Windows 错误码") return {
			kind: rule.kind,
			file: "",
			line: "",
			message: `${match[1]}${match[2] ? ` ${match[2]}` : ""}`
		};
		return {
			kind: rule.kind,
			file: match[1] ?? "",
			line: match[2] ?? "",
			message: (match[3] ?? "").trim()
		};
	}
	return null;
}
function needsMoreContext(text, parsed) {
	const compact = text.replace(/\s+/g, "");
	return !parsed && compact.length < 8;
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/local/set-of-mark.ts
var interactive = /button|edit|hyperlink|menuitem|listitem|checkbox|radiobutton|combobox|tabitem|splitbutton/i;
function markElements(elements) {
	return elements.filter((element) => interactive.test(element.controlType) && element.bounds.width > 0 && element.bounds.height > 0).slice(0, 32).map((element, index) => ({
		...element,
		id: index + 1
	}));
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/core/services/screen-service.ts
var empty = (reason, watch) => ({
	title: "没有采集",
	confidence: "未采集",
	summary: reason,
	bullets: [],
	action: "改为文字描述",
	sourceWindow: "",
	resolution: "",
	capturedAt: "",
	continuous: false,
	tree: "",
	image: "",
	untrusted: true,
	marks: [],
	refusal: reason,
	needsTaskConfirm: false,
	timings: {
		captureMs: 0,
		waitMs: 0,
		generateMs: 0
	},
	watch
});
function buildScreenAnalysis(input, startedAt = Date.now()) {
	const watch = watchDecision(input.watch);
	if (input.captureError) return empty(input.captureError, input.watch.failures >= 2 ? "fused" : watch.status);
	const decision = captureAllowed({
		authorized: input.authorized,
		window: input.window,
		revoked: input.revoked
	});
	if (!decision.capture) return empty(decision.reason, watch.status);
	const observed = markUntrusted([
		input.window.tree,
		input.window.selectedText,
		input.ocrText
	].filter(Boolean).join("\n"), "screen");
	const parsed = parseKnownError(`${input.window.tree}\n${input.ocrText}`);
	const marks = markElements(input.elements);
	const captureMs = input.timings?.captureMs ?? (input.imageCaptured ? 1 : 0);
	const waitMs = input.timings?.waitMs ?? 0;
	const generateMs = input.timings?.generateMs ?? 0;
	if (input.imageCaptured && input.visionError && !parsed) return {
		...empty(input.visionError, watch.status),
		title: input.window.title || "当前窗口",
		sourceWindow: input.window.title,
		confidence: "未理解",
		summary: `${input.visionError}，没有声称已经理解画面`,
		tree: observed,
		marks,
		image: "captured",
		resolution: input.resolution || "已采集",
		capturedAt: new Date(startedAt).toISOString(),
		timings: {
			captureMs,
			waitMs,
			generateMs
		}
	};
	if (needsMoreContext(`${input.window.tree}${input.ocrText}`, parsed) && !input.supplemented && !(input.imageCaptured && input.visionAvailable && input.modelSummary?.trim())) return {
		...empty("画面信息不足，请补充文字或换一个更清晰的窗口后再分析", watch.status),
		title: input.window.title || "当前窗口",
		sourceWindow: input.window.title,
		tree: observed,
		marks,
		timings: {
			captureMs,
			waitMs,
			generateMs
		},
		capturedAt: new Date(startedAt).toISOString()
	};
	if (!parsed && !input.visionAvailable) return {
		...empty(input.visionError || "当前模型不支持图像，没有声称已经理解画面", watch.status),
		title: input.window.title || "当前窗口",
		sourceWindow: input.window.title,
		tree: observed,
		marks,
		confidence: "未理解",
		summary: `${input.visionError || "当前模型不支持图像"}，没有声称已经理解画面`,
		timings: {
			captureMs,
			waitMs,
			generateMs
		}
	};
	const intent = operationIntent(`${input.window.title}\n${parsed?.message ?? ""}`);
	const mayExecute = screenInstructionIsExecutable("screen");
	const summary = input.modelSummary ? input.modelSummary : parsed ? `规则解析到${parsed.kind}：${parsed.file} ${parsed.line} ${parsed.message}`.trim() : "已阅读控件树和识别到的文字。这些内容只作观察，不会当成指令执行。";
	return {
		title: parsed ? parsed.kind : input.window.title || "当前画面",
		confidence: input.modelSummary ? "模型" : parsed ? "规则" : "观察",
		summary,
		bullets: [observed.slice(0, 240), intent ? "这是操作意图，确认后才会创建任务" : "本次只解释内容，不改文件"].filter(Boolean),
		action: mayExecute ? "外部内容不可执行" : intent ? "确认是否创建任务" : "仅作说明",
		sourceWindow: `${input.window.processName} ${input.window.title}`.trim(),
		resolution: input.imageCaptured ? input.resolution || "已采集" : "未采集",
		capturedAt: new Date(startedAt).toISOString(),
		continuous: watch.status === "running",
		tree: observed,
		image: input.imageCaptured ? "captured" : "",
		untrusted: true,
		marks,
		refusal: "",
		needsTaskConfirm: intent && !mayExecute,
		timings: {
			captureMs,
			waitMs,
			generateMs
		},
		watch: watch.status
	};
}
var ScreenService = class {
	failures = 0;
	analyze(input) {
		const analysis = buildScreenAnalysis(input);
		if (analysis.refusal && input.captureError && !input.captureSkipped) this.failures += 1;
		if (!analysis.refusal) this.failures = 0;
		return Promise.resolve({
			ok: true,
			data: analysis
		});
	}
};
//#endregion
//#region src/main/local/phonemize.ts
var run = (0, node_util.promisify)(node_child_process.execFile);
var script = [
	"import sys, io",
	"sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding=\"utf-8\")",
	"from misaki import zh",
	"g = zh.ZHG2P()",
	"ps, _ = g(sys.argv[1])",
	"sys.stdout.write(ps)"
].join("\n");
var PhonemizerUnavailable = class extends Error {
	constructor(detail) {
		super(`中文音素转换不可用：${detail}`);
		this.name = "PhonemizerUnavailable";
	}
};
/** 汉字转音素只做这一步，推理仍在本进程内完成。 */
async function phonemizeChinese(text, interpreter = chinesePythonInterpreter()) {
	try {
		const { stdout } = await run(interpreter, [
			"-c",
			script,
			text.slice(0, 400)
		], {
			encoding: "utf8",
			env: {
				...process.env,
				PYTHONIOENCODING: "utf-8"
			},
			timeout: 2e4,
			windowsHide: true
		});
		const phonemes = stdout.trim();
		if (!phonemes) throw new PhonemizerUnavailable("没有返回音素");
		return phonemes;
	} catch (error) {
		if (error instanceof PhonemizerUnavailable) throw error;
		throw new PhonemizerUnavailable(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "调用失败");
	}
}
/** Match the installer location, while honoring an explicit user environment first. */
function chinesePythonInterpreter() {
	const explicit = process.env.MURASAME_PYTHON?.trim();
	if (explicit) return explicit;
	if (process.env.VIRTUAL_ENV) {
		const virtual = (0, node_path.join)(process.env.VIRTUAL_ENV, process.platform === "win32" ? "Scripts/python.exe" : "bin/python");
		if ((0, node_fs.existsSync)(virtual)) return virtual;
	}
	if (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA) {
		const embedded = (0, node_path.join)(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA, "Murasame", "python", "python.exe");
		if ((0, node_fs.existsSync)(embedded)) return embedded;
	}
	return "python";
}
var probes = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
/** A real conversion verifies Python, misaki imports, and its Chinese resources.
* Concurrent callers share the probe; failures expire sooner so repair is detected.
*/
function probeChinesePhonemizer() {
	const interpreter = chinesePythonInterpreter();
	const cached = probes.get(interpreter);
	if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) return cached.result;
	const entry = {
		expiresAt: Date.now() + 6e4,
		result: Promise.resolve({
			ready: false,
			detail: ""
		})
	};
	entry.result = phonemizeChinese("你好，准备朗读。", interpreter).then(() => ({
		ready: true,
		detail: "中文音素转换已验证"
	})).catch((error) => {
		entry.expiresAt = Date.now() + 1e4;
		return {
			ready: false,
			detail: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "中文音素转换不可用"
		};
	});
	probes.set(interpreter, entry);
	return entry.result;
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/local/tts.ts
var KOKORO_DIR = "kokoro";
var SAMPLE_RATE = 24e3;
var STYLE_DIM = 256;
var KOKORO_FILES = [
	"config.json",
	"tokenizer.json",
	"tokenizer_config.json",
	(0, node_path.join)("onnx", "model_quantized.onnx")
];
function missingKokoroFiles(userDataDir, voice) {
	const base = (0, node_path.join)(modelsRoot(userDataDir), KOKORO_DIR);
	const missing = KOKORO_FILES.filter((file) => !(0, node_fs.existsSync)((0, node_path.join)(base, file)));
	if (!(0, node_fs.existsSync)((0, node_path.join)(base, "voices", `${voice}.bin`))) missing.push(`voices/${voice}.bin`);
	return missing;
}
var loaded = null;
async function load(userDataDir) {
	if (loaded) return loaded;
	await configureLocalModels(userDataDir);
	const { AutoTokenizer, StyleTextToSpeech2Model } = await import("@huggingface/transformers");
	loaded = {
		tokenizer: await AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(KOKORO_DIR),
		model: await StyleTextToSpeech2Model.from_pretrained(KOKORO_DIR, {
			dtype: "q8",
			device: "cpu"
		})
	};
	return loaded;
}
async function synthesizeChinese(userDataDir, text, voice = "zf_xiaoxiao") {
	const trimmed = text.trim();
	if (!trimmed) return {
		audio: null,
		sampleRate: SAMPLE_RATE,
		played: false,
		message: "没有可朗读的内容"
	};
	const missing = missingKokoroFiles(userDataDir, voice);
	if (missing.length > 0) return {
		audio: null,
		sampleRate: SAMPLE_RATE,
		played: false,
		message: `本地语音合成模型缺少 ${missing.join("、")}，已保留文字回复。`
	};
	try {
		const phonemes = await phonemizeChinese(trimmed);
		const { tokenizer, model } = await load(userDataDir);
		const { input_ids } = tokenizer(phonemes, { truncation: true });
		const tokenCount = Number(input_ids.dims.at(-1)) - 2;
		const voiceBuffer = (0, node_fs.readFileSync)((0, node_path.join)(modelsRoot(userDataDir), KOKORO_DIR, "voices", `${voice}.bin`));
		const offset = voiceBuffer.byteOffset + tokenCount * STYLE_DIM * 4;
		if (offset + STYLE_DIM * 4 > voiceBuffer.byteOffset + voiceBuffer.length) return {
			audio: null,
			sampleRate: SAMPLE_RATE,
			played: false,
			message: "这段文字超出音色数据范围，已保留文字回复。"
		};
		const style = Float32Array.from(new Float32Array(voiceBuffer.buffer, offset, STYLE_DIM));
		const { Tensor } = await import("@huggingface/transformers");
		return {
			audio: (await model({
				input_ids,
				style: new Tensor("float32", style, [1, STYLE_DIM]),
				speed: new Tensor("float32", [1], [1])
			})).waveform.data,
			sampleRate: SAMPLE_RATE,
			played: true,
			message: "本地合成完成"
		};
	} catch (error) {
		return {
			audio: null,
			sampleRate: SAMPLE_RATE,
			played: false,
			message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "本地语音合成失败，已保留文字回复。"
		};
	}
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/core/services/voice-service.ts
var VoiceService = class {
	userDataDir;
	constructor(userDataDir) {
		this.userDataDir = userDataDir;
	}
	async readiness(voice = "zf_xiaoxiao") {
		const stt = missingWhisperFiles(this.userDataDir);
		const tts = missingKokoroFiles(this.userDataDir, voice);
		const parts = [];
		if (stt.length > 0) parts.push(`识别缺少 ${stt.length} 个文件`);
		if (tts.length > 0) parts.push(`合成缺少 ${tts.length} 个文件`);
		const phonemes = tts.length === 0 ? await probeChinesePhonemizer() : {
			ready: false,
			detail: ""
		};
		if (phonemes.detail) parts.push(phonemes.detail);
		if (stt.length === 0) parts.push("识别模型文件齐全，麦克风及识别效果尚待录音测试");
		if (tts.length === 0 && phonemes.ready) parts.push("合成依赖已验证，模型运行与扬声器效果尚待试听");
		return {
			stt: stt.length === 0,
			tts: tts.length === 0 && phonemes.ready,
			detail: `${parts.join("；")}。语音始终使用本地环境。`
		};
	}
	transcribe(audio, microphoneAllowed) {
		if (!microphoneAllowed) return Promise.resolve({
			text: "",
			accepted: false,
			message: "录音授权被拒绝。文字输入仍然可用，这次没有生成内容。"
		});
		return transcribeAudio(this.userDataDir, audio);
	}
	speak(text, voice = "zf_xiaoxiao") {
		return synthesizeChinese(this.userDataDir, text, voice);
	}
};
//#endregion
//#region src/main/local/foreground.ts
function foregroundHint(input) {
	if (input.fullscreen) return {
		busy: true,
		reason: "前台是全屏应用"
	};
	if (input.typedWithinMs < 3e4) return {
		busy: true,
		reason: "30 秒内有键盘输入"
	};
	if (input.idleMs < 6e4) return {
		busy: false,
		reason: input.title
	};
	return {
		busy: false,
		reason: `空闲 ${Math.round(input.idleMs / 1e3)} 秒，前台是 ${input.title}`
	};
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/core/services/proactive-service.ts
var paceFloor = {
	low: 40,
	mid: 20,
	high: 10
};
function proactiveDecision(input) {
	if (input.hidden) return {
		speak: false,
		reason: "桌宠已隐藏，不主动开口"
	};
	if (input.doNotDisturb) return {
		speak: false,
		reason: "勿扰模式中"
	};
	if (input.taskRunning) return {
		speak: false,
		reason: "任务还在执行"
	};
	const front = foregroundHint(input);
	if (front.busy) return {
		speak: false,
		reason: front.reason
	};
	if (input.minutesSinceLast < paceFloor[input.pace]) return {
		speak: false,
		reason: "还没到主动开口的间隔"
	};
	if (input.spokenThisHour >= 2 || input.spokenToday >= 12) return {
		speak: false,
		reason: "已到频率上限"
	};
	if (input.reasons.length === 0) return {
		speak: false,
		reason: "没有值得开口的理由"
	};
	return {
		speak: true,
		reason: input.reasons[0]
	};
}
var ProactiveService = class {
	decide(input) {
		return proactiveDecision(input);
	}
};
//#endregion
//#region src/main/agent/approval-gate.ts
function approvalExpired(requestedAt, now, limitMs = 6e5) {
	return now - requestedAt >= limitMs;
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/agent/planner.ts
function recognizeIntent(text, confirmed) {
	if (!/整理|移动|删除|写入|创建任务|修复/.test(text)) return {
		kind: "chat",
		confirmed: false,
		execute: false,
		question: null
	};
	if (text.trim().length < 4) return {
		kind: "task",
		confirmed: false,
		execute: false,
		question: "要处理哪个目录或文件？"
	};
	if (confirmed !== true) return {
		kind: "task",
		confirmed: false,
		execute: false,
		question: "要创建这个任务吗？未确认前不会执行。"
	};
	return {
		kind: "task",
		confirmed: true,
		execute: true,
		question: null
	};
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/agent/verifier.ts
function judgeCall(executionStatus, verdict, raw) {
	if (executionStatus === "aborted") return "已取消";
	if (executionStatus !== "ok") return "失败";
	if (raw == null || raw === "") return "失败";
	if (verdict === "confirmed") return "成功";
	if (verdict === "refuted") return "失败";
	return "部分完成";
}
function summarizeCalls(calls) {
	calls = calls.filter((call) => !call.agentRole || call.agentRole === "Worker");
	if (!calls.length) return "失败";
	const judgements = calls.map((call) => judgeCall(call.execution.status, call.verification.verdict, call.execution.raw));
	if (!calls.some((call) => call.verification.evidence.some((e) => e.matched)) && calls.some((call) => call.agentRole)) return judgements.includes("已取消") ? "已取消" : "失败";
	if (judgements.every((item) => item === "成功")) return "成功";
	if (judgements.some((item) => item === "已取消")) return "已取消";
	if (judgements.some((item) => item === "部分完成" || item === "成功") && judgements.some((item) => item !== "成功")) return "部分完成";
	return "失败";
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/agent/state-machine.ts
var allowed = {
	待确认: ["已确认", "已取消"],
	已确认: ["规划中", "已取消"],
	规划中: [
		"执行中",
		"失败",
		"已取消"
	],
	执行中: [
		"等待批准",
		"正在停止",
		"已完成",
		"部分完成",
		"失败",
		"已中断"
	],
	等待批准: ["执行中", "已取消"],
	正在停止: ["已取消"],
	已完成: [],
	部分完成: [],
	失败: [],
	已取消: [],
	已中断: []
};
function transition(from, to) {
	if (!allowed[from].includes(to)) throw new Error(`非法状态迁移：${from} → ${to}`);
	return to;
}
var terminal = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([
	"已完成",
	"部分完成",
	"失败",
	"已取消",
	"已中断"
]);
function interruptOnRestart(status) {
	if (terminal.has(status)) return status;
	return "已中断";
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/core/services/task-service.ts
function notifyTask(task) {
	if (!getSettings().taskNotifications) return;
	if (![
		"已完成",
		"失败",
		"部分完成",
		"已取消"
	].includes(task.status)) return;
	if (!electron.Notification.isSupported()) return;
	new electron.Notification({
		title: `任务${task.status}`,
		body: (task.result || task.title).slice(0, 180)
	}).show();
}
var ok$1 = (data) => ({
	ok: true,
	data
});
var fail$1 = (error) => ({
	ok: false,
	error
});
function bindingFingerprint(model, salt) {
	return (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(salt).update(JSON.stringify({
		id: model.id,
		provider: model.provider,
		endpoint: model.endpoint,
		model: model.model,
		apiKey: model.apiKey
	})).digest("hex");
}
function snapshotModel(model) {
	const bindingSalt = (0, node_crypto.randomUUID)();
	return {
		id: model.id,
		model: model.model,
		name: model.name,
		provider: model.provider,
		endpoint: model.endpoint ? (() => {
			try {
				const url = new URL(model.endpoint);
				url.username = "";
				url.password = "";
				url.search = "";
				url.hash = "";
				return url.href;
			} catch {
				return;
			}
		})() : void 0,
		bindingSalt,
		bindingFingerprint: bindingFingerprint(model, bindingSalt)
	};
}
function confirmedDirectory(path) {
	const canonical = normalizeTaskRoot(path);
	try {
		if (!(0, node_fs.statSync)(canonical).isDirectory()) throw new Error("所选对象不是目录");
	} catch (error) {
		throw new Error("工作目录不存在、不可访问或不是目录；请重新选择实际目录", { cause: error });
	}
	return canonical;
}
var TaskService = class {
	db;
	modelSnapshotProvider;
	modelResolver;
	modelBindings = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	planner = null;
	summaryGenerator;
	toolGate = null;
	changed = null;
	plans = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	running = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	isolatedRunner;
	executionGuards = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	pending = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	constructor(db) {
		this.db = db;
		this.db?.exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS task_execution_plans(task_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, plan_json TEXT NOT NULL)");
		this.db?.exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS task_execution_checkpoints(task_id TEXT NOT NULL, step_id TEXT NOT NULL, checkpoint_json TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(task_id,step_id))");
		this.db?.exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS task_model_snapshots(task_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL)");
		this.db?.exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS task_teammate_requests(task_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES tasks(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, request_json TEXT NOT NULL)");
		this.db?.exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS task_grounded_checkpoints(task_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES tasks(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, checkpoint_json TEXT NOT NULL)");
		this.recover();
	}
	setModelSnapshotProvider(provider, resolver) {
		this.modelSnapshotProvider = provider;
		this.modelResolver = resolver;
	}
	boundModel(id) {
		if (this.modelBindings.has(id)) return structuredClone(this.modelBindings.get(id) ?? null);
		const row = this.db?.prepare("SELECT snapshot_json FROM task_model_snapshots WHERE task_id = ?").get(id);
		if (!row) {
			if (this.read(id).model_profile_id) throw new Error("任务缺少原模型绑定证据，未重新规划；请检查原配置或创建新任务");
			this.modelBindings.set(id, null);
			return null;
		}
		const snapshot = JSON.parse(row.snapshot_json);
		const resolved = this.modelResolver?.(snapshot.id);
		if (!resolved || !snapshot.bindingSalt || !snapshot.bindingFingerprint || bindingFingerprint(resolved, snapshot.bindingSalt) !== snapshot.bindingFingerprint) throw new Error("任务原模型配置已变更、被删除或无法核对，未改用当前模型；原任务已保留，请恢复原配置或创建新任务");
		const binding = structuredClone(resolved);
		this.modelBindings.set(id, binding);
		return structuredClone(binding);
	}
	rememberImageRequest(id, request) {
		if (request && typeof request === "object" && "tool" in request && request.tool === "copy-image") this.db?.prepare("INSERT INTO task_teammate_requests(task_id, request_json) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(task_id) DO NOTHING").run(id, JSON.stringify(request));
	}
	setIsolatedRunner(runner) {
		this.isolatedRunner = runner;
	}
	/** 返回不能用的原因；返回 null 表示这个工具当前已启用。 */
	setToolGate(gate) {
		this.toolGate = gate;
	}
	/** 后台执行的每一步进度、结束结果都从这里推出去。 */
	onChange(listener) {
		this.changed = listener;
	}
	/** 等后台执行结束。 */
	settled(id) {
		return this.pending.get(id) ?? Promise.resolve();
	}
	emit(id) {
		const task = this.require(id);
		this.changed?.(task);
		return task;
	}
	/** 有已验证的模型时由模型出计划；没有时 planner 返回 null，执行器按规则处理并如实标注。 */
	setPlanner(planner) {
		this.planner = planner;
	}
	setSummaryGenerator(generator) {
		this.summaryGenerator = generator;
	}
	groundedOptions(id) {
		if (!this.getPlan(id)?.workflow) return {};
		return {
			summaryGenerator: this.summaryGenerator,
			summaryModel: this.boundModel(id),
			groundedCheckpoint: this.groundedCheckpoint(id),
			onGroundedCheckpoint: (checkpoint) => {
				this.db?.prepare("INSERT INTO task_grounded_checkpoints(task_id, checkpoint_json) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(task_id) DO UPDATE SET checkpoint_json = excluded.checkpoint_json").run(id, JSON.stringify(checkpoint));
			}
		};
	}
	groundedCheckpoint(id) {
		const row = this.db?.prepare("SELECT checkpoint_json FROM task_grounded_checkpoints WHERE task_id = ?").get(id);
		return row ? JSON.parse(row.checkpoint_json) : void 0;
	}
	groundedModelGuard(id) {
		if (!this.getPlan(id)?.workflow || !this.modelResolver) return null;
		const row = this.db?.prepare("SELECT snapshot_json FROM task_model_snapshots WHERE task_id = ?").get(id);
		if (!row) return "摘要任务缺少原模型绑定证据，需重新确认";
		const snapshot = JSON.parse(row.snapshot_json);
		const model = this.modelResolver(snapshot.id);
		return !model || !snapshot.bindingSalt || bindingFingerprint(model, snapshot.bindingSalt) !== snapshot.bindingFingerprint ? "任务原模型配置已变更或被删除，未向其他服务发送来源；请恢复原配置或新建任务" : null;
	}
	async plan(title, model) {
		if (!this.planner) return null;
		try {
			return await this.planner(title, structuredClone(model));
		} catch (error) {
			throw new Error(`计划生成失败，没有执行：${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`, { cause: error });
		}
	}
	list() {
		if (!this.db) return [];
		try {
			return this.db.prepare(`SELECT id, title, status, tone, progress, model_profile_id, scope, summary, result, approval_json, artifacts_json, created_at FROM tasks ORDER BY updated_at DESC`).all().map((row) => this.toTask(row));
		} catch {
			return [];
		}
	}
	/** 判定只认证据：把执行器产出的 ToolCallRecord 交给 Verifier 汇总。 */
	judge(outcome) {
		const verdict = summarizeCalls(outcome.calls.map((call) => ({
			agentRole: call.agentRole,
			execution: {
				status: call.executionStatus,
				raw: call.raw,
				startedAt: "",
				endedAt: ""
			},
			verification: {
				method: call.evidence[0]?.kind ?? "fs-stat",
				evidence: call.evidence,
				verdict: call.verdict
			}
		})));
		if (verdict === "成功") return "已完成";
		if (verdict === "已取消") return "已取消";
		if (verdict === "部分完成") return "部分完成";
		return "失败";
	}
	async create(title, selectedRoot) {
		if (!this.db) return fail$1("任务存储不可用");
		const intent = recognizeIntent(title, true);
		if (intent.question) return fail$1(intent.question);
		let root;
		try {
			root = selectedRoot !== void 0 ? confirmedDirectory(selectedRoot) : extractRoot(title);
		} catch (error) {
			return fail$1(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "目录无法核对");
		}
		const selected = structuredClone(this.modelSnapshotProvider?.() ?? null);
		const snapshot = selected ? snapshotModel(selected) : null;
		let plan;
		const plannerGoal = selectedRoot !== void 0 && root ? `${title}\n用户明确确认的工作目录："${root}"；所有文件操作限定在该目录内。` : title;
		try {
			plan = await this.plan(plannerGoal, selected) ?? localRulePlan(title);
		} catch (error) {
			return fail$1(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
		}
		const id = `task-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`;
		let planned;
		try {
			planned = plan.root?.trim() ? normalizeTaskRoot(plan.root) : null;
			if (planned && root && !taskRootsEqual(planned, root)) return fail$1("计划目录与用户指定目录不一致，需重新确认");
			if (root && plan.tool !== "browser") root = confirmedDirectory(root);
			if (planned) plan.root = planned;
		} catch (error) {
			return fail$1(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "计划目录无法核对");
		}
		const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
		this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO tasks(id, title, status, tone, progress, scope, summary, result, artifacts_json, created_at, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, '待确认', 'blue', 0, ?, '', '', '[]', ?, ?)`).run(id, title, root ?? "", now, now);
		this.modelBindings.set(id, selected);
		if (snapshot) {
			this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO task_model_snapshots(task_id, snapshot_json) VALUES (?, ?)").run(id, JSON.stringify(snapshot));
			this.db.prepare("UPDATE tasks SET model_profile_id = ? WHERE id = ?").run(snapshot.id, id);
		}
		if (plan) try {
			this.savePlan(id, plan);
		} catch (error) {
			this.touch(id, error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error), "计划产物范围需要确认");
			return ok$1(this.emit(id));
		}
		const blocked = plan ? this.toolGate?.(plan.tool) : null;
		if (blocked) {
			this.touch(id, `任务受阻：${blocked}`, "需要的工具已停用，没有执行");
			return ok$1(this.emit(id));
		}
		const question = plan?.question.trim();
		if (question && question !== "无") {
			this.touch(id, `${question} 回答之前不会执行。`, "计划还缺信息");
			return ok$1(this.emit(id));
		}
		if (!root && (plan?.tool ?? chooseTool(title)) !== "browser") {
			this.touch(id, planned ? `计划建议的工作目录是 ${planned}。请使用“选择目录并继续”确认实际位置；确认前不会读取文件内容或执行操作。` : "要处理哪个目录或文件？请使用“选择目录并继续”确认实际位置；确认前不会执行。", "还缺少明确确认的目录");
			return ok$1(this.emit(id));
		}
		this.move(id, "已确认");
		this.move(id, "规划中");
		const preview = await executeGoal({
			goal: title,
			root,
			approved: false,
			cancelled: false,
			plan: this.getPlan(id) ?? plan,
			...this.groundedOptions(id)
		});
		if (preview.status === "等待批准") {
			this.move(id, "执行中");
			this.move(id, "等待批准");
			this.saveOutcome(id, preview);
			return ok$1(this.emit(id));
		}
		if (preview.status === "待确认") {
			this.db.prepare("UPDATE tasks SET status = ? WHERE id = ?").run("待确认", id);
			this.touch(id, preview.result, preview.summary);
			return ok$1(this.emit(id));
		}
		this.move(id, "执行中");
		this.move(id, this.judge(preview));
		this.saveOutcome(id, preview);
		notifyTask(this.require(id));
		return ok$1(this.emit(id));
	}
	/** 队友直接记下的任务。状态按结果写入，不经过整理文件的计划器。 */
	openTeammateTask(input) {
		if (!this.db) throw new Error("任务存储不可用");
		const id = `task-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`;
		const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
		this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO tasks(id, title, status, tone, progress, scope, summary, result, approval_json, artifacts_json, created_at, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`).run(id, input.title, input.status, toneOf(input.status), input.status === "已完成" ? 100 : Math.max(0, Math.min(99, input.progress ?? 0)), input.scope ?? "", input.summary, input.result, input.approval ? JSON.stringify(input.approval) : null, JSON.stringify(input.artifacts ?? []), now, now);
		this.rememberImageRequest(id, input.approval);
		return this.emit(id);
	}
	setCopyImage(handler) {
		this.copyImage = handler;
	}
	copyImage;
	/** 批准后立即返回「执行中」，执行在后台按文件推进，进度和结果经 onChange 推送。 */
	approve(id, scope = "once") {
		const current = this.read(id);
		if (current.status !== "等待批准") return Promise.resolve(fail$1("当前没有待批准的操作"));
		const approval = current.approval_json ? JSON.parse(current.approval_json) : {};
		this.rememberImageRequest(id, approval);
		const gateTool = approval.tool === "copy-image" ? "filesystem" : approval.tool ?? this.getPlan(id)?.tool ?? "filesystem";
		const gateReason = this.toolGate?.(gateTool);
		if (gateReason) {
			this.touch(id, `任务受阻：${gateReason}`, "工具已停用");
			return Promise.resolve(ok$1(this.emit(id)));
		}
		if (typeof approval.requestedAt !== "number" || approvalExpired(approval.requestedAt, Date.now())) {
			this.db?.prepare("UPDATE tasks SET status = ?, approval_json = NULL, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").run("待确认", (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
			this.touch(id, "审批无效或已过期，没有执行；请核对并继续以重新生成审批。", "审批需要更新");
			return Promise.resolve(ok$1(this.emit(id)));
		}
		if (approval.tool === "copy-image") this.appendCall(id, {
			agentRole: "Planner",
			toolId: "approval",
			declared: "批准本次准确图片目标",
			executionStatus: "ok",
			raw: JSON.stringify({
				taskId: id,
				scope,
				approval
			}),
			evidence: [],
			verdict: "confirmed",
			stepId: "approval",
			traceId: (0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()
		});
		if (approval.tool === "copy-image") return this.copyApprovedImage(id, approval);
		const tool = approval.tool ?? this.getPlan(id)?.tool ?? "filesystem";
		const blocked = this.toolGate?.(tool);
		if (blocked) {
			this.touch(id, `任务受阻：${blocked}`, "需要的工具已停用，没有执行");
			return Promise.resolve(ok$1(this.emit(id)));
		}
		this.move(id, "执行中");
		this.appendCall(id, {
			agentRole: "Planner",
			toolId: "approval",
			declared: `批准范围：${scope === "once" ? "本次一项操作" : "本任务此版本计划"}`,
			executionStatus: "ok",
			raw: JSON.stringify({
				taskId: id,
				scope,
				root: current.scope,
				approvedAt: Date.now(),
				requestedAt: approval.requestedAt,
				plan: this.getPlan(id)
			}),
			evidence: [],
			verdict: "confirmed",
			stepId: "approval",
			traceId: (0, node_crypto.randomUUID)(),
			args: {
				scope,
				taskId: id,
				root: current.scope
			}
		});
		const control = {
			stop: false,
			controller: new AbortController()
		};
		const approvedPlan = JSON.stringify(this.getPlan(id) ?? null);
		const approvedAt = approval.requestedAt;
		this.executionGuards.set(id, () => approvalExpired(approvedAt, Date.now()) ? "批准已过期，未开始后续操作" : approvedPlan !== JSON.stringify(this.getPlan(id) ?? null) ? "计划变更，需重新批准" : this.groundedModelGuard(id));
		this.running.set(id, control);
		this.db?.prepare(`UPDATE tasks SET approval_json = NULL, summary = ?, result = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`).run("执行中", "正在执行，可以随时取消。取消后不会再移动新文件。", (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
		this.pending.set(id, this.run(id, current, control, scope, approval.impact));
		return Promise.resolve(ok$1(this.emit(id)));
	}
	async copyApprovedImage(id, approval) {
		if (typeof approval.requestedAt !== "number" || approvalExpired(approval.requestedAt, Date.now())) {
			this.touch(id, "任务受阻：审批无效或已超过 10 分钟，未复制。", "需要重新确认");
			return ok$1(this.emit(id));
		}
		this.move(id, "执行中");
		const control = {
			stop: false,
			controller: new AbortController()
		};
		this.running.set(id, control);
		const job = (async () => {
			const startedAt = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
			const traceId = (0, node_crypto.randomUUID)();
			this.appendCall(id, {
				agentRole: "Worker",
				toolId: "filesystem",
				declared: "开始本次已批准图片复制；结果尚未知",
				executionStatus: "error",
				raw: "开始复制，结果尚未知；恢复时先核对，不能重放",
				evidence: [],
				verdict: "inconclusive",
				stepId: "copy-image",
				traceId,
				phase: "started",
				startedAt,
				args: {
					hostPath: approval.hostPath,
					containerPath: approval.containerPath
				}
			});
			let knownOutcome = true;
			let copiedPath;
			try {
				if (!this.copyImage || !approval.hostPath) throw new Error("没有可核对的图片路径");
				const blocked = this.toolGate?.("filesystem");
				if (blocked) throw new Error(blocked);
				const copied = await this.copyImage({
					...approval,
					signal: control.controller.signal,
					taskId: id
				});
				if (copied.path) {
					const intended = resolveInside(approval.hostPath, ".");
					if (resolveInside(intended, copied.path) === intended) {
						copiedPath = intended;
						this.db?.prepare("UPDATE tasks SET artifacts_json = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").run(JSON.stringify([intended]), (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
					}
				}
				if (!copied.ok) {
					knownOutcome = copied.outcomeKnown ?? !/清理失败|停止尚未核实/.test(copied.reason);
					throw new Error(copied.reason);
				}
				copiedPath = resolveInside(approval.hostPath, ".");
				if (resolveInside(copiedPath, copied.path) !== copiedPath) throw new Error("实际复制位置不符合本次批准目标");
				const stat = (0, node_fs.statSync)(copiedPath);
				if (!stat.isFile() || stat.size <= 0 || stat.size !== copied.bytes) throw new Error("复制结果大小或类型核验失败");
				const hash = (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update((0, node_fs.readFileSync)(copiedPath)).digest("hex");
				if (copied.hash && copied.hash !== hash || copied.sourceHash && copied.sourceHash !== hash) throw new Error("图片原件与导出目标哈希不一致");
				this.db?.prepare("UPDATE tasks SET artifacts_json = ?, progress = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").run(JSON.stringify([copiedPath]), 100, (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
				this.appendCall(id, {
					agentRole: "Worker",
					toolId: "filesystem",
					declared: "按批准目标复制图片",
					executionStatus: control.stop ? "aborted" : "ok",
					raw: `${copiedPath} (${stat.size} bytes)`,
					evidence: [{
						kind: "fs-stat",
						target: copiedPath,
						expected: `${copied.bytes} bytes`,
						actual: `${stat.size} bytes`,
						matched: true
					}, {
						kind: "fs-hash",
						target: copiedPath,
						expected: copied.sourceHash ?? copied.hash ?? hash,
						actual: hash,
						matched: true
					}],
					verdict: "confirmed",
					stepId: "copy-image",
					traceId,
					phase: "result",
					args: {
						containerPath: approval.containerPath,
						hostPath: approval.hostPath,
						outcomeKnown: true,
						sourceHash: copied.sourceHash,
						procedureId: copied.procedureId
					},
					startedAt,
					endedAt: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()
				});
				this.move(id, control.stop ? "已取消" : "已完成");
				this.touch(id, `${control.stop ? "取消前已复制" : "已复制"} ${copiedPath}，${stat.size} 字节。${copied.note ?? ""}`, control.stop ? "已取消，保留实际产物" : "图片已核验");
			} catch (error) {
				const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
				this.appendCall(id, {
					agentRole: "Worker",
					toolId: "filesystem",
					declared: "复制图片",
					executionStatus: control.stop ? "aborted" : "error",
					raw: `${message}${copiedPath ? `；可能已写入 ${copiedPath}` : ""}`,
					evidence: [],
					verdict: "refuted",
					stepId: "copy-image",
					traceId,
					phase: "result",
					args: {
						hostPath: approval.hostPath,
						containerPath: approval.containerPath,
						outcomeKnown: knownOutcome
					},
					startedAt,
					endedAt: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()
				});
				this.move(id, control.stop ? "已取消" : "失败");
				this.touch(id, message, "复制未通过核验");
			} finally {
				this.running.delete(id);
				this.pending.delete(id);
				notifyTask(this.emit(id));
			}
		})();
		this.pending.set(id, job);
		job.finally(() => this.pending.delete(id));
		return ok$1(this.emit(id));
	}
	async run(id, current, control, scope, approvedImpact) {
		try {
			const done = await executeGoal({
				goal: current.title,
				root: current.scope || null,
				approved: true,
				cancelled: false,
				plan: this.getPlan(id) ?? null,
				...this.groundedOptions(id),
				approvedGroundedImpact: approvedImpact,
				runner: this.isolatedRunner,
				taskId: id,
				approvalScope: scope,
				checkpoints: this.checkpoints(id),
				onCheckpoint: (checkpoint) => {
					this.db?.prepare("INSERT INTO task_execution_checkpoints(task_id, step_id, checkpoint_json) VALUES (?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(task_id,step_id) DO UPDATE SET checkpoint_json = excluded.checkpoint_json").run(id, checkpoint.stepId, JSON.stringify(checkpoint));
				},
				toolGate: (tool) => this.toolGate?.(tool) ?? null,
				executionGuard: this.executionGuards.get(id),
				onCall: (call) => {
					this.appendCall(id, call);
					if (call.agentRole !== "Worker" || !call.stepId) return;
					const running = call.phase === "started";
					const completed = !running && call.verdict === "confirmed";
					this.db?.prepare("UPDATE task_steps SET state = ?, tone = ?, detail = ? WHERE task_id = ? AND id = ?").run(running ? "进行中" : completed ? "完成" : "待执行", running ? "blue" : completed ? "green" : "red", running ? `${call.declared || "正在执行"}；结果尚未返回。` : call.raw, id, `${id}-${call.stepId}`);
					this.db?.prepare("UPDATE tasks SET progress = (SELECT 100.0 * SUM(CASE WHEN state = '完成' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) / MAX(COUNT(*), 1) FROM task_steps WHERE task_id = ?), updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").run(id, (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
					this.emit(id);
				},
				control: {
					stopped: () => control.stop,
					signal: control.controller.signal,
					effect: (effect) => {
						const task = this.read(id);
						const artifacts = JSON.parse(task.artifacts_json ?? "[]");
						if (effect.status === "moved") artifacts.push(effect.destination);
						this.db?.prepare("UPDATE tasks SET artifacts_json = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").run(JSON.stringify([...new Set(artifacts)]), (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
						this.appendCall(id, {
							agentRole: "Worker",
							toolId: "filesystem.effect",
							declared: "逐文件实际影响",
							executionStatus: effect.status === "moved" ? "ok" : "error",
							raw: JSON.stringify(effect),
							evidence: [],
							verdict: "inconclusive",
							stepId: `effect-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`,
							args: effect
						});
					},
					progress: (count, total, note) => {
						this.db?.prepare(`UPDATE tasks SET progress = ?, summary = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`).run(Math.round(count / Math.max(total, 1) * 100), `已处理 ${count}/${total}：${note}`, (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
						this.emit(id);
					}
				}
			});
			this.move(id, control.stop ? "已取消" : done.status);
			this.saveOutcome(id, done);
		} catch (error) {
			const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "执行出错";
			this.move(id, control.stop ? "已取消" : "失败");
			this.touch(id, `执行中断：${message}。已经移动的文件保持原样。`, "执行出错");
		} finally {
			this.running.delete(id);
			this.executionGuards.delete(id);
			this.pending.delete(id);
			notifyTask(this.emit(id));
		}
	}
	deny(id) {
		const current = this.read(id);
		if (current.status !== "等待批准") return Promise.resolve(fail$1("当前没有待批准的操作"));
		if (current.approval_json) this.rememberImageRequest(id, JSON.parse(current.approval_json));
		this.db?.prepare("UPDATE tasks SET status = ?, approval_json = NULL, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").run("待确认", (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
		this.touch(id, "任务受阻：敏感操作未获批准，没有执行。可以补充要求后重新确认。", "未批准，未执行");
		return Promise.resolve(ok$1(this.emit(id)));
	}
	cancel(id) {
		const control = this.running.get(id);
		if (control) {
			control.stop = true;
			control.controller.abort();
			if (this.read(id).status === "执行中") this.move(id, "正在停止");
			this.touch(id, "正在停止：手上这个文件处理完就停，不再移动新文件。", "正在停止");
			return Promise.resolve(ok$1(this.emit(id)));
		}
		const current = this.read(id);
		if ([
			"已完成",
			"失败",
			"部分完成",
			"已取消",
			"已中断"
		].includes(current.status)) return Promise.resolve(ok$1(this.emit(id)));
		if (current.status === "执行中") this.move(id, "正在停止");
		const latest = this.read(id);
		this.move(latest.status === "正在停止" || latest.status === "等待批准" || latest.status === "待确认" || latest.status === "已确认" || latest.status === "规划中" ? latest.id : id, "已取消");
		this.touch(id, "任务已取消。进行中的操作已停止安排，已经产生的文件保持原样。", "已停止安排后续操作");
		return Promise.resolve(ok$1(this.emit(id)));
	}
	recover() {
		if (!this.db) return;
		try {
			const rows = this.db.prepare(`SELECT id, status FROM tasks`).all();
			for (const row of rows) {
				const next = interruptOnRestart(row.status);
				if (next !== row.status) {
					this.db.prepare(`UPDATE tasks SET status = ?, result = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`).run(next, "已中断，待用户决定。不会自动重做已产生副作用的步骤。", (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), row.id);
					this.db.prepare("UPDATE task_steps SET state = '待核对', tone = 'amber', detail = detail || '；上次执行已中断，实际结果需要核对，尚未恢复执行。' WHERE task_id = ? AND state IN ('进行中', '正在停止')").run(row.id);
				}
			}
		} catch {}
	}
	move(id, to) {
		const next = transition(this.read(id).status, to);
		this.db?.prepare(`UPDATE tasks SET status = ?, tone = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`).run(next, toneOf(next), (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
	}
	touch(id, result, summary) {
		this.db?.prepare(`UPDATE tasks SET result = ?, summary = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`).run(result, summary, (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
	}
	saveOutcome(id, outcome) {
		if (!this.db) return;
		const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
		this.db.prepare(`UPDATE tasks SET summary = ?, result = ?, progress = ?, approval_json = ?, artifacts_json = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`).run(outcome.summary, outcome.result, outcome.steps.filter((step) => step.state === "完成").length / Math.max(outcome.steps.length, 1) * 100, outcome.approval ? JSON.stringify(outcome.approval) : null, JSON.stringify([.../* @__PURE__ */ new Set([...JSON.parse(this.read(id).artifacts_json ?? "[]"), ...outcome.artifacts])]), now, id);
		this.db.prepare(`DELETE FROM task_steps WHERE task_id = ?`).run(id);
		const insertStep = this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO task_steps(id, task_id, title, detail, state, tone, time_label, position) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`);
		outcome.steps.forEach((step, index) => {
			insertStep.run(`${id}-${step.id}`, id, step.title, step.detail, step.state, step.tone, "", index);
		});
		outcome.calls.forEach((item) => this.appendCall(id, item));
	}
	appendCall(id, item) {
		const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
		const snapshot = item.agentRole === "Planner" && item.toolId === "model" ? this.db?.prepare("SELECT snapshot_json FROM task_model_snapshots WHERE task_id = ?").get(id) : void 0;
		const callId = `${id}-${item.traceId ?? item.stepId ?? "work"}-${item.agentRole}-${item.phase ?? "result"}`;
		this.db?.prepare(`INSERT INTO tool_calls(id, task_id, step_id, tool_id, declared_json, execution_json, verification_json, created_at, agent_role) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET execution_json = excluded.execution_json, verification_json = excluded.verification_json`).run(callId, id, `${id}-${item.stepId ?? "work"}`, item.toolId, JSON.stringify({
			intent: item.declared,
			args: {
				...typeof item.args === "object" && item.args ? item.args : {},
				traceId: item.traceId,
				phase: item.phase,
				...snapshot ? { modelSnapshot: JSON.parse(snapshot.snapshot_json) } : {}
			}
		}), JSON.stringify({
			status: item.executionStatus,
			raw: item.raw,
			startedAt: item.startedAt ?? now,
			endedAt: item.endedAt ?? now
		}), JSON.stringify({
			method: "independent",
			evidence: item.evidence,
			verdict: item.verdict
		}), now, item.agentRole);
	}
	savePlan(id, plan, approvedRoot) {
		const root = approvedRoot ?? this.read(id).scope;
		plan = structuredClone(plan);
		for (const step of plan.steps) {
			const tool = step.tool ?? plan.tool;
			if (tool === "filesystem" || Boolean(step.outputs?.length)) {
				const blocked = this.toolGate?.("filesystem");
				if (blocked) throw new Error(blocked);
			}
			if (tool === "filesystem" && (step.args?.action === "organize" || plan.steps.length === 1 && /按类型|按扩展名|归类/.test(step.title))) step.args = {
				...step.args,
				action: "organize",
				...root ? { files: organizeManifest(resolveInside(root, step.workspace ?? ".")) } : {}
			};
		}
		for (const step of plan.steps) for (const output of root ? step.outputs ?? [] : []) {
			const path = resolveInside(resolveInside(root, step.workspace ?? "."), output.path);
			try {
				if (!(0, node_fs.statSync)(path).isFile()) throw new Error("声明的产物目标不是文件");
				output.baselineHash = (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update((0, node_fs.readFileSync)(path)).digest("hex");
			} catch (error) {
				if (error.code === "ENOENT") output.baselineHash = null;
				else throw error;
			}
			const check = step.checks?.find((item) => item.path === output.path && (item.kind === "file-content" || item.kind === "file-hash"));
			if (check?.kind === "file-content" && output.expectedContent === void 0) output.expectedContent = check.value;
			if (check?.kind === "file-hash" && output.expectedHash === void 0) output.expectedHash = check.value;
		}
		this.plans.set(id, structuredClone(plan));
		this.db?.prepare("INSERT INTO task_execution_plans(task_id, plan_json) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(task_id) DO UPDATE SET plan_json = excluded.plan_json").run(id, JSON.stringify(plan));
	}
	getPlan(id) {
		const current = this.plans.get(id);
		if (current) return current;
		const row = this.db?.prepare("SELECT plan_json FROM task_execution_plans WHERE task_id = ?").get(id);
		if (!row) return void 0;
		const plan = JSON.parse(row.plan_json);
		this.plans.set(id, plan);
		return plan;
	}
	checkpoints(id) {
		return (this.db?.prepare("SELECT checkpoint_json FROM task_execution_checkpoints WHERE task_id = ?").all(id) ?? []).map((row) => JSON.parse(row.checkpoint_json));
	}
	async continueTask(id, answer) {
		const current = this.read(id);
		if (![
			"待确认",
			"已中断",
			"失败",
			"部分完成",
			"已取消"
		].includes(current.status)) return fail$1("当前任务不在可补充或恢复的状态");
		const checkpoints = this.checkpoints(id);
		const grounded = Boolean(this.getPlan(id)?.workflow);
		const resumeRequested = /^\s*(继续|恢复|resume|continue)\s*$/i.test(answer);
		const priorSummary = this.groundedCheckpoint(id);
		if (priorSummary && !priorSummary.outcomeKnown && (!grounded || !resumeRequested)) return fail$1("上次摘要写入的实际结果尚未核对，不能替换原计划；请先使用“核对并继续”核对原目标，之后再新建其他任务。");
		if (this.require(id).toolCalls.some((call) => {
			if (call.agentRole !== "Worker" || call.toolId === "filesystem.effect") return false;
			if (grounded && (call.toolId === "model.summary" || call.stepId === `${id}-summary-save`)) return false;
			const args = call.declared.args;
			return args.phase === "started" ? !checkpoints.some((checkpoint) => checkpoint.traceId === args.traceId && checkpoint.outcomeKnown) && !this.require(id).toolCalls.some((result) => {
				const final = result.declared.args;
				return final.traceId === args.traceId && final.phase === "result" && final.outcomeKnown === true;
			}) : !args.traceId && checkpoints.length === 0;
		}) || checkpoints.some((checkpoint) => !checkpoint.outcomeKnown)) return fail$1("仍有实际副作用未知的调用，需先核对结果；不会重放");
		const imageRow = this.db?.prepare("SELECT request_json FROM task_teammate_requests WHERE task_id = ?").get(id);
		if (imageRow) {
			if (!/^\s*(继续|恢复|resume|continue)\s*$/i.test(answer)) return fail$1("此任务是固定目标的图片请求。可核对并继续；若要改变画面或保存位置，请新建请求。");
			const request = JSON.parse(imageRow.request_json);
			if (!request.hostPath || !(0, node_path.isAbsolute)(request.hostPath) || request.hostPath !== request.target) return fail$1("图片目标无法核对，请重新发起图片请求");
			if ((0, node_fs.existsSync)(request.hostPath)) return fail$1("图片目标已经存在，请先检查已有成果；不会覆盖或重复生成");
			const blocked = this.toolGate?.("filesystem");
			if (blocked) return fail$1(blocked);
			request.requestedAt = Date.now();
			this.db?.prepare("UPDATE tasks SET status = ?, approval_json = ?, summary = ?, result = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").run("等待批准", JSON.stringify(request), "图片请求已重新核对，等待批准", "仍将写入原来确认的目标。批准前不会生成或复制图片。", (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
			return ok$1(this.emit(id));
		}
		const resume = (checkpoints.length > 0 || grounded) && resumeRequested;
		const goal = resumeRequested ? current.title : `${current.title}\n用户补充：${answer}`;
		let plan;
		try {
			plan = resume ? this.getPlan(id) ?? null : await this.plan(goal, this.boundModel(id)) ?? localRulePlan(goal);
		} catch (error) {
			return fail$1(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
		}
		let root;
		try {
			root = extractRoot(answer) ?? (current.scope || null);
			if (root && plan?.root?.trim() && !taskRootsEqual(root, plan.root)) return fail$1("计划目录与本次确认的目录不一致；原计划与授权范围已保留，请重新确认");
			if (root && !resume && plan?.tool !== "browser") root = confirmedDirectory(root);
		} catch (error) {
			return fail$1(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "目录无法核对");
		}
		if (!root && (plan?.tool ?? chooseTool(goal)) !== "browser") return fail$1("仍缺少明确授权目录");
		if (plan && !resume) this.savePlan(id, plan, root ?? "");
		let preview;
		try {
			const blocked = this.groundedModelGuard(id);
			if (blocked) return fail$1(blocked);
			preview = await executeGoal({
				goal,
				root,
				approved: false,
				cancelled: false,
				plan: this.getPlan(id) ?? plan,
				taskId: id,
				checkpoints,
				...this.groundedOptions(id)
			});
		} catch (error) {
			return fail$1(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "摘要恢复失败，未自动重放");
		}
		this.db?.prepare("UPDATE tasks SET title = ?, scope = ?, status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?").run(goal, root ?? "", preview.status, (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString(), id);
		this.saveOutcome(id, preview);
		return ok$1(this.emit(id));
	}
	require(id) {
		return this.toTask(this.read(id));
	}
	read(id) {
		const row = this.db?.prepare(`SELECT id, title, status, tone, progress, model_profile_id, scope, summary, result, approval_json, artifacts_json, created_at FROM tasks WHERE id = ?`).get(id);
		if (!row) throw new Error("任务不存在");
		return row;
	}
	toTask(row) {
		const steps = this.db?.prepare(`SELECT id, title, detail, state, tone, time_label FROM task_steps WHERE task_id = ? ORDER BY position`).all(row.id);
		const calls = this.db?.prepare(`SELECT id, task_id, step_id, tool_id, declared_json, execution_json, verification_json, agent_role FROM tool_calls WHERE task_id = ? ORDER BY created_at`).all(row.id);
		const approval = row.approval_json ? JSON.parse(row.approval_json) : null;
		return {
			id: row.id,
			title: row.title,
			status: row.status,
			tone: toneOf(row.status),
			progress: row.progress,
			created: row.created_at,
			model: row.model_profile_id ?? "",
			modelProfileId: row.model_profile_id,
			scope: row.scope,
			summary: row.summary,
			steps: (steps ?? []).map((step) => ({
				id: step.id,
				title: step.title,
				detail: step.detail,
				state: step.state,
				tone: step.tone,
				time: step.time_label
			})),
			artifacts: row.artifacts_json ? JSON.parse(row.artifacts_json) : [],
			approval: approval && row.status === "等待批准" ? {
				target: approval.target,
				impact: approval.impact,
				tool: approval.tool
			} : null,
			result: row.result,
			toolCalls: (calls ?? []).map((item) => this.toCall(item)),
			verdict: null
		};
	}
	toCall(item) {
		const declared = JSON.parse(item.declared_json);
		const execution = JSON.parse(item.execution_json);
		const verification = JSON.parse(item.verification_json);
		return {
			id: item.id,
			taskId: item.task_id,
			stepId: item.step_id,
			toolId: item.tool_id,
			agentRole: item.agent_role,
			declared,
			execution,
			verification
		};
	}
};
function toneOf(status) {
	if (status === "失败") return "red";
	if (status === "等待批准" || status === "已中断" || status === "部分完成" || status === "正在停止") return "amber";
	if (status === "已完成") return "green";
	if (status === "已取消") return "gray";
	return "blue";
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/tools/types.ts
function availability(tool) {
	if (!tool.enabled && tool.running) return {
		canStart: false,
		runningVisible: true,
		note: "不再接受新调用，进行中的操作仍单独显示"
	};
	if (!tool.enabled) return {
		canStart: false,
		runningVisible: false,
		note: "未启用，不参与执行"
	};
	return {
		canStart: true,
		runningVisible: tool.running,
		note: "可调用"
	};
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/tools/plugin-loader.ts
function readPluginManifest(raw) {
	try {
		const value = JSON.parse(raw);
		if (!value.id || !value.name || !value.entry) return {
			ok: false,
			reason: "插件清单缺少 id、名称或入口文件"
		};
		return {
			ok: true,
			manifest: {
				id: value.id,
				name: value.name,
				ability: value.ability ?? "",
				sensitive: Boolean(value.sensitive),
				entry: value.entry,
				parameters: value.parameters ?? { type: "object" }
			}
		};
	} catch {
		return {
			ok: false,
			reason: "插件清单不是有效的 JSON"
		};
	}
}
function loadPluginDirectory(directory) {
	const plugins = [];
	const errors = [];
	let names;
	try {
		names = (0, node_fs.readdirSync)(directory);
	} catch {
		return {
			plugins,
			errors
		};
	}
	for (const name of names) {
		const folder = (0, node_path.join)(directory, name);
		try {
			if (!(0, node_fs.statSync)(folder).isDirectory()) continue;
			const manifest = readPluginManifest((0, node_fs.readFileSync)((0, node_path.join)(folder, "plugin.json"), "utf8"));
			if (!manifest.ok) {
				errors.push(`${name}: ${manifest.reason}`);
				continue;
			}
			plugins.push(manifest.manifest);
		} catch (error) {
			errors.push(`${name}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "无法读取插件"}`);
		}
	}
	return {
		plugins,
		errors
	};
}
