var seed = [
	{
		id: "filesystem",
		name: "文件系统",
		kind: "工具",
		ability: "读取与写入授权目录",
		parameters: { type: "object" },
		sensitive: true,
		enabled: true,
		running: false
	},
	{
		id: "terminal",
		name: "终端命令",
		kind: "工具",
		ability: "在授权目录中执行命令",
		parameters: { type: "object" },
		sensitive: true,
		enabled: true,
		running: false
	},
	{
		id: "git",
		name: "Git",
		kind: "工具",
		ability: "查看状态与差异",
		parameters: { type: "object" },
		sensitive: false,
		enabled: true,
		running: false
	},
	{
		id: "browser",
		name: "浏览器",
		kind: "插件",
		ability: "读取网页可见内容",
		parameters: { type: "object" },
		sensitive: true,
		enabled: true,
		running: false
	},
	{
		id: "obsidian",
		name: "Obsidian",
		kind: "插件",
		ability: "检索本地知识库",
		parameters: { type: "object" },
		sensitive: false,
		enabled: false,
		running: false
	},
	{
		id: "calendar",
		name: "日历",
		kind: "插件",
		ability: "读取空闲时间",
		parameters: { type: "object" },
		sensitive: false,
		enabled: false,
		running: false
	}
];
var ToolRegistry = class {
	tools;
	constructor(tools = seed.map((tool) => ({ ...tool }))) {
		this.tools = tools;
	}
	list() {
		return this.tools.map((tool) => ({ ...tool }));
	}
	setEnabled(id, enabled) {
		const tool = this.tools.find((item) => item.id === id);
		if (!tool) throw new Error("能力不存在");
		tool.enabled = enabled;
		return { ...tool };
	}
	assertCallable(id) {
		const tool = this.tools.find((item) => item.id === id);
		if (!tool || !availability(tool).canStart) throw new Error("未启用的能力不参与执行");
	}
	register(tool) {
		if (this.tools.some((item) => item.id === tool.id)) return;
		this.tools.push({ ...tool });
	}
};
//#endregion
//#region src/main/core/services/tool-service.ts
var unavailable = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set(["obsidian", "calendar"]);
var ToolService = class {
	db;
	registry = new ToolRegistry();
	constructor(db) {
		this.db = db;
		for (const [id, enabled] of Object.entries(readToolEnabled())) try {
			this.registry.setEnabled(id, enabled);
		} catch {}
	}
	list() {
		return this.registry.list().map((tool) => {
			const state = availability(tool);
			const blocked = unavailable.has(tool.id);
			return {
				id: tool.id,
				name: tool.name,
				kind: tool.kind,
				ability: tool.ability,
				permission: blocked ? "不可用" : tool.enabled ? tool.sensitive ? "需确认" : "可调用" : "已停用",
				enabled: blocked ? false : tool.enabled,
				last: this.lastUsed(tool.id),
				note: blocked ? "尚未实现，不会显示为可用" : tool.running && !tool.enabled ? "不再接受新调用，进行中的操作仍单独显示" : state.note
			};
		});
	}
	loadDirectory(directory) {
		const loaded = loadPluginDirectory(directory);
		for (const plugin of loaded.plugins) this.registry.register({
			id: plugin.id,
			name: plugin.name,
			kind: "插件",
			ability: plugin.ability,
			parameters: plugin.parameters,
			sensitive: plugin.sensitive,
			enabled: false,
			running: false
		});
		return loaded.errors;
	}
	bundledDirectory(appPath) {
		return (0, node_path.join)(appPath, "plugins");
	}
	setEnabled(id, enabled) {
		if (unavailable.has(id)) return Promise.resolve({
			ok: false,
			error: "该能力尚未实现，不能启用"
		});
		try {
			const tool = this.registry.setEnabled(id, enabled);
			writeToolEnabled(id, enabled);
			const listed = this.list().find((item) => item.id === tool.id);
			if (!listed) return Promise.resolve({
				ok: false,
				error: "能力不存在"
			});
			return Promise.resolve({
				ok: true,
				data: listed
			});
		} catch (error) {
			return Promise.resolve({
				ok: false,
				error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "能力不存在"
			});
		}
	}
	assertCallable(id) {
		this.registry.assertCallable(id);
	}
	lastUsed(id) {
		if (!this.db) return "";
		try {
			return this.db.prepare(`SELECT created_at FROM tool_calls WHERE tool_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`).get(id)?.created_at ?? "";
		} catch {
			return "";
		}
	}
};

//#region src/main/isolation/creation.ts
var CREATION_LIMITS = {
	modelCalls: 6,
	milliseconds: 18e4,
	attempts: 2,
	bytes: 524288
};
var creationDraftSchema = zod.z.object({
	title: zod.z.string().trim().min(1).max(120),
	content: zod.z.string().trim().min(1).max(24e3),
	expectedOutput: zod.z.string().max(4e3)
}).strict();
var creationReviewSchema = zod.z.object({
	accepted: zod.z.boolean(),
	reason: zod.z.string().trim().min(1).max(2e3)
}).strict();
var creationEvidenceSchema = zod.z.object({
	name: zod.z.enum(["main.py", "document.odt"]),
	bytes: zod.z.number().int().positive().max(CREATION_LIMITS.bytes),
	sha256: zod.z.string().regex(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/),
	base64: zod.z.string().max(71e4),
	text: zod.z.string().max(3e4),
	stdout: zod.z.string().max(32e3),
	stderr: zod.z.string().max(32e3),
	exitCode: zod.z.number().int(),
	timedOut: zod.z.boolean()
}).strict();
/** End this workflow promptly even if its provider request is still queued.
* The provider receives the same signal and checks it before any later send. */
async function creationRequest(operation, signal) {
	signal.throwIfAborted();
	let cancel;
	const interrupted = new Promise((_resolve, reject) => {
		cancel = () => reject(signal.reason ?? /* @__PURE__ */ new Error("创作已停止"));
		signal.addEventListener("abort", cancel, { once: true });
	});
	try {
		return await Promise.race([operation(), interrupted]);
	} finally {
		signal.removeEventListener("abort", cancel);
	}
}
function verifyCreationEvidence(kind, draft, raw) {
	const item = creationEvidenceSchema.parse(raw);
	const data = Buffer.from(item.base64, "base64");
	if (data.toString("base64") !== item.base64 || data.length !== item.bytes || (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(data).digest("hex") !== item.sha256) throw new Error("实际产物内容或哈希无法核对");
	if (item.timedOut || item.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(`实际运行${item.timedOut ? "超时" : `失败（退出码${item.exitCode}）`}`);
	if (kind === "code") {
		if (item.name !== "main.py" || data.toString("utf8") !== draft.content || item.text !== draft.content) throw new Error("实际代码与候选不一致");
		if (!draft.expectedOutput.trim() || item.stdout.trim() !== draft.expectedOutput.trim()) throw new Error("实际输出与候选声明不一致");
	} else if (item.name !== "document.odt" || data.subarray(0, 4).toString("hex") !== "504b0304" || item.text !== `${draft.title}\n${draft.content.replaceAll("\r\n", "\n")}`) throw new Error("LibreOffice文档回读与候选不一致");
	return item;
}
async function runCreation(containerId, activityId, attempt, kind, draft, run, signal) {
	const raw = await runInContainerAsync(containerId, [
		"python3",
		"-I",
		"/opt/murasame/creation-runner.py"
	], run, {
		signal,
		timeoutMs: 5e4,
		input: JSON.stringify({
			action: "create",
			activityId,
			attempt,
			kind,
			draft: creationDraftSchema.parse(draft)
		})
	});
	return creationEvidenceSchema.parse(JSON.parse(raw));
}
async function readCreation(containerId, activityId, attempt, kind, run, signal) {
	const payload = JSON.stringify({
		action: "read",
		activityId,
		attempt,
		kind
	});
	return creationEvidenceSchema.parse(JSON.parse(await runInContainerAsync(containerId, [
		"python3",
		"-I",
		"/opt/murasame/creation-runner.py"
	], run, {
		signal,
		timeoutMs: 8e3,
		input: payload
	})));
}

//#region src/main/isolation/frame-evidence.ts
function crc32(bytes) {
	let crc = 4294967295;
	for (const byte of bytes) {
		crc ^= byte;
		for (let bit = 0; bit < 8; bit++) crc = crc >>> 1 ^ (crc & 1 ? 3988292384 : 0);
	}
	return (crc ^ 4294967295) >>> 0;
}
/** scrot produces non-interlaced PNGs. Reject unsupported/corrupt images, including bad CRC/scanlines. */
function decodeFrame(encoded) {
	try {
		const raw = encoded.replace(/^data:image\/png;base64,/, "").trim();
		if (!raw || raw.length > 33554432 || !/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$/.test(raw)) return null;
		const file = Buffer.from(raw, "base64");
		if (!file.subarray(0, 8).equals(Buffer.from("89504e470d0a1a0a", "hex"))) return null;
		let offset = 8;
		let width = 0;
		let height = 0;
		let channels = 0;
		let ended = false;
		const data = [];
		while (offset + 12 <= file.length) {
			const length = file.readUInt32BE(offset);
			if (length > 25165824 || offset + length + 12 > file.length) return null;
			const kind = file.toString("ascii", offset + 4, offset + 8);
			const bytes = file.subarray(offset + 8, offset + 8 + length);
			if (crc32(file.subarray(offset + 4, offset + length + 8)) !== file.readUInt32BE(offset + length + 8)) return null;
			if (kind === "IHDR") {
				if (offset !== 8 || length !== 13) return null;
				width = bytes.readUInt32BE(0);
				height = bytes.readUInt32BE(4);
				channels = {
					0: 1,
					2: 3,
					4: 2,
					6: 4
				}[bytes[9]] ?? 0;
				if (!width || !height || width * height > 8e6 || bytes[8] !== 8 || bytes[10] || bytes[11] || bytes[12] || !channels) return null;
			} else if (kind === "IDAT") data.push(bytes);
			else if (kind === "IEND") {
				if (length) return null;
				ended = true;
				offset += 12;
				break;
			}
			offset += length + 12;
		}
		if (!ended || offset !== file.length || !data.length || !channels) return null;
		const stride = width * channels;
		const scan = (0, node_zlib.inflateSync)(Buffer.concat(data), { maxOutputLength: (stride + 1) * height });
		if (scan.length !== (stride + 1) * height) return null;
		const pixels = Buffer.alloc(stride * height);
		for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
			const filter = scan[y * (stride + 1)];
			if (filter > 4) return null;
			for (let x = 0; x < stride; x++) {
				const left = x >= channels ? pixels[y * stride + x - channels] : 0;
				const up = y ? pixels[(y - 1) * stride + x] : 0;
				const upperLeft = y && x >= channels ? pixels[(y - 1) * stride + x - channels] : 0;
				let predictor = 0;
				if (filter === 1) predictor = left;
				if (filter === 2) predictor = up;
				if (filter === 3) predictor = Math.floor((left + up) / 2);
				if (filter === 4) {
					const p = left + up - upperLeft;
					const a = Math.abs(p - left);
					const b = Math.abs(p - up);
					const c = Math.abs(p - upperLeft);
					predictor = a <= b && a <= c ? left : b <= c ? up : upperLeft;
				}
				pixels[y * stride + x] = scan[y * (stride + 1) + x + 1] + predictor & 255;
			}
		}
		return {
			width,
			height,
			pixels,
			digest: (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(`${width}:${height}:${channels}:`).update(pixels).digest("hex")
		};
	} catch {
		return null;
	}
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/isolation/input-injector.ts
function verifyAfterAction(before, after, expected, facts) {
	const oldFrame = decodeFrame(before);
	const newFrame = decodeFrame(after);
	if (!newFrame || !oldFrame) return {
		verified: false,
		note: "画面不是完整的可解码 PNG，不能核验动作结果"
	};
	if (Boolean(expected && facts && (expected.text || expected.url) && (!expected.text || facts.text.includes(expected.text)) && (!expected.url || facts.url === expected.url))) return {
		verified: true,
		note: "已按独立页面事实核验预期结果"
	};
	return {
		verified: false,
		note: oldFrame.digest === newFrame.digest ? "画面没有变化，预期结果尚未核实" : "画面发生变化，但不能证明预期动作成功；没有重复执行"
	};
}
function activityLimitReason(steps, elapsedMs, limits = ACTIVITY_LIMITS) {
	if (steps > limits.steps) return `已到 ${limits.steps} 步上限，活动停止，没有记成完成。`;
	if (elapsedMs >= limits.minutes * 6e4) return `已到 ${limits.minutes} 分钟上限，活动停止，没有记成完成。`;
	return null;
}
function understandVideo(frames, captions) {
	const unique = [];
	for (const frame of frames) if (unique.at(-1) !== frame) unique.push(frame);
	const sampled = unique.filter((_, index) => index % 30 === 0);
	return markUntrusted([captions.length ? captions.join("\n") : "没有字幕，只根据抽样画面。", ...sampled.map((frame) => `关键帧 ${frame}`)].join("\n"), "video");
}
var ACTIVITY_LIMITS = {
	steps: 12,
	minutes: 10,
	costYuan: 1
};

var probe = String.raw`
import socket, json, struct, base64, hashlib, os, sys, time

  more=s.recv(4096)
  if not more: raise Exception('WebSocket closed before handshake')
  buf+=more

def read(n):
  global pending
  while len(pending)<n:
    more=s.recv(max(4096,n-len(pending)))
    if not more: raise Exception('WebSocket closed')
    pending+=more
  out=pending[:n];pending=pending[n:];return out
def send(payload,opcode=1):
  b=payload if isinstance(payload,bytes) else json.dumps(payload).encode()
  mask=os.urandom(4); n=len(b)
  size=bytes([0x80|n]) if n<126 else (bytes([0xfe])+struct.pack('!H',n) if n<65536 else bytes([0xff])+struct.pack('!Q',n))
  s.sendall(bytes([0x80|opcode])+size+mask+bytes(v^mask[i%4] for i,v in enumerate(b)))
def receive():
  parts=b''
  while True:
    a,b=read(2); n=b&127
    if n==126:n=struct.unpack('!H',read(2))[0]
    if n==127:n=struct.unpack('!Q',read(8))[0]
    if n>2000000:raise Exception('BiDi output too large')
    mask=read(4) if b&128 else None
    data=read(n)
    if mask:data=bytes(v^mask[i%4] for i,v in enumerate(data))
    opcode=a&15
    if opcode==8:raise Exception('BiDi closed')
    if opcode==9:send(data,10);continue
    if opcode not in (0,1):continue

    if len(parts)>2000000:raise Exception('BiDi output too large')
    if a&128:return json.loads(parts)
seq=0
def command(method,params):
  global seq
  seq+=1;send({'id':seq,'method':method,'params':params})
  while True:
    response=receive()
    if response.get('id')!=seq:continue
    if response.get('type')=='error':raise Exception(response.get('message','BiDi failed'))
    return response['result']
command('session.new',{'capabilities':{}})
def evaluate(request):
  target=request['target']
  selected=None
  for attempt in range(20):
    contexts=command('browsingContext.getTree',{})['contexts']
    if target.get('contextId'):
      candidates=[c for c in contexts if c['context']==target['contextId']]
    else:
      candidates=[c for c in contexts if target.get('expectedUrl') and c.get('url')==target['expectedUrl']]
      if len(candidates)!=1:candidates=[c for c in contexts if c.get('url') and not c['url'].startswith('about:')]

  except Exception:pass
  finally:client.close()

var bridgeClient = String.raw`
