//#region src/main/core/services/asset-service.ts
function validateCharacterDirectory(directory) {
	const names = (0, node_fs.existsSync)(directory) ? (0, node_fs.readdirSync)(directory) : [];
	if (names.some((name) => name.endsWith(".moc") && !name.endsWith(".moc3"))) return {
		ok: false,
		reason: "不支持 Cubism 2.1 的 .moc 资源"
	};
	const model = names.find((name) => name.endsWith(".model3.json"));
	if (!model) return {
		ok: false,
		reason: "缺少 model3.json"
	};
	if (!names.some((name) => name.endsWith(".moc3"))) return {
		ok: false,
		reason: "缺少 moc3"
	};
	const textures = JSON.parse((0, node_fs.readFileSync)((0, node_path.join)(directory, model), "utf8")).FileReferences?.Textures ?? [];
	if (textures.length === 0 || textures.some((texture) => !(0, node_fs.existsSync)((0, node_path.join)(directory, texture)))) return {
		ok: false,
		reason: "贴图缺失，原有角色保持不变"
	};
	return {
		ok: true,
		reason: "资源可以加载"
	};
}
function directoryWithModel(directory) {
	const names = (0, node_fs.readdirSync)(directory);
	if (names.some((name) => name.endsWith(".model3.json") || name.endsWith(".moc") && !name.endsWith(".moc3"))) return directory;
	const nested = names.map((name) => (0, node_path.join)(directory, name)).filter((path) => {
		try {
			return (0, node_fs.statSync)(path).isDirectory();
		} catch {
			return false;
		}
	});
	if (nested.length === 1) return directoryWithModel(nested[0]);
	return directory;
}
function unzip(source, destination) {
	return (0, node_child_process.spawnSync)("powershell.exe", [
		"-NoProfile",
		"-NonInteractive",
		"-Command",
		`Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $env:MURASAME_ZIP -DestinationPath $env:MURASAME_UNZIP -Force`
	], {
		env: {
			...process.env,
			MURASAME_ZIP: source,
			MURASAME_UNZIP: destination
		},
		windowsHide: true,
		encoding: "utf8",
		timeout: 2e4
	}).status === 0;
}
function importCharacterDirectory(source, destRoot) {
	const failed = (reason) => ({
		ok: false,
		reason,
		id: "",
		modelUrl: ""
	});
	if (!(0, node_fs.existsSync)(source)) return failed("找不到要导入的目录或压缩包，原有角色保持不变");
	let staging = source;
	let temporary = null;
	if (source.toLowerCase().endsWith(".zip")) {
		temporary = (0, node_fs.mkdtempSync)((0, node_path.join)((0, node_os.tmpdir)(), "murasame-live2d-"));
		if (!unzip(source, temporary)) {
			(0, node_fs.rmSync)(temporary, {
				recursive: true,
				force: true
			});
			return failed("压缩包无法解压，原有角色保持不变");
		}
		staging = directoryWithModel(temporary);
	}
	const check = validateCharacterDirectory(staging);
	if (!check.ok) {
		if (temporary) (0, node_fs.rmSync)(temporary, {
			recursive: true,
			force: true
		});
		return failed(`${check.reason}。原有角色保持不变`);
	}
	const id = `char-${Date.now()}`;
	const destination = (0, node_path.join)(destRoot, id);
	(0, node_fs.cpSync)(staging, destination, { recursive: true });
	if (temporary) (0, node_fs.rmSync)(temporary, {
		recursive: true,
		force: true
	});
	const model = (0, node_fs.readdirSync)(destination).find((name) => name.endsWith(".model3.json"));
	if (!model) {
		(0, node_fs.rmSync)(destination, {
			recursive: true,
			force: true
		});
		return failed("缺少 model3.json。原有角色保持不变");
	}
	return {
		ok: true,
		reason: "资源可以加载",
		id,
		modelUrl: `characters/${id}/${model}`
	};
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/hotkey-status.ts
var current = "";
var message = "唤起快捷键尚未注册";
function readHotkey() {
	return message;
}
/** 换快捷键时先放掉旧的；新的注册不上就尽量恢复旧的，并如实说明。 */
function applyHotkey(accelerator, onPress) {
	const next = accelerator.trim();
	const previous = current;
	if (previous) electron.globalShortcut.unregister(previous);
	let registered;
	try {
		registered = next.length > 0 && electron.globalShortcut.register(next, onPress);
	} catch {
		registered = false;
	}
	if (registered) {
		current = next;
		message = `${next} 已注册，可唤起桌宠对话`;
		return {
			ok: true,
			message
		};
	}
	let restored;
	try {
		restored = Boolean(previous) && previous !== next && electron.globalShortcut.register(previous, onPress);
	} catch {
		restored = false;
	}
	current = restored ? previous : "";
	message = restored ? `${next} 注册失败，可能被其他程序占用或写法不对。仍在使用 ${previous}。` : `${next} 注册失败，可能被其他程序占用或写法不对。请在设置里换一个组合，例如 Ctrl+Alt+M。`;
	return {
		ok: false,
		message
	};
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/agent/learn-verified-image-skill.ts
/** Learns only the registered deterministic image procedure. User/model text is
* descriptive data and is never interpreted as a program. Repeated broadcasts
* are idempotent and failure history stays within its original root scope. */
function learnVerifiedImageSkill(dir, task) {
	const calls = task.toolCalls.filter((call) => call.agentRole === "Worker" && call.stepId.endsWith("-copy-image"));
	if (!calls.length) return null;
	const latest = [...calls].reverse().find((call) => call.declared.args.phase === "result") ?? calls.at(-1);
	const args = latest.declared.args;
	if (!args.hostPath) return null;
	const path = (0, node_path.resolve)(args.hostPath);
	const root = (0, node_path.dirname)(path);
	const scope = {
		workspace: /^(desktop|桌面)$/i.test((0, node_path.basename)(root)) ? "desktop" : "pictures",
		root
	};
	if ([
		"失败",
		"已取消",
		"部分完成"
	].includes(task.status)) {
		noteSkillFailure(dir, "draw-image", task.result || task.summary, scope, task.id);
		return null;
	}
	if (task.status !== "已完成" || args.procedureId !== "isolated-example-image-v1" || !args.sourceHash || latest.execution.status !== "ok" || latest.verification.verdict !== "confirmed") return null;
	const hashProof = latest.verification.evidence.find((item) => item.kind === "fs-hash" && item.matched && (0, node_path.resolve)(item.target) === path && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(item.actual) && item.actual === item.expected && item.actual === args.sourceHash);
	if (!hashProof || !task.artifacts.some((artifact) => (0, node_path.resolve)(artifact) === path)) return null;
	try {
		const actual = resolveInside(root, path);
		if (!(0, node_fs.statSync)(actual).isFile()) return null;
		const bytes = (0, node_fs.readFileSync)(actual);
		if (!bytes.subarray(0, 8).equals(Buffer.from([
			137,
			80,
			78,
			71,
			13,
			10,
			26,
			10
		])) || (0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex") !== hashProof.actual) {
			noteSkillFailure(dir, "draw-image", "已交付图片在学习前发生变化，未记录为成功技能", scope, task.id);
			return null;
		}
		const known = readSkills(dir).find((skill) => skill.verification?.taskId === task.id);
		if (known) return known;
		const previous = findSkill(dir, "draw-image", scope);
		return rememberSkill(dir, {
			id: `image-skill-${(0, node_crypto.randomUUID)()}`,
			kind: "draw-image",
			procedure: "isolated-example-image-v1",
			steps: "隔离生成确定性示例图片 → 独立核对原件 → 批准后排他复制 → 独立核对目标哈希",
			check: "原件与目标 SHA-256 一致，目标为有效的非空 PNG；每次仍须新的目标批准",
			allowedTools: ["filesystem"],
			workspace: scope.workspace,
			root,
			superseded: false,
			lastFailure: "",
			failures: previous?.failures ? [...previous.failures] : [],
			createdAt: (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString()
		}, {
			taskId: task.id,
			status: "已完成",
			evidence: [hashProof]
		});
	} catch {
		return null;
	}
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/updater.ts
var configured = false;
var timer = null;
function disabled(message) {
	return {
		status: "disabled",
		message
	};
}
function configure() {
	if (configured) return true;
	const url = process.env.MURASAME_UPDATE_URL?.trim();
	if (!electron.app.isPackaged) {
		logger_default.info("Auto-update disabled in development build");
		return false;
	}
	if (!url) {
		logger_default.info("Auto-update disabled: MURASAME_UPDATE_URL is not configured");
		return false;
	}
	electron_updater.autoUpdater.setFeedURL({
		provider: "generic",
		url
	});
	electron_updater.autoUpdater.autoDownload = true;
	electron_updater.autoUpdater.autoInstallOnAppQuit = true;
	electron_updater.autoUpdater.logger = logger_default;
	electron_updater.autoUpdater.on("error", (error) => logger_default.error("Auto-update error", error));
	electron_updater.autoUpdater.on("update-available", (info) => logger_default.info(`Update available: ${info.version}`));
	electron_updater.autoUpdater.on("update-not-available", () => logger_default.info("Update not available"));
	electron_updater.autoUpdater.on("update-downloaded", (info) => logger_default.info(`Update downloaded: ${info.version}`));
	configured = true;
	return true;
}
async function checkForUpdates() {
	if (!configure()) return disabled(!electron.app.isPackaged ? "开发环境不检查更新。安装版配置更新源后可自动检查。" : "尚未配置 MURASAME_UPDATE_URL，自动更新保持关闭。");
	try {
		const result = await electron_updater.autoUpdater.checkForUpdates();
		if (!result) return {
			status: "not-available",
			message: "当前已是最新版本。"
		};
		if (result.updateInfo.version !== electron.app.getVersion()) return {
			status: "available",
			message: `发现新版本 ${result.updateInfo.version}，正在后台下载。`,
			version: result.updateInfo.version
		};
		return {
			status: "not-available",
			message: "当前已是最新版本。"
		};
	} catch (error) {
		logger_default.error("Update check failed", error);
		return {
			status: "error",
			message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "检查更新失败，请稍后重试。"
		};
	}
}
function initializeUpdater() {
	if (!configure()) return;
	timer = setInterval(() => {
		checkForUpdates();
	}, 216e5);
	setTimeout(() => {
		checkForUpdates();
	}, 1e4);
}
function stopUpdater() {
	if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
	timer = null;
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/ipc.ts
var registered = false;
function broadcast(channel, payload) {
	for (const window of electron.BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()) if (!window.isDestroyed()) window.webContents.send(channel, payload);
}
function validationError(error) {
	const field = error.issues[0]?.path.join(".");
	return field ? `请求参数无效：${field}` : "请求参数无效";
}
function redact(message) {
	return message.replace(/sk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}/g, "sk-••••").replace(/(api[-_ ]?key\s*[:=]\s*)\S+/gi, "$1••••");
}
function errorMessage(error) {
	if (error instanceof NotImplementedError) {
		const appError = new AppError("NOT_IMPLEMENTED", "尚未实现", error.message, "请改用当前已经可用的功能，或稍后再试。", false, { cause: error });
		logger_default.error(appError.code, appError.category);
		return appError.toUserText();
	}
	if (error instanceof AppError) {
		logger_default.error(error.code, error.category);
		return redact(error.toUserText());
	}
	return redact(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "操作失败，请稍后重试");
}
function handleCore(channel, schema, action) {
	electron.ipcMain.handle(channel, async (_event, raw) => {
		const parsed = schema.safeParse(raw);
		if (!parsed.success) return {
			ok: false,
			error: validationError(parsed.error)
		};
		try {
			return await action(parsed.data);
		} catch (error) {
			logger_default.error(`Core IPC failed: ${channel}`, error);
			return {
				ok: false,
				error: errorMessage(error)
			};
		}
	});
}
function applyLoginSetting(settings) {
	electron.app.setLoginItemSettings({
		openAtLogin: settings.startAtLogin,
		args: ["--hidden"]
	});
}
function syncPetSettings(previous, next) {
	if (previous.petVisible !== next.petVisible) {
		if (next.petVisible) showPet();
		else hidePet();
	}
	if (previous.petScale !== next.petScale) setPetScale(next.petScale);
	if (previous.petPreset !== next.petPreset) refreshPetState();
}
function registerIpcHandlers(core) {
	if (registered) return;
	registered = true;
	let isolationPrepare = null;
	let prepareController = null;
	let prepareGeneration = 0;
	const autonomyViewState = (state) => {
		if (!isolationPrepare || state.running || state.canStop) return state;
		const cancelling = prepareController?.signal.aborted === true;
		return {
			...state,
			activityId: "",
			goal: "",
			kind: void 0,
			creation: void 0,
			result: void 0,
			viewUrl: "",
			running: false,
			phase: cancelling ? "stopping" : "preparing",
			canStop: true,
			busy: true,
			reason: cancelling ? "正在取消隔离环境准备。" : "正在准备隔离环境，离开页面不会中断；可停止准备。"
		};
	};
	const publishAutonomy = (state = core.autonomy.status()) => {
		try {
			broadcast(IPC.autonomy.updated, autonomyViewState(state));
		} catch {}
	};
	core.autonomy.subscribe(publishAutonomy);
	const privacyKey = () => JSON.stringify([
		getSettings().permissions.screen,
		getSettings().outboundImages,
		getSettings().doNotDisturb,
		getSettings().screenWatchEnabled
	]);
	let previousPrivacy = privacyKey();
	core.onScreenAnalysis = (analysis) => broadcast(IPC.core.screenUpdated, analysis);
	core.onConversationUpdate = (conversation) => broadcast(IPC.core.conversationUpdated, conversation);
	core.onConversationDeleted = (event) => broadcast(IPC.core.conversationDeleted, event);
	core.memory.subscribe(() => broadcast(IPC.core.memoriesUpdated, core.memory.list()));
	core.subscribeModels((models) => broadcast(IPC.core.modelsUpdated, models));
	core.memory.subscribeIndex((status) => broadcast(IPC.core.memoryIndexStatus, status));
	core.onMemoryExtractionStatus = (status) => broadcast(IPC.core.memoryExtractionStatus, status);
	const presentedTaskStates = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	core.tasks.onChange((task) => {
		try {
			learnVerifiedImageSkill(electron.app.getPath("userData"), task);
		} catch (error) {
			logger_default.warn("任务已记录，可复用步骤未保存", error);
		}
		broadcast(IPC.core.taskUpdated, task);
		if (presentedTaskStates.get(task.id) === task.status) return;
		presentedTaskStates.set(task.id, task.status);
		if (presentedTaskStates.size > 100) presentedTaskStates.delete(presentedTaskStates.keys().next().value);
		core.invalidatePresentation();
		if (getSettings().doNotDisturb) return;
		const intent = task.status === "已完成" ? "开心" : task.status === "失败" ? "困惑" : "思考";
		const preset = getSettings().petPreset;
		const cue = performanceCue(preset, intent, core.affect.pad);
		if (preset === "naiwa" && task.status === "执行中") {
			cue.motion = "Working";
			cue.expression = null;
		}
		broadcastPetPerformance({
			...cue,
			text: petSpeech("任务「" + task.title + "」" + task.status + "。" + (task.result.split("\n")[0] || task.summary))
		});
	});
	onSettingsChanged((settings) => {
		core.syncMemoryPolicy();
		const nextPrivacy = privacyKey();
		if (nextPrivacy !== previousPrivacy) {
			core.cancelSensitiveRequests();
			previousPrivacy = nextPrivacy;
		}
		broadcast(IPC.settings.changed, settings);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.app.getInfo, () => {
		return {
			version: electron.app.getVersion(),
			platform: process.platform,
			isPackaged: electron.app.isPackaged,
			logsPath: electron.app.getPath("logs"),
			userDataPath: electron.app.getPath("userData")
		};
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.app.quit, () => confirmQuit());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.app.hideToTray, () => hideMain());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.app.openLogs, async () => {
		const failure = await electron.shell.openPath(electron.app.getPath("logs"));
		if (failure) throw new Error(failure);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.app.openUserData, async () => {
		const failure = await electron.shell.openPath(electron.app.getPath("userData"));
		if (failure) throw new Error(failure);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.app.clearLogs, () => {
		logger_default.transports.file.getFile().clear();
		logger_default.info("Application logs cleared by user");
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.app.pickOpen, async (_event, raw) => {
		const value = raw ?? {};
		const result = await electron.dialog.showOpenDialog({
			title: value.title,
			properties: value.directory ? ["openDirectory"] : ["openFile"],
			filters: value.filters
		});
		return result.canceled ? null : result.filePaths[0] ?? null;
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.app.hotkey, () => readHotkey());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.app.pickSave, async (_event, raw) => {
		const value = raw ?? {};
		const result = await electron.dialog.showSaveDialog({
			title: value.title,
			defaultPath: value.defaultPath
		});
		return result.canceled ? null : result.filePath ?? null;
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.window.showMain, () => showMain());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.window.hideMain, () => hideMain());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.window.minimize, () => getMainWindow()?.minimize());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.window.openPath, (_event, raw) => {
		openMainPath(typeof raw === "string" ? raw : "");
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.show, () => showPet());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.hide, () => hidePet());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.toggle, () => togglePet());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.getState, () => getPetStateSafe());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.getBounds, () => getPetBounds());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.resetPosition, () => resetPetPosition());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.setScale, (_event, raw) => {
		const parsed = petScaleSchema.safeParse(raw);
		if (!parsed.success) throw new Error(validationError(parsed.error));
		return setPetScale(parsed.data.scale);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.setPosition, (_event, raw) => {
		const parsed = petPositionSchema.safeParse(raw);
		if (!parsed.success) throw new Error(validationError(parsed.error));
		return setPetPosition(parsed.data.x, parsed.data.y);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.setIgnoreMouseEvents, (_event, raw) => {
		const parsed = ignoreMouseSchema.safeParse(raw);
		if (!parsed.success) throw new Error(validationError(parsed.error));
		setPetMousePassthrough(parsed.data.ignore);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.setChatOpen, (_event, raw) => {
		const parsed = petChatSchema.safeParse(raw);
		if (!parsed.success) throw new Error(validationError(parsed.error));
		return setPetChatOpen(parsed.data.open);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.beginDrag, (_event, raw) => {
		const point = raw;
		if (typeof point?.x !== "number" || typeof point?.y !== "number") return;
		beginPetDrag(point.x, point.y);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.dragMove, (_event, raw) => {
		const point = raw;
		if (typeof point?.x !== "number" || typeof point?.y !== "number") return;
		movePetDrag(point.x, point.y);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.endDrag, () => endPetDrag());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.settings.get, () => getSettings());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.settings.update, (_event, raw) => {
		const parsed = settingsPatchSchema.safeParse(raw);
		if (!parsed.success) throw new Error(validationError(parsed.error));
		const previous = getSettings();
		let next = updateSettings(parsed.data);
		applyLoginSetting(next);
		syncPetSettings(previous, next);
		if (parsed.data.petHotkey && parsed.data.petHotkey !== previous.petHotkey) {
			if (!applyHotkey(parsed.data.petHotkey, () => focusPetChat()).ok) next = updateSettings({ petHotkey: previous.petHotkey });
		}
		return next;
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.updates.check, () => checkForUpdates());
	handleCore(IPC.core.getBootstrap, zod.z.undefined(), () => core.getBootstrap());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.core.createConversation, async () => {
		const result = await core.createConversation();
		if (result.ok) {
			broadcast(IPC.core.conversationUpdated, result.data);
			broadcast(IPC.core.activeChanged, { id: result.data.id });
		}
		return result;
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.core.setActiveConversation, async (_event, raw) => {
		const parsed = idSchema.safeParse(raw);
		if (!parsed.success) return {
			ok: false,
			error: validationError(parsed.error)
		};
		const result = await core.setActiveConversation(parsed.data.id);
		if (result.ok) broadcast(IPC.core.activeChanged, { id: result.data.id });
		return result;
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.core.sendMessage, async (_event, raw) => {
		const parsed = sendMessageSchema.safeParse(raw);
		if (!parsed.success) return {
			ok: false,
			error: validationError(parsed.error)
		};
		const { conversationId, text } = parsed.data;
		try {
			const result = await core.sendMessage(conversationId, text, (delta) => broadcast(IPC.core.messageDelta, {
				conversationId,
				delta
			}), () => broadcast(IPC.core.messageStarted, {
				conversationId,
				userText: text
			}));
			if (result.ok) broadcast(IPC.core.conversationUpdated, result.data);
			else broadcast(IPC.core.messageFailed, {
				conversationId,
				error: result.error
			});
			return result;
		} catch (error) {
			logger_default.error("Chat IPC failed", error);
			const message = errorMessage(error);
			broadcast(IPC.core.messageFailed, {
				conversationId,
				error: message
			});
			return {
				ok: false,
				error: message
			};
		}
	});
	handleCore(IPC.core.deleteConversation, deleteConversationSchema, (input) => {
		const value = input;
		return core.deleteConversation(value.id, value.scopes);
	});
	handleCore(IPC.core.addMemory, memoryAddSchema, (input) => {
		const value = input;
		return core.addMemory(value.category, value.text);
	});
	handleCore(IPC.core.updateMemory, memoryUpdateSchema, (input) => {
		const value = input;
		return core.updateMemory(value.id, value.category, value.text);
	});
	handleCore(IPC.core.deleteMemory, memoryDeleteSchema, (input) => core.deleteMemory(input.id));
	handleCore(IPC.core.clearMemories, zod.z.undefined(), () => core.clearMemories());
	handleCore(IPC.core.createTask, taskCreateSchema, (input) => core.createTask(input.title, input.root));
	handleCore(IPC.core.approveTask, zod.z.object({
		id: zod.z.string().min(1),
		scope: zod.z.enum(["once", "task"]).optional()
	}), (input) => {
		const value = input;
		return core.approveTask(value.id, value.scope ?? "once");
	});
	handleCore(IPC.core.cancelTask, idSchema, (input) => core.cancelTask(input.id));
	handleCore(IPC.core.setToolEnabled, toolToggleSchema, (input) => {
		const value = input;
		return core.setToolEnabled(value.id, value.enabled);
	});
	handleCore(IPC.core.verifyModel, modelVerifySchema, (input) => {
		const value = input;
		return core.verifyModel(value.id, value.input);
	});
	handleCore(IPC.core.createModel, zod.z.undefined(), () => core.createModel());
	handleCore(IPC.core.saveModel, modelSaveSchema, (input) => {
		const value = input;
		return core.saveModel(value.id, value.input);
	});
	handleCore(IPC.core.deleteModel, idSchema, (input) => core.deleteModel(input.id));
	handleCore(IPC.core.setDefaultModel, idSchema, (input) => core.setDefaultModel(input.id));
	handleCore(IPC.core.saveCharacter, characterSchema, (input) => core.saveCharacter(input));
	handleCore(IPC.core.analyzeScreen, zod.z.undefined(), () => core.analyzeScreen());
	handleCore(IPC.core.searchHistory, zod.z.object({ keyword: zod.z.string().max(200) }), (input) => core.searchHistory(input.keyword));
	handleCore(IPC.core.denyTask, idSchema, (input) => core.denyTask(input.id));
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.character.importAsset, (_event, raw) => {
		const parsed = characterImportSchema.safeParse(raw);
		if (!parsed.success) return {
			ok: false,
			error: validationError(parsed.error)
		};
		const imported = importCharacterDirectory(parsed.data.path, (0, node_path.join)(electron.app.getPath("userData"), "characters"));
		if (!imported.ok) return {
			ok: false,
			error: imported.reason
		};
		try {
			getDatabase().prepare(`INSERT INTO characters_imported(id, name, format, root_path, loaded, error, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, 'cubism', ?, 1, NULL, ?)`).run(imported.id, imported.modelUrl, (0, node_path.join)(electron.app.getPath("userData"), "characters", imported.id), (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString());
		} catch (error) {
			return {
				ok: false,
				error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "导入记录保存失败，原有角色保持不变"
			};
		}
		return {
			ok: true,
			data: imported
		};
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.pet.getAffect, () => core.affect.pad);
	const relationshipView = (record) => ({
		stage: record.stage,
		since: record.since,
		history: record.history,
		lineId: record.lineId,
		address: relationshipVoice(record.stage).title,
		archivedLines: record.archivedLines
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.relationship.get, () => relationshipView(readRelationship()));
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.relationship.reset, () => {
		const relationship = writeRelationship(resetRelationship(readRelationship()));
		const view = relationshipView(relationship);
		broadcast(IPC.relationship.changed, view);
		return view;
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.relationship.changeAppearance, (_event, raw) => {
		const parsed = zod.z.object({
			mode: zod.z.enum(["continue", "new"]),
			patch: settingsPatchSchema.pick({
				petPreset: true,
				importedModelUrl: true,
				importedCharacterName: true
			}).strict()
		}).safeParse(raw);
		if (!parsed.success) throw new Error(validationError(parsed.error));
		const previous = getSettings();
		const next = updateSettings(parsed.data.patch);
		const relationship = parsed.data.mode === "new" ? writeRelationship(startNewLine(readRelationship())) : readRelationship();
		syncPetSettings(previous, next);
		if (previous.importedModelUrl !== next.importedModelUrl) refreshPetState();
		const view = relationshipView(relationship);
		broadcast(IPC.relationship.changed, view);
		return {
			settings: next,
			relationship: view
		};
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.voice.readiness, () => core.voice.readiness());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.voice.transcribe, async (_event, raw) => {
		const payload = raw;
		const samples = Array.isArray(payload?.samples) ? Float32Array.from(payload.samples) : /* @__PURE__ */ new Float32Array();
		return core.voice.transcribe(samples, getSettings().permissions.microphone);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.voice.speak, async (_event, raw) => {
		const payload = raw;
		const spoken = await core.voice.speak(String(payload?.text ?? ""));
		return {
			samples: spoken.audio ? Array.from(spoken.audio) : [],
			sampleRate: spoken.sampleRate,
			played: spoken.played,
			message: spoken.message
		};
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.data.exportBundle, async (_event, raw) => {
		const requested = raw ?? {};
		const passphrase = String(requested.passphrase ?? "");
		if (passphrase.length < 6) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "导出口令至少 6 位。没有口令不会导出。"
		};
		const bootstrap = await core.getBootstrap();
		if (!bootstrap.ok) return bootstrap;
		const bundle = stripSecrets({
			version: 3,
			conversations: bootstrap.data.conversations,
			memories: bootstrap.data.memories,
			character: bootstrap.data.character,
			models: bootstrap.data.models.map((model) => ({
				id: model.id,
				name: model.name,
				endpoint: model.endpoint
			}))
		});
		const target = requested.path?.trim() || (0, node_path.join)(electron.app.getPath("downloads"), `murasame-${(/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.murapack`);
		try {
			(0, node_fs.writeFileSync)(target, exportBundle(bundle, passphrase));
			return {
				ok: true,
				data: {
					path: target,
					note: "数据包已加密，不含明文密钥。"
				}
			};
		} catch (error) {
			return {
				ok: false,
				error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "导出失败"
			};
		}
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.data.importBundle, async (_event, raw) => {
		const value = raw;
		const source = String(value?.path ?? "");
		try {
			const bundle = importBundle((0, node_fs.readFileSync)(source), String(value?.passphrase ?? ""), 3);
			const dbFile = databasePath(getUserDataDir());
			if ((0, node_fs.existsSync)(dbFile)) (0, node_fs.copyFileSync)(dbFile, `${dbFile}.before-import`);
			const merged = mergeImportedBundle(getDatabase(), bundle);
			if (bundle.character?.name) await core.saveCharacter({
				name: bundle.character.name,
				style: bundle.character.style ?? "",
				example: bundle.character.example ?? "",
				keywords: bundle.character.keywords ?? [],
				proactive: Boolean(bundle.character.proactive),
				memory: bundle.character.memory !== false
			});
			return {
				ok: true,
				data: {
					conversations: merged.conversations,
					memories: merged.memories,
					note: `已合并导入。原数据库备份在 ${dbFile}.before-import。`
				}
			};
		} catch (error) {
			return {
				ok: false,
				error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "导入失败，现有数据保持不变"
			};
		}
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.environment, () => isolationEnvironmentAsync());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.preview, () => core.autonomy.preview());
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.status, async () => {
		const state = await core.autonomy.health();
		return autonomyViewState(state);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.create, (_event, raw) => {
		const input = zod.z.object({
			kind: zod.z.enum(["office", "code"]),
			goal: zod.z.string().trim().min(1).max(2e3)
		}).strict().safeParse(raw);
		if (!input.success) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "请选择办公或Python创作，并填写2000字以内的目标"
		};
		return core.createIsolationArtifact(input.data.kind, input.data.goal);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.exportArtifact, async (_event, raw) => {
		const input = zod.z.object({ id: zod.z.string().uuid() }).strict().safeParse(raw);
		const state = core.autonomy.status();
		const artifact = state.creation?.artifact;
		if (!input.success || !state.running || !artifact || artifact.id !== input.data.id) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "当前没有可导出的核验产物"
		};
		const selection = await electron.dialog.showSaveDialog({
			title: "保存已核验产物（请使用新文件名）",
			defaultPath: artifact.name,
			filters: [{
				name: artifact.kind === "office" ? "OpenDocument文字文档" : "Python脚本",
				extensions: [artifact.kind === "office" ? "odt" : "py"]
			}]
		});
		if (selection.canceled || !selection.filePath) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "已取消保存，产物仍留在本次隔离活动中"
		};
		return core.autonomy.exportCreation(artifact.id, selection.filePath);
	});
	const prepareIsolation = (send) => {
		if (isolationPrepare) return isolationPrepare;
		const controller = new AbortController();
		prepareController = controller;
		isolationPrepare = (async () => {
			const docker = await ensureDocker({
				onStatus: send,
				signal: controller.signal
			});
			if (!docker.ok) return docker;
			if ((await isolationEnvironmentAsync(void 0, { signal: controller.signal })).image) return {
				ok: true,
				detail: "隔离环境已经就绪。"
			};
			const context = electron.app.isPackaged ? (0, node_path.join)(process.resourcesPath, "isolation") : (0, node_path.join)(electron.app.getAppPath(), "isolation");
			if (!(0, node_fs.existsSync)((0, node_path.join)(context, "Dockerfile"))) return {
				ok: false,
				detail: "安装目录里没有隔离环境的制作文件。"
			};
			send("第一次使用，正在准备隔离环境，大约几分钟。");
			let last = "";
			return buildIsolationImage(context, (line) => {
				const text = friendlyBuildLine(line);
				if (!text || text === last) return;
				last = text;
				send(text);
			}, { signal: controller.signal });
		})().finally(() => {
			isolationPrepare = null;
			if (prepareController === controller) prepareController = null;
			publishAutonomy();
		});
		publishAutonomy();
		return isolationPrepare;
	};
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.buildImage, (event) => prepareIsolation((line) => {
		if (!event.sender.isDestroyed()) event.sender.send(IPC.autonomy.buildProgress, line);
	}));
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.start, async (event, raw) => {
		const input = zod.z.object({
			confirmed: zod.z.boolean().optional(),
			allowNetwork: zod.z.boolean().optional(),
			kind: zod.z.enum([
				"browse",
				"video",
				"office",
				"code"
			]).optional()
		}).strict().safeParse(raw ?? {});
		if (!input.success) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "活动范围无效，请重新确认"
		};
		const value = input.data;
		if (!value.confirmed) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "还没有确认活动范围，容器没有启动。"
		};
		const generation = prepareGeneration;
		const prepared = await prepareIsolation((line) => {
			if (!event.sender.isDestroyed()) event.sender.send(IPC.autonomy.buildProgress, line);
		});
		if (generation !== prepareGeneration) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "启动准备已取消，没有创建活动"
		};
		if (!prepared.ok) return {
			ok: false,
			error: prepared.detail
		};
		return core.autonomy.start({
			dockerAvailable: true,
			approved: true,
			needsPermission: false,
			share: null,
			allowNetwork: value.allowNetwork !== false,
			kind: value.kind
		});
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.open, (_event, raw) => {
		const url = String(raw?.url ?? "");
		return core.autonomy.openPage(url);
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.look, () => {
		if (!getSettings().outboundImages) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "让模型看桌面需要先允许发送截图。当前产物仍在，下方真实画面可直接查看。"
		};
		return core.autonomy.look((image, signal) => core.describeIsolationImage(image, signal));
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.browse, (_event, raw) => {
		const goal = String(raw?.goal ?? "").trim();
		if (!goal) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "先说想让它在隔离桌面里看什么。"
		};
		if (!getSettings().outboundImages) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "自己逛需要把隔离桌面的截图发给看图模型。请先在设置里打开「允许把截图发给模型」。"
		};
		return core.autonomy.browse(goal, (image, target, notes, signal) => core.decideIsolationStep(image, target, notes, signal));
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.watch, (_event, raw) => {
		const url = String(raw?.url ?? "").trim();
		if (!getSettings().outboundImages) return {
			ok: false,
			error: "看视频需要把截到的画面发给看图模型。请先在设置里打开「允许把截图发给模型」。"
		};
		return core.autonomy.watchVideo(url, (image, signal) => core.describeIsolationImage(image, signal));
	});
	electron.ipcMain.handle(IPC.autonomy.stop, async () => {
		prepareGeneration++;
		prepareController?.abort(/* @__PURE__ */ new Error("用户停止了活动准备"));
		if (isolationPrepare) publishAutonomy();
		return core.autonomy.stop({ containerGone: false });
	});
	handleCore(IPC.core.continueTask, zod.z.object({
		id: zod.z.string().min(1),
		answer: zod.z.string().trim().min(1).max(1e4)
	}), (input) => {
		const value = input;
		return core.continueTask(value.id, value.answer);
	});
	handleCore(IPC.core.openArtifact, zod.z.object({
		id: zod.z.string().min(1),
		artifact: zod.z.string().min(1).max(2e3)
	}), (input) => {
		const value = input;
		return core.openArtifact(value.id, value.artifact);
	});
	logger_default.info("IPC handlers registered");
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/tray.ts
var tray = null;
function resourcePath(name) {
	return electron.app.isPackaged ? (0, node_path.join)(process.resourcesPath, "resources", name) : (0, node_path.join)(electron.app.getAppPath(), "resources", name);
}
function createTray() {
	if (tray) return tray;
	const image = electron.nativeImage.createFromPath(resourcePath("icon.png")).resize({
		width: 20,
		height: 20
	});
	tray = new electron.Tray(image);
	const refresh = () => {
		if (!tray) return;
		const quiet = getSettings().doNotDisturb;
		tray.setToolTip(quiet ? "Murasame · 勿扰中" : "Murasame · AI 桌面伙伴");
		tray.setContextMenu(electron.Menu.buildFromTemplate([
			{
				label: "和它说句话",
				click: () => focusPetChat()
			},
			{
				label: "显示 / 隐藏桌宠",
				click: () => togglePet()
			},
			{
				label: quiet ? "关闭勿扰" : "暂时别打扰我",
				click: () => {
					updateSettings({ doNotDisturb: !getSettings().doNotDisturb });
					refresh();
				}
			},
			{ type: "separator" },
			{
				label: "显示控制台",
				click: () => showMain()
			},
			{
				label: "检查更新",
				click: () => {
					checkForUpdates().then((result) => {
						electron.dialog.showMessageBox({
							type: result.status === "error" ? "error" : "info",
							title: "Murasame 更新",
							message: result.message,
							buttons: ["关闭"]
						});
					});
				}
			},
			{ type: "separator" },
			{
				label: "退出 Murasame",
				click: () => {
					confirmQuit();
				}
			}
		]));
	};
	refresh();
	tray.on("double-click", () => focusPetChat());
	tray.on("click", () => focusPetChat());
	logger_default.info("Tray initialized");
	return tray;
}
function destroyTray() {
	tray?.destroy();
	tray = null;
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/security/media-permission.ts
function allowMicrophone(input) {
	if (input.permission !== "media" || !input.audioOnly || !input.enabled) return false;
	try {
		const url = new URL(input.url);
		if (url.protocol === "murasame:" && url.hostname === "app") return true;
		return Boolean(input.developmentOrigin && url.origin === new URL(input.developmentOrigin).origin);
	} catch {
		return false;
	}
}
//#endregion
//#region src/main/core/proactive-loop.ts
var HOUR = 36e5;
var ProactiveLoop = class {
	deps;
	timer = null;
	pending = null;
	epoch = 0;
	lastSpokeAt = 0;
	spokenThisHour = 0;
	spokenToday = 0;
	hourStartedAt = Date.now();
	dayStartedAt = Date.now();
	constructor(deps) {
		this.deps = deps;
	}
	start(intervalMs = 3e5) {
		if (this.timer) return;
		this.timer = setInterval(() => {
			this.tick();
		}, intervalMs);
	}
	stop() {
		if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);
		this.timer = null;
		this.epoch++;
		this.pending?.abort();
	}
	async tick(now = Date.now()) {
		if (this.pending) return {
			spoke: false,
			reason: "上一次前台检查还未结束"
		};
		if (this.deps.hidden() || this.deps.doNotDisturb() || this.deps.taskRunning() || !this.deps.reasons().length) return {
			spoke: false,
			reason: "当前不适合主动开口，或没有值得分享的新内容"
		};
		const controller = new AbortController();
		const epoch = this.epoch;
		this.pending = controller;
		try {
			if (now - this.hourStartedAt >= HOUR) {
				this.hourStartedAt = now;
				this.spokenThisHour = 0;
			}
			if (now - this.dayStartedAt >= 24 * HOUR) {
				this.dayStartedAt = now;
				this.spokenToday = 0;
			}
			const front = await (this.deps.foreground ? this.deps.foreground(controller.signal) : this.readActivity(controller.signal));
			if (controller.signal.aborted || epoch !== this.epoch) return {
				spoke: false,
				reason: "前台检查已取消"
			};
			const reasons = this.deps.reasons();
			const decision = proactiveDecision({
				...front,
				typedWithinMs: front.idleMs,
				hidden: this.deps.hidden(),
				doNotDisturb: this.deps.doNotDisturb(),
				taskRunning: this.deps.taskRunning(),
				minutesSinceLast: this.lastSpokeAt === 0 ? 1e4 : Math.floor((now - this.lastSpokeAt) / 6e4),
				spokenThisHour: this.spokenThisHour,
				spokenToday: this.spokenToday,
				pace: this.deps.pace(),
				reasons,
				stage: this.deps.stage()
			});
			if (!decision.speak) return {
				spoke: false,
				reason: decision.reason
			};
			this.lastSpokeAt = now;
			this.spokenThisHour++;
			this.spokenToday++;
			this.deps.say(reasons[0] ?? decision.reason);
			return {
				spoke: true,
				reason: reasons[0] ?? decision.reason
			};
		} catch {
			return {
				spoke: false,
				reason: "读不到可靠的前台或输入空闲状态，这次不开口"
			};
		} finally {
			if (this.pending === controller) this.pending = null;
		}
	}
	async readActivity(signal) {
		const facts = await readForegroundAsync(void 0, signal);
		if (facts.idleMs === void 0 || !Number.isFinite(facts.idleMs) || facts.fullscreen === void 0) throw new Error("前台状态不完整");
		return {
			title: facts.title,
			idleMs: Math.max(0, facts.idleMs),
			fullscreen: facts.fullscreen
		};
	}
};
//#endregion
//#region src/main/index.ts
electron.protocol.registerSchemesAsPrivileged([{
	scheme: "murasame",
	privileges: {
		standard: true,
		secure: true,
		supportFetchAPI: true,
		corsEnabled: true
	}
}]);
var rendererMimeTypes = {
	".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
	".htm": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
	".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
	".ico": "image/x-icon",
	".js": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
	".json": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
	".map": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
	".moc3": "application/octet-stream",
	".png": "image/png",
	".svg": "image/svg+xml",
	".woff2": "font/woff2"
};
function registerRendererProtocol() {
	const rendererRoot = (0, node_path.resolve)(__dirname, "../renderer");
	electron.protocol.handle("murasame", async (request) => {
		try {
			const requestUrl = new URL(request.url);
			const relativePath = decodeURIComponent(requestUrl.pathname).replace(/^[/\\]+/, "");
			if (relativePath.startsWith("characters/")) {
				const charactersRoot = (0, node_path.resolve)(electron.app.getPath("userData"), "characters");
				const filePath = (0, node_path.resolve)(charactersRoot, relativePath.slice(11));
				if (filePath !== charactersRoot && !filePath.startsWith(`${charactersRoot}${node_path.sep}`)) return new Response("Forbidden", { status: 403 });
				const contents = await (0, node_fs_promises.readFile)(filePath);
				const contentType = rendererMimeTypes[(0, node_path.extname)(filePath).toLowerCase()] ?? "application/octet-stream";
				return new Response(new Uint8Array(contents), { headers: {
					"Content-Type": contentType,
					"Cache-Control": "no-cache"
				} });
			}
			const filePath = (0, node_path.resolve)(rendererRoot, relativePath || "index.html");
			if (filePath !== rendererRoot && !filePath.startsWith(`${rendererRoot}${node_path.sep}`)) return new Response("Forbidden", { status: 403 });
			const contents = await (0, node_fs_promises.readFile)(filePath);
			const contentType = rendererMimeTypes[(0, node_path.extname)(filePath).toLowerCase()] ?? "application/octet-stream";
			return new Response(new Uint8Array(contents), { headers: {
				"Content-Type": contentType,
				"Cache-Control": electron.app.isPackaged ? "public, max-age=31536000, immutable" : "no-cache"
			} });
		} catch {
			return new Response("Not found", { status: 404 });
		}
	});
}
if (process.env.MURASAME_USER_DATA) electron.app.setPath("userData", (0, node_path.resolve)(process.env.MURASAME_USER_DATA));
var gotSingleInstanceLock = electron.app.requestSingleInstanceLock();
function hardenSession() {
	electron.session.defaultSession.webRequest.onHeadersReceived((details, callback) => {
		callback({ responseHeaders: {
			...details.responseHeaders,
			"Content-Security-Policy": [contentSecurityPolicy(!electron.app.isPackaged)]
		} });
	});
	const developmentOrigin = electron.app.isPackaged ? void 0 : process.env.ELECTRON_RENDERER_URL;
	electron.session.defaultSession.setPermissionCheckHandler((_contents, permission, origin, details) => allowMicrophone({
		permission,
		url: origin,
		audioOnly: details.mediaType === "audio",
		enabled: getSettings().permissions.microphone,
		developmentOrigin
	}));
	electron.session.defaultSession.setPermissionRequestHandler((_contents, permission, callback, details) => {
		const media = "mediaTypes" in details ? details.mediaTypes : [];
		callback(allowMicrophone({
			permission,
			url: details.requestingUrl,
			audioOnly: Array.isArray(media) && media.length > 0 && media.every((kind) => kind === "audio"),
			enabled: getSettings().permissions.microphone,
			developmentOrigin
		}));
	});
}
if (!gotSingleInstanceLock) electron.app.quit();
else {
	electron.app.setAppUserModelId("com.murasame.desktop");
	electron.app.on("second-instance", () => {
		logger_default.info("Second instance requested; focusing the existing console");
		showMain();
	});
	electron.app.whenReady().then(async () => {
		hardenSession();
		registerRendererProtocol();
		if (electron.app.isPackaged) setBundledModelsRoot((0, node_path.join)(process.resourcesPath, "models"));
		try {
			getDatabase();
		} catch (error) {
			const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "本地数据库无法打开";
			logger_default.error("Startup failed before the window opened", error);
			electron.dialog.showErrorBox("Murasame 无法启动", message);
			electron.app.exit(1);
			return;
		}
		const capacityWarning = databaseCapacityWarning();
		if (capacityWarning) logger_default.warn(capacityWarning);
		const settings = getSettings();
		configureContainerOwner((0, node_crypto.createHash)("sha256").update(electron.app.getPath("userData")).digest("hex").slice(0, 24));
		const core = MurasameCore.create(getDatabase(), new ModelRegistry(), electron.app.getPath("userData"));
		core.tools.loadDirectory(electron.app.isPackaged ? (0, node_path.join)(process.resourcesPath, "plugins") : (0, node_path.join)(electron.app.getAppPath(), "plugins"));
		core.tools.loadDirectory((0, node_path.join)(electron.app.getPath("userData"), "plugins"));
		electron.app.setLoginItemSettings({
			openAtLogin: settings.startAtLogin,
			args: ["--hidden"]
		});
		let screenLocked = electron.powerMonitor.getSystemIdleState(1) === "locked";
		let suspended = false;
		const refreshLock = () => core.setLocked(screenLocked || suspended);
		electron.powerMonitor.on("lock-screen", () => {
			screenLocked = true;
			refreshLock();
		});
		electron.powerMonitor.on("unlock-screen", () => {
			screenLocked = false;
			refreshLock();
		});
		electron.powerMonitor.on("suspend", () => {
			suspended = true;
			refreshLock();
		});
		electron.powerMonitor.on("resume", () => {
			suspended = false;
			refreshLock();
		});
		refreshLock();
		const recovery = await cleanupOwnedContainersAsync().catch((error) => {
			logger_default.warn("上次活动的容器恢复未完成", error);
			return null;
		});
		if (recovery?.failed.length) logger_default.error("以下旧容器需要再次清理", recovery.failed.join(", "));
		registerIpcHandlers(core);
		initializeWindows();
		createTray();
		const hotkey = applyHotkey(getSettings().petHotkey, () => focusPetChat());
		refreshMemoryStatus(electron.app.getPath("userData"));
		if (!hotkey.ok) logger_default.warn(hotkey.message);
		try {
			const file = logger_default.transports.file.getFile();
			if (logExpired((Date.now() - (0, node_fs.statSync)(file.path).mtimeMs) / 864e5, settings.logRetentionDays)) file.clear();
		} catch {}
		let lastWatch = 0;
		const watchTimer = setInterval(() => {
			const current = getSettings();
			if (!current.screenWatchEnabled || !current.permissions.screen || current.doNotDisturb) return;
			if (Date.now() - lastWatch < current.screenWatchSeconds * 1e3) return;
			lastWatch = Date.now();
			core.analyzeScreen(true).catch((error) => logger_default.warn("持续观察暂停", error));
		}, 5e3);
		let healthPending = false;
		const healthTimer = setInterval(() => {
			if (healthPending || !core.autonomy.status().running) return;
			healthPending = true;
			core.autonomy.health().catch((error) => logger_default.warn("活动健康检查失败", error)).finally(() => {
				healthPending = false;
			});
		}, 5e3);
		electron.app.on("before-quit", () => clearInterval(watchTimer));
		let cleanupStarted = false;
		let cleanupFinished = false;
		electron.app.on("before-quit", (event) => {
			clearInterval(healthTimer);
			if (cleanupFinished) return;
			event.preventDefault();
			if (cleanupStarted) return;
			cleanupStarted = true;
			core.cancelSensitiveRequests();
			(async () => {
				const running = core.tasks.list().filter((task) => [
					"执行中",
					"规划中",
					"正在停止"
				].includes(task.status));
				await Promise.allSettled(running.map(async (task) => {
					await core.tasks.cancel(task.id);
					await core.tasks.settled(task.id);
				}));
				const stopped = await core.autonomy.stop({ containerGone: false });
				if (!stopped.ok) logger_default.error("退出时隔离活动未完全清理", stopped.error);
			})().catch((error) => logger_default.error("退出清理失败", error)).finally(() => {
				cleanupFinished = true;
				electron.app.quit();
			});
		});
		initializeUpdater();
		const proactive = new ProactiveLoop({
			hidden: () => isPetHidden(),
			doNotDisturb: () => getSettings().doNotDisturb || !core.characterService.load().proactive,
			taskRunning: () => core.tasks.list().some((task) => task.status === "执行中" || task.status === "等待批准"),
			pace: () => getSettings().proactivePace,
			stage: () => readRelationship().stage,
			reasons: () => {
				const finished = core.tasks.list().filter((task) => task.status === "已完成" || task.status === "部分完成");
				const blocked = core.tasks.list().filter((task) => task.status === "等待批准");
				const reasons = [];
				if (finished.length > 0) reasons.push(`你交代的「${finished[0].title}」已经有结果了`);
				if (blocked.length > 0) reasons.push(`「${blocked[0].title}」还等着你批准`);
				return reasons;
			},
			say: (text) => {
				broadcastPetPerformance({
					intent: "打招呼",
					motion: null,
					expression: null,
					degraded: false,
					pad: core.affect.pad,
					text
				});
				logger_default.info(`Proactive: ${text}`);
			}
		});
		proactive.start();
		electron.app.on("before-quit", () => proactive.stop());
		if (process.argv.includes("--hidden")) setTimeout(() => hideMain(), 250);
		electron.app.on("activate", () => showMain());
		logger_default.info(`Murasame ${electron.app.getVersion()} started; packaged=${electron.app.isPackaged}`);
	});
	electron.app.on("before-quit", () => {
		markQuitting();
		stopUpdater();
	});
	electron.app.on("will-quit", () => {
		electron.globalShortcut.unregisterAll();
		destroyTray();
	});
	electron.app.on("window-all-closed", () => {});
	process.on("uncaughtException", (error) => {
		logger_default.error("Uncaught main-process exception", error);
		electron.dialog.showErrorBox("Murasame 遇到意外错误", "应用可能无法继续运行。详情已写入本地日志，可在「运行与隐私」中打开日志目�
